# 配置日志
logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.ERROR,
    format='%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s',
    handlers=[
        logging.FileHandler('error.log'),
        logging.StreamHandler()
    ]
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def handle_global_exception(exc_type, exc_value, exc_traceback):
    """
    全局异常处理函数 - 记录日志后退出
    """
    if issubclass(exc_type, KeyboardInterrupt):
        # 不处理键盘中断，直接退出
        sys.__excepthook__(exc_type, exc_value, exc_traceback)
        return

    # 记录错误日志
    logger.critical(
        "未捕获的异常，程序将退出:",
        exc_info=(exc_type, exc_value, exc_traceback)
    )
    if IS_FROZEN:
        print("全局异常信息：")
        print("异常类型：", exc_type)
        print("异常值：", exc_value)
        print("异常追踪：", exc_traceback)
        input("确定退出...")
    # 调用原始异常处理器，显示错误并退出
    sys.__excepthook__(exc_type, exc_value, exc_traceback)
    exit(1)

# 设置全局异常处理器
sys.excepthook = handle_global_exception

# 获取.exe所在目录
if IS_FROZEN:
    # 如果是打包后的程序
    app_path = os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
    os.chdir(app_path)  # 更改工作目录

import os

# ===== 配置 =====
EXTENSIONS = {
    ".py", ".cpp", ".h", ".c", ".hpp",
    ".js", ".ts", ".java", ".go", ".rs"
}

IGNORE_DIRS = {
    ".git", "__pycache__", "build", "dist", ".idea", ".vscode"
}

OUTPUT_FILE = "all_code.txt"


def count_lines(filepath):
    total = 0
    try:
        with open(filepath, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
            for _ in f:
                total += 1
    except Exception:
        return 0
    return total


def scan_and_merge(root):
    total_files = 0
    total_lines = 0

    with open(OUTPUT_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
        for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(root):
            # 过滤目录
            dirnames[:] = [d for d in dirnames if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]

            for file in filenames:
                ext = os.path.splitext(file)[1]
                if ext not in EXTENSIONS:
                    continue

                filepath = os.path.join(dirpath, file)

                lines = count_lines(filepath)
                total_lines += lines
                total_files += 1

                # 写入文件头（路径标识）
                out.write("\n" + "=" * 80 + "\n")
                out.write(f"FILE: {filepath}\n")
                out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")

                try:
                    with open(filepath, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
                        out.write(f.read())
                except Exception:
                    out.write("[ERROR: Cannot read file]\n")

                out.write("\n")  # 文件间隔

    return total_files, total_lines


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys

    # if len(sys.argv) < 2:
    #     print("Usage: python code_counter_and_merge.py <project_path>")
    #     exit(1)

    # root = sys.argv[1]
    root = './'
    total_files, total_lines = scan_and_merge(root)

    print("=" * 50)
    print(f"Total Files : {total_files}")
    print(f"Total Lines : {total_lines}")
    print(f"Output File : {OUTPUT_FILE}")
    print("=" * 50)

import os
import shutil
import time

from core.pipeline.fault_detection import detect
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
import numpy as np


def dataset():
    path = r'\output\1'
    os.makedirs('cache\dataset', exist_ok=True)
    i = 0
    for p1, p2, p3 in os.walk(path):
        for f in p3:
            if f.endswith('.jpg'):
                p = os.path.join(p1, f)
                shutil.copy(p,
                            os.path.join('cache', 'dataset', f'{i}.jpg'),
                            )
                i = i + 1
        print(p1, p2, p3)


import cv2


def visualize_detect(image, outputs):
    img = image.copy()

    for det in outputs:
        x = int(det.x)
        y = int(det.y)
        w = int(det.w)
        h = int(det.h)

        pt1 = (x, y)
        pt2 = (x + w, y + h)

        # 画矩形框
        cv2.rectangle(img, pt1, pt2, (0, 255, 0), 2)

        # 写类别
        label = det.ftype
        cv2.putText(
            img,
            label,
            (x, y - 5),
            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
            0.7,
            (0, 255, 0),
            2
        )

    return img


if __name__ == '__main__':
    path = r'cache/dataset'
    # dataset()
    files = os.listdir(path)
    files = ['7.jpg']
    # files = ['104.jpg']
    # image = Image.open(os.path.join(path,)'cache/dataset/115.jpg')
    # image = np.array(image)

    key, idx = -1, 0
    while key != 27:
        idx = max(min(idx, len(files) - 1), 0)
        file = files[idx]
        image = Image.open(os.path.join(path, files[idx]))
        image = np.array(image)
        st = time.time()
        outputs = detect(image)
        print(f'{file}: time:{time.time() - st}')

        vis = visualize_detect(image, outputs)

        cv2.imshow("result", vis)
        while True:
            key = cv2.waitKey(0)
            if key == 119:  # w
                idx -= 1
                break
            elif key == 115: # s
                idx += 1
                break
            elif key == 27: # esc
                break
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

import sys
import sqlite3
import hashlib
import os
import json
import datetime
from enum import Enum
from threading import Lock


# ==========================
# 枚举定义
# ==========================

class Role(Enum):
    ADMIN = "admin"
    MANAGER = "manager"
    WORKER = "worker"


class Permission(Enum):
    CREATE_USER = "createUser"
    ADD_BATCH = "addBatch"
    VIEW_ALL = "viewAll"


# ==========================
# 单例用户管理类
# ==========================

class UserManager:
    _instance = None
    _lock = Lock()
    dbName = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.curdir), 'config', "users.db")

    def __new__(cls, *args, **kwargs):
        if not cls._instance:
            with cls._lock:
                if not cls._instance:
                    cls._instance = super().__new__(cls)
        return cls._instance

    def __init__(self):
        self._initDb()
        self._ensureDefaultAdmin()

    def _initDb(self):
        if not os.path.exists(self.dbName):
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.dbName),exist_ok=True)
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.dbName)
        cursor = conn.cursor()

        cursor.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            username TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
            passwordHash TEXT NOT NULL,
            salt TEXT NOT NULL,
            role TEXT NOT NULL,
            strList TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
            createdAt TEXT NOT NULL
        )
        """)

        conn.commit()
        conn.close()

    def _ensureDefaultAdmin(self):
        if not self._userExists("admin"):
            pwdHash, salt = self._hashPassword("admin123")
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.dbName)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
            INSERT INTO users (username, passwordHash, salt, role, createdAt)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (
                "admin",
                pwdHash,
                salt,
                Role.ADMIN.value,
                datetime.datetime.now().isoformat()
            ))
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()

    def _userExists(self, username):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.dbName)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = ?", (username,))
        result = cursor.fetchone()
        conn.close()
        return result is not None

    # -----------------------
    # 密码
    # -----------------------

    def _hashPassword(self, password, salt=None):
        if salt is None:
            salt = os.urandom(16)

        pwdHash = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac(
            "sha256",
            password.encode(),
            salt,
            100000
        )
        return pwdHash.hex(), salt.hex()

    def _verifyPassword(self, storedHash, storedSalt, inputPassword):
        salt = bytes.fromhex(storedSalt)
        inputHash, _ = self._hashPassword(inputPassword, salt)
        return inputHash == storedHash

    # -----------------------
    # 权限
    # -----------------------

    def _rolePermissions(self):
        return {
            Role.ADMIN: {Permission.CREATE_USER, Permission.ADD_BATCH, Permission.VIEW_ALL},
            Role.MANAGER: {Permission.ADD_BATCH, Permission.VIEW_ALL},
            Role.WORKER: {Permission.ADD_BATCH}
        }

    def _hasPermission(self, role, permission):
        return permission in self._rolePermissions().get(role, set())

    # -----------------------
    # 登录
    # -----------------------

    def login(self, username, password):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.dbName)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("""
        SELECT id, passwordHash, salt, role FROM users WHERE username = ?
        """, (username,))
        user = cursor.fetchone()
        conn.close()

        if not user:
            return False, "User not found"

        userId, pwdHash, salt, roleStr = user
        if self._verifyPassword(pwdHash, salt, password):
            return True, {
                "id": userId,
                "username": username,
                "role": Role(roleStr)
            }
        return False, "Wrong password"

    # -----------------------
    # 创建用户
    # -----------------------

    def createUser(self, operatorRole, username, password, role):
        if not self._hasPermission(operatorRole, Permission.CREATE_USER):
            return False, "Permission denied"

        pwdHash, salt = self._hashPassword(password)

        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.dbName)
        cursor = conn.cursor()

        try:
            cursor.execute("""
            INSERT INTO users (username, passwordHash, salt, role, createdAt)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (
                username,
                pwdHash,
                salt,
                role.value,
                datetime.datetime.now().isoformat()
            ))
            conn.commit()
            return True, "User created"
        except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
            return False, "Username exists"
        finally:
            conn.close()

    # -----------------------
    # 添加批次
    # -----------------------

    def addBatch(self, operatorRole, userId, batchName):
        if not self._hasPermission(operatorRole, Permission.ADD_BATCH):
            return False, "Permission denied"

        user = self.getUser(userId)
        if not user:
            return False, "User not found"

        strList = user["strList"]
        strList.append(batchName)

        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.dbName)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("""
        UPDATE users SET strList = ? WHERE id = ?
        """, (json.dumps(strList), userId))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()

        return True, "Batch added"

    def findUserByBatch(self, batchName):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.dbName)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("SELECT username, strList FROM users")
        users = cursor.fetchall()
        conn.close()

        result = []
        for username, strList in users:
            if batchName in json.loads(strList):
                result.append(username)
        return result

    def getUser(self, userId):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.dbName)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("""
        SELECT id, username, role, strList FROM users WHERE id = ?
        """, (userId,))
        user = cursor.fetchone()
        conn.close()

        if not user:
            return None

        return {
            "id": user[0],
            "username": user[1],
            "role": Role(user[2]),
            "strList": json.loads(user[3])
        }

    def getAllUsernames(self):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.dbName)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("SELECT username FROM users")
        names = [u[0] for u in cursor.fetchall()]
        conn.close()
        return names

    def getUsersByBatch(self, batchName):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.dbName)
        cursor = conn.cursor()

        cursor.execute("""
            SELECT username, strList FROM users
            WHERE strList LIKE ?
        """, (f'%"{batchName}"%',))

        users = cursor.fetchall()
        conn.close()

        result = []

        for username, strListStr in users:
            try:
                batchList = json.loads(strListStr)
            except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
                # 打日志，而不是静默忽略
                print(f"[ERROR] JSON decode failed for user {username}: {e}")
                continue

            if batchName in batchList:
                result.append(username)

        return result

import serial
import serial
import serial.tools.list_ports
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass
class Post():
    pass


class Serial():
    @staticmethod
    def posts():
        # 获取所有串口设备实例。
        # 如果没找到串口设备，则输出：“无串口设备。”
        # 如果找到串口设备，则依次输出每个设备对应的串口号和描述信息。
        ports_list = list(serial.tools.list_ports.comports())
        return ports_list

    @classmethod
    def open(cls, port: str, baudrate: int) -> 'Serial':
        _instance = cls()
        _instance.setSerial(serial=serial.Serial(port=port,
                                                 baudrate=baudrate, ))
        return _instance

    @staticmethod
    def hex2int(string, bin=16, signed=False):
        if signed:
            """把任意长度的十六进制字符串转为有符号整数"""
            dec_num = int(string, 16)
            m = 16 ** len(string)
            mid = m // 2 - 1
            if dec_num > mid:
                dec_num = dec_num - 16 ** len(string)
            val = dec_num
        else:
            val = int(string, bin)
        return val

    def __init__(self):
        self._serial:serial.Serial = None
        pass

    def setSerial(self, serial: serial.Serial) -> None:
        self._serial = serial

    def close(self):
        self._serial.close()
        return self._serial.close()

    def read(self):
        return self._serial.read()

    def isOpen(self) -> bool:
        return self._serial.is_open

# -- coding: utf-8 --
import threading
import time
import sys
import inspect
import ctypes
import random
import os
from ctypes import *

# sys.path.append(os.getenv('MVCAM_COMMON_RUNENV') + "/Samples/Python/MvImport")

from core.HK_MvImport.CameraParams_header import *
from core.HK_MvImport.MvCameraControl_class import *

# 强制关闭线程
def Async_raise(tid, exctype):
    tid = ctypes.c_long(tid)
    if not inspect.isclass(exctype):
        exctype = type(exctype)
    res = ctypes.pythonapi.PyThreadState_SetAsyncExc(tid, ctypes.py_object(exctype))
    if res == 0:
        raise ValueError("invalid thread id")
    elif res != 1:
        ctypes.pythonapi.PyThreadState_SetAsyncExc(tid, None)
        raise SystemError("PyThreadState_SetAsyncExc failed")


# 停止线程
def Stop_thread(thread):
    Async_raise(thread.ident, SystemExit)


# 转为16进制字符串
def To_hex_str(num):
    chaDic = {10: 'a', 11: 'b', 12: 'c', 13: 'd', 14: 'e', 15: 'f'}
    hexStr = ""
    if num < 0:
        num = num + 2 ** 32
    while num >= 16:
        digit = num % 16
        hexStr = chaDic.get(digit, str(digit)) + hexStr
        num //= 16
    hexStr = chaDic.get(num, str(num)) + hexStr
    return hexStr


# 是否是Mono图像
def Is_mono_data(enGvspPixelType):
    if PixelType_Gvsp_Mono8 == enGvspPixelType or PixelType_Gvsp_Mono10 == enGvspPixelType \
            or PixelType_Gvsp_Mono10_Packed == enGvspPixelType or PixelType_Gvsp_Mono12 == enGvspPixelType \
            or PixelType_Gvsp_Mono12_Packed == enGvspPixelType:
        return True
    else:
        return False


# 是否是彩色图像
def Is_color_data(enGvspPixelType):
    if PixelType_Gvsp_BayerGR8 == enGvspPixelType or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerRG8 == enGvspPixelType \
            or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerGB8 == enGvspPixelType or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerBG8 == enGvspPixelType \
            or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerGR10 == enGvspPixelType or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerRG10 == enGvspPixelType \
            or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerGB10 == enGvspPixelType or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerBG10 == enGvspPixelType \
            or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerGR12 == enGvspPixelType or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerRG12 == enGvspPixelType \
            or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerGB12 == enGvspPixelType or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerBG12 == enGvspPixelType \
            or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerGR10_Packed == enGvspPixelType or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerRG10_Packed == enGvspPixelType \
            or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerGB10_Packed == enGvspPixelType or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerBG10_Packed == enGvspPixelType \
            or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerGR12_Packed == enGvspPixelType or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerRG12_Packed == enGvspPixelType \
            or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerGB12_Packed == enGvspPixelType or PixelType_Gvsp_BayerBG12_Packed == enGvspPixelType \
            or PixelType_Gvsp_YUV422_Packed == enGvspPixelType or PixelType_Gvsp_YUV422_YUYV_Packed == enGvspPixelType:
        return True
    else:
        return False


# 相机操作类
class CameraOperation:

    def __init__(self, obj_cam, st_device_list, n_connect_num=0, b_open_device=False, b_start_grabbing=False,
                 h_thread_handle=None,
                 b_thread_closed=False, st_frame_info=None, b_exit=False, b_save_bmp=False, b_save_jpg=False,
                 buf_save_image=None,
                 n_save_image_size=0, n_win_gui_id=0, frame_rate=0, exposure_time=0, gain=0):

        self.obj_cam = obj_cam
        self.st_device_list = st_device_list
        self.n_connect_num = n_connect_num
        self.b_open_device = b_open_device
        self.b_start_grabbing = b_start_grabbing
        self.b_thread_closed = b_thread_closed
        self.st_frame_info = st_frame_info
        self.b_exit = b_exit
        self.b_save_bmp = b_save_bmp
        self.b_save_jpg = b_save_jpg
        self.buf_save_image = buf_save_image
        self.n_save_image_size = n_save_image_size
        self.h_thread_handle = h_thread_handle
        self.b_thread_closed
        self.frame_rate = frame_rate
        self.exposure_time = exposure_time
        self.gain = gain
        self.buf_lock = threading.Lock()  # 取图和存图的buffer锁

    # 打开相机
    def Open_device(self):
        if not self.b_open_device:
            if self.n_connect_num < 0:
                return MV_E_CALLORDER

            # ch:选择设备并创建句柄 | en:Select device and create handle
            nConnectionNum = int(self.n_connect_num)
            stDeviceList = cast(self.st_device_list.pDeviceInfo[int(nConnectionNum)],
                                POINTER(MV_CC_DEVICE_INFO)).contents
            self.obj_cam = MvCamera()
            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_CreateHandle(stDeviceList)
            if ret != 0:
                self.obj_cam.MV_CC_DestroyHandle()
                return ret

            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_OpenDevice()
            if ret != 0:
                return ret
            print("open device successfully!")
            self.b_open_device = True
            self.b_thread_closed = False

            # ch:探测网络最佳包大小(只对GigE相机有效) | en:Detection network optimal package size(It only works for the GigE camera)
            if stDeviceList.nTLayerType == MV_GIGE_DEVICE or stDeviceList.nTLayerType == MV_GENTL_GIGE_DEVICE:
                nPacketSize = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_GetOptimalPacketSize()
                if int(nPacketSize) > 0:
                    ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_SetIntValue("GevSCPSPacketSize", nPacketSize)
                    if ret != 0:
                        print("warning: set packet size fail! ret[0x%x]" % ret)
                else:
                    print("warning: set packet size fail! ret[0x%x]" % nPacketSize)

            stBool = c_bool(False)
            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_GetBoolValue("AcquisitionFrameRateEnable", stBool)
            if ret != 0:
                print("get acquisition frame rate enable fail! ret[0x%x]" % ret)

            # ch:设置触发模式为off | en:Set trigger mode as off
            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_SetEnumValue("TriggerMode", MV_TRIGGER_MODE_OFF)
            if ret != 0:
                print("set trigger mode fail! ret[0x%x]" % ret)
            return MV_OK

    # 开始取图
    def Start_grabbing(self, winHandle):
        if not self.b_start_grabbing and self.b_open_device:
            self.b_exit = False
            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_StartGrabbing()
            if ret != 0:
                return ret
            self.b_start_grabbing = True
            print("start grabbing successfully!")
            try:
                thread_id = random.randint(1, 10000)
                self.h_thread_handle = threading.Thread(target=CameraOperation.Work_thread, args=(self, winHandle))
                self.h_thread_handle.start()
                self.b_thread_closed = True
            finally:
                pass
            return MV_OK

        return MV_E_CALLORDER

    # 停止取图
    def Stop_grabbing(self):
        if self.b_start_grabbing and self.b_open_device:
            # 退出线程
            if self.b_thread_closed:
                Stop_thread(self.h_thread_handle)
                self.b_thread_closed = False
            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_StopGrabbing()
            if ret != 0:
                return ret
            print("stop grabbing successfully!")
            self.b_start_grabbing = False
            self.b_exit = True
            return MV_OK
        else:
            return MV_E_CALLORDER

    # 关闭相机
    def Close_device(self):
        if self.b_open_device:
            # 退出线程
            if self.b_thread_closed:
                Stop_thread(self.h_thread_handle)
                self.b_thread_closed = False
            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_CloseDevice()
            if ret != 0:
                return ret

        # ch:销毁句柄 | Destroy handle
        self.obj_cam.MV_CC_DestroyHandle()
        self.b_open_device = False
        self.b_start_grabbing = False
        self.b_exit = True
        print("close device successfully!")

        return MV_OK

    # 设置触发模式
    def Set_trigger_mode(self, is_trigger_mode):
        if not self.b_open_device:
            return MV_E_CALLORDER

        if not is_trigger_mode:
            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_SetEnumValue("TriggerMode", 0)
            if ret != 0:
                return ret
        else:
            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_SetEnumValue("TriggerMode", 1)
            if ret != 0:
                return ret
            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_SetEnumValue("TriggerSource", 7)
            if ret != 0:
                return ret

        return MV_OK

    # 软触发一次
    def Trigger_once(self):
        if self.b_open_device:
            return self.obj_cam.MV_CC_SetCommandValue("TriggerSoftware")

    # 获取参数
    def Get_parameter(self):
        if self.b_open_device:
            stFloatParam_FrameRate = MVCC_FLOATVALUE()
            memset(byref(stFloatParam_FrameRate), 0, sizeof(MVCC_FLOATVALUE))
            stFloatParam_exposureTime = MVCC_FLOATVALUE()
            memset(byref(stFloatParam_exposureTime), 0, sizeof(MVCC_FLOATVALUE))
            stFloatParam_gain = MVCC_FLOATVALUE()
            memset(byref(stFloatParam_gain), 0, sizeof(MVCC_FLOATVALUE))
            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_GetFloatValue("AcquisitionFrameRate", stFloatParam_FrameRate)
            if ret != 0:
                return ret
            self.frame_rate = stFloatParam_FrameRate.fCurValue

            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_GetFloatValue("ExposureTime", stFloatParam_exposureTime)
            if ret != 0:
                return ret
            self.exposure_time = stFloatParam_exposureTime.fCurValue

            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_GetFloatValue("Gain", stFloatParam_gain)
            if ret != 0:
                return ret
            self.gain = stFloatParam_gain.fCurValue

            return MV_OK

    # 设置参数
    def Set_parameter(self, frameRate, exposureTime, gain):
        if '' == frameRate or '' == exposureTime or '' == gain:
            print('show info', 'please type in the text box !')
            return MV_E_PARAMETER
        if self.b_open_device:
            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_SetEnumValue("ExposureAuto", 0)
            time.sleep(0.2)
            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_SetFloatValue("ExposureTime", float(exposureTime))
            if ret != 0:
                print('show error', 'set exposure time fail! ret = ' + To_hex_str(ret))
                return ret

            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_SetFloatValue("Gain", float(gain))
            if ret != 0:
                print('show error', 'set gain fail! ret = ' + To_hex_str(ret))
                return ret

            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_SetFloatValue("AcquisitionFrameRate", float(frameRate))
            if ret != 0:
                print('show error', 'set acquistion frame rate fail! ret = ' + To_hex_str(ret))
                return ret

            print('show info', 'set parameter success!')

            return MV_OK

    # 取图线程函数
    def Work_thread(self, winHandle):
        stOutFrame = MV_FRAME_OUT()
        memset(byref(stOutFrame), 0, sizeof(stOutFrame))

        while True:
            ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_GetImageBuffer(stOutFrame, 1000)
            if 0 == ret:
                # 拷贝图像和图像信息
                if self.buf_save_image is None:
                    self.buf_save_image = (c_ubyte * stOutFrame.stFrameInfo.nFrameLen)()
                self.st_frame_info = stOutFrame.stFrameInfo

                # 获取缓存锁
                self.buf_lock.acquire()
                cdll.msvcrt.memcpy(byref(self.buf_save_image), stOutFrame.pBufAddr, self.st_frame_info.nFrameLen)
                self.buf_lock.release()

                print("get one frame: Width[%d], Height[%d], nFrameNum[%d]"
                      % (self.st_frame_info.nWidth, self.st_frame_info.nHeight, self.st_frame_info.nFrameNum))
                # 释放缓存
                self.obj_cam.MV_CC_FreeImageBuffer(stOutFrame)
            else:
                print("no data, ret = " + To_hex_str(ret))
                continue

            # 使用Display接口显示图像
            stDisplayParam = MV_DISPLAY_FRAME_INFO()
            memset(byref(stDisplayParam), 0, sizeof(stDisplayParam))
            stDisplayParam.hWnd = int(winHandle)
            stDisplayParam.nWidth = self.st_frame_info.nWidth
            stDisplayParam.nHeight = self.st_frame_info.nHeight
            stDisplayParam.enPixelType = self.st_frame_info.enPixelType
            stDisplayParam.pData = self.buf_save_image
            stDisplayParam.nDataLen = self.st_frame_info.nFrameLen
            self.obj_cam.MV_CC_DisplayOneFrame(stDisplayParam)

            # 是否退出
            if self.b_exit:
                if self.buf_save_image is not None:
                    del self.buf_save_image
                break

    # 存jpg图像
    def Save_jpg(self):

        if self.buf_save_image is None:
            return

        # 获取缓存锁
        self.buf_lock.acquire()

        file_path = str(self.st_frame_info.nFrameNum) + ".jpg"
        c_file_path = file_path.encode('ascii')
        stSaveParam = MV_SAVE_IMAGE_TO_FILE_PARAM_EX()
        stSaveParam.enPixelType = self.st_frame_info.enPixelType  # ch:相机对应的像素格式 | en:Camera pixel type
        stSaveParam.nWidth = self.st_frame_info.nWidth  # ch:相机对应的宽 | en:Width
        stSaveParam.nHeight = self.st_frame_info.nHeight  # ch:相机对应的高 | en:Height
        stSaveParam.nDataLen = self.st_frame_info.nFrameLen
        stSaveParam.pData = cast(self.buf_save_image, POINTER(c_ubyte))
        stSaveParam.enImageType = MV_Image_Jpeg  # ch:需要保存的图像类型 | en:Image format to save
        stSaveParam.nQuality = 80
        stSaveParam.pcImagePath = ctypes.create_string_buffer(c_file_path)
        stSaveParam.iMethodValue = 1
        ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_SaveImageToFileEx(stSaveParam)

        self.buf_lock.release()
        return ret

    # 存BMP图像
    def Save_Bmp(self):

        if 0 == self.buf_save_image:
            return

        # 获取缓存锁
        self.buf_lock.acquire()

        file_path = str(self.st_frame_info.nFrameNum) + ".bmp"
        c_file_path = file_path.encode('ascii')

        stSaveParam = MV_SAVE_IMAGE_TO_FILE_PARAM_EX()
        stSaveParam.enPixelType = self.st_frame_info.enPixelType  # ch:相机对应的像素格式 | en:Camera pixel type
        stSaveParam.nWidth = self.st_frame_info.nWidth  # ch:相机对应的宽 | en:Width
        stSaveParam.nHeight = self.st_frame_info.nHeight  # ch:相机对应的高 | en:Height
        stSaveParam.nDataLen = self.st_frame_info.nFrameLen
        stSaveParam.pData = cast(self.buf_save_image, POINTER(c_ubyte))
        stSaveParam.enImageType = MV_Image_Bmp  # ch:需要保存的图像类型 | en:Image format to save
        stSaveParam.pcImagePath = ctypes.create_string_buffer(c_file_path)
        stSaveParam.iMethodValue = 1
        ret = self.obj_cam.MV_CC_SaveImageToFileEx(stSaveParam)

        self.buf_lock.release()

        return ret

import threading
import time

import numpy as np

from ..HK_MvImport.MvCameraControl_class import *
from ..HK_MvImport.CameraParams_header import *

ret = MvCamera.MV_CC_Initialize()
if ret != 0:
    raise RuntimeError(f'初始化SDK失败，错误码: {ret}')

from .tools import CameraToolsMixin
from ._io import CameraIOMixin
from .setting import CameraSettingMixin
from .error import CameraInitError

import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from ..HK_MvImport.MvCameraControl_class import *
from ..HK_MvImport.CameraParams_header import *
from .CamOperation_class import CameraOperation
from .error import CameraReadError

# _img = Image.open(r'img.png').convert('L')
# _img = np.array(_img)[:, :].astype(np.uint8)
class Camera(CameraToolsMixin, CameraIOMixin, CameraSettingMixin):

    def __init__(self, port, device_list=None):
        self._camera = MvCamera()
        self._cache: np.ndarray = None
        CameraIOMixin.__init__(self)
        device_list = device_list or self.device_list()
        self.op = CameraOperation(obj_cam=self._camera, st_device_list=device_list)

        # 通过 SDK 寻找设备
        device_enum = self.enum_devices()
        if port not in device_enum:
            raise CameraInitError("Not found devices")
        idx = self.enum_devices().index(port)

        # 枚举设备
        stDeviceList = cast(device_list.pDeviceInfo[idx], POINTER(MV_CC_DEVICE_INFO)).contents
        ret = self._camera.MV_CC_CreateHandle(stDeviceList)
        # 打开设备（使用已创建的句柄）
        ret = self._camera.MV_CC_OpenDevice()

        if ret != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"打开设备失败，错误码: {ret}")
        self.device_parameters()

        # 启动读取线程, 异步读取
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._read_thread)
        self._thread.daemon = True
        self._thread.start()
        self.op.b_open_device = True

    def device_parameters(self):
        # 获取相机参数
        width = c_uint()
        height = c_uint()
        pixel_format = c_uint()
        payload_size = c_uint()
        stParam = MVCC_INTVALUE()
        camera = self._camera

        ret = camera.MV_CC_GetIntValue("PayloadSize", stParam)
        if ret != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"获取PayloadSize失败，错误码: {ret}")

        payload_size.value = stParam.nCurValue

        # 获取宽度
        ret = camera.MV_CC_GetIntValue("Width", stParam)
        if ret != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"获取宽度失败，错误码: {ret}")
        width.value = stParam.nCurValue

        # 获取高度
        ret = camera.MV_CC_GetIntValue("Height", stParam)
        if ret != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"获取高度失败，错误码: {ret}")

        height.value = stParam.nCurValue

        pixel_format.value = 17301505  # RGB8
        # 或
        # pixel_format.value = 17301514  # Mono8

        # 曝光时间
        exposure_time = 15000  # 单位：微秒
        ret = camera.MV_CC_SetFloatValue("ExposureTime", exposure_time)

        # 开始抓图
        ret = camera.MV_CC_StartGrabbing()
        if ret != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"开始抓图失败，错误码: {ret}")

        # 分配缓冲区
        data_buf = (c_ubyte * payload_size.value)()
        data_size = c_uint(payload_size.value)

        stFrameInfo = MV_FRAME_OUT_INFO_EX()

        data_buf = (c_ubyte * payload_size.value)()
        ret = camera.MV_CC_GetOneFrameTimeout(
            byref(data_buf),
            payload_size.value,
            stFrameInfo,
            1000
        )

        self._stFrameInfo = stFrameInfo
        self._payload_size = payload_size

    def close(self) -> None:
        """ close camera """
        return self._camera.MV_CC_CloseDevice()

    def _read_thread(self):
        while True:
            # time.sleep(0.1)
            try:
                self._cache = self._read()
            except CameraReadError as e:
                pass
            except Exception as e:
                raise e
        # self._cache = _img#self._read()

    def width(self):
        return self._stFrameInfo.width

    def height(self):
        return self._stFrameInfo.height

    def read(self) -> np.ndarray or None:
        return self._cache


__all__ = (
    'MvCamera'
)

import sys
import warnings
import time
from typing import List
from .CamOperation_class import CameraOperation
from ..HK_MvImport.MvCameraControl_class import *
from ..HK_MvImport.MvErrorDefine_const import *

# from PyUICBasicDemo import Ui_MainWindow
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import sys
from ..HK_MvImport.MvCameraControl_class import *
from ..HK_MvImport.MvErrorDefine_const import *
from ..HK_MvImport.CameraParams_header import *
from .utils import To_hex_str


class CameraSettingMixin(object):
    _camera: MvCamera

    def setExposure(self, exposureTime: int):
        assert isinstance(exposureTime, int), 'the time of exposure must be int'
        ret = self._camera.MV_CC_SetEnumValue("ExposureAuto", 0)
        time.sleep(0.2)
        ret = self._camera.MV_CC_SetFloatValue("ExposureTime", float(exposureTime))
        if ret != 0:
            raise RuntimeError('show error', 'set exposure time fail! ret = ' + To_hex_str(ret))

    def setGain(self, gain: float):
        ret = self._camera.MV_CC_SetFloatValue("Gain", float(gain))
        if ret != 0:
            raise RuntimeError('show error', 'set gain fail! ret = ' + To_hex_str(ret))

    def setFrameRate(self, frameRate: int):
        ret = self._camera.MV_CC_SetFloatValue("AcquisitionFrameRate", float(frameRate))
        if ret != 0:
            raise RuntimeError('show error', 'set acquistion frame rate fail! ret = ' + To_hex_str(ret))

        # ch:设置触发模式 | en:set trigger mode

    def set_continue_mode(self):
        """ch:设置触发模式 | en:set trigger mode"""
        ret = obj_cam_operation.Set_trigger_mode(False)
        if ret != 0:
            strError = "Set continue mode failed ret:" + ToHexStr(ret) + " mode is " + str(is_trigger_mode)
            QMessageBox.warning(mainWindow, "Error", strError, QMessageBox.Ok)
        else:
            ui.radioContinueMode.setChecked(True)
            ui.radioTriggerMode.setChecked(False)
            ui.bnSoftwareTrigger.setEnabled(False)


    def set_software_trigger_mode(self):
        """ ch:设置软触发模式 | en:set software trigger mode """
        ret = obj_cam_operation.Set_trigger_mode(True)
        if ret != 0:
            strError = "Set trigger mode failed ret:" + ToHexStr(ret)
            QMessageBox.warning(mainWindow, "Error", strError, QMessageBox.Ok)
        else:
            ui.radioContinueMode.setChecked(False)
            ui.radioTriggerMode.setChecked(True)
            ui.bnSoftwareTrigger.setEnabled(isGrabbing)

        # ch:设置触发命令 | en:set trigger software

    def trigger_once(self):
        ret = obj_cam_operation.Trigger_once()
        if ret != 0:
            strError = "TriggerSoftware failed ret:" + ToHexStr(ret)
            QMessageBox.warning(mainWindow, "Error", strError, QMessageBox.Ok)

import sys
import warnings
from typing import List
from .CamOperation_class import CameraOperation
from core.HK_MvImport.MvCameraControl_class import *
from core.HK_MvImport.MvErrorDefine_const import *
from core.HK_MvImport.CameraParams_header import *
# from PyUICBasicDemo import Ui_MainWindow
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import sys
from ..HK_MvImport.MvCameraControl_class import *
from ..HK_MvImport.MvErrorDefine_const import *
from ..HK_MvImport.CameraParams_header import *


class CameraToolsMixin(object):
    @staticmethod
    def decoding_char(c_ubyte_value):
        c_char_p_value = ctypes.cast(c_ubyte_value, ctypes.c_char_p)
        try:
            decode_str = c_char_p_value.value.decode('gbk')  # Chinese characters
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            decode_str = str(c_char_p_value.value)
        return decode_str

    @classmethod
    def device_list(cls):
        deviceList = MV_CC_DEVICE_INFO_LIST()
        n_layer_type = MV_GIGE_DEVICE | MV_USB_DEVICE | MV_GENTL_CAMERALINK_DEVICE
        ret = MvCamera.MV_CC_EnumDevices(n_layer_type, deviceList)
        return deviceList

    @classmethod
    def enum_devices(cls, show=False) -> List[str]:
        global deviceList
        global obj_cam_operation

        deviceList = MV_CC_DEVICE_INFO_LIST()
        n_layer_type = (MV_GIGE_DEVICE | MV_USB_DEVICE | MV_GENTL_CAMERALINK_DEVICE
                        | MV_GENTL_CXP_DEVICE | MV_GENTL_XOF_DEVICE)
        ret = MvCamera.MV_CC_EnumDevices(n_layer_type, deviceList)
        if ret != 0:
            # strError = "Enum devices fail! ret = :" + ToHexStr(ret)
            # QMessageBox.warning(mainWindow, "Error", strError, QMessageBox.Ok)
            # return ret
            raise RuntimeError()

        if deviceList.nDeviceNum == 0:
            # QMessageBox.warning(mainWindow, "Info", "Find no device", QMessageBox.Ok)
            # return ret
            warnings.warn('Not found device')
            return []
        if show: print("Find %d devices!" % deviceList.nDeviceNum)

        devList = []
        for i in range(0, deviceList.nDeviceNum):
            mvcc_dev_info = cast(deviceList.pDeviceInfo[i], POINTER(MV_CC_DEVICE_INFO)).contents
            if mvcc_dev_info.nTLayerType == MV_GIGE_DEVICE or mvcc_dev_info.nTLayerType == MV_GENTL_GIGE_DEVICE:
                if show: print("\ngige device: [%d]" % i)
                user_defined_name = cls.decoding_char(mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stGigEInfo.chUserDefinedName)
                model_name = cls.decoding_char(mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stGigEInfo.chModelName)
                if show: print("device user define name: " + user_defined_name)
                if show: print("device model name: " + model_name)

                nip1 = ((mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stGigEInfo.nCurrentIp & 0xff000000) >> 24)
                nip2 = ((mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stGigEInfo.nCurrentIp & 0x00ff0000) >> 16)
                nip3 = ((mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stGigEInfo.nCurrentIp & 0x0000ff00) >> 8)
                nip4 = (mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stGigEInfo.nCurrentIp & 0x000000ff)
                if show: print("current ip: %d.%d.%d.%d " % (nip1, nip2, nip3, nip4))
                devList.append(
                    "[" + str(i) + "]GigE: " + user_defined_name + " " + model_name + "(" + str(nip1) + "." + str(
                        nip2) + "." + str(nip3) + "." + str(nip4) + ")")
            elif mvcc_dev_info.nTLayerType == MV_USB_DEVICE:
                if show: print("\nu3v device: [%d]" % i)
                user_defined_name = cls.decoding_char(mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stUsb3VInfo.chUserDefinedName)
                model_name = cls.decoding_char(mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stUsb3VInfo.chModelName)
                if show: print("device user define name: " + user_defined_name)
                if show: print("device model name: " + model_name)

                strSerialNumber = ""
                for per in mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stUsb3VInfo.chSerialNumber:
                    if per == 0:
                        break
                    strSerialNumber = strSerialNumber + chr(per)
                if show: print("user serial number: " + strSerialNumber)
                devList.append("[" + str(i) + "]USB: " + user_defined_name + " " + model_name
                               + "(" + str(strSerialNumber) + ")")
            elif mvcc_dev_info.nTLayerType == MV_GENTL_CAMERALINK_DEVICE:
                if show: print("\nCML device: [%d]" % i)
                user_defined_name = cls.decoding_char(mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stCMLInfo.chUserDefinedName)
                model_name = cls.decoding_char(mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stCMLInfo.chModelName)
                if show: print("device user define name: " + user_defined_name)
                if show: print("device model name: " + model_name)

                strSerialNumber = ""
                for per in mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stCMLInfo.chSerialNumber:
                    if per == 0:
                        break
                    strSerialNumber = strSerialNumber + chr(per)
                if show: print("user serial number: " + strSerialNumber)
                devList.append("[" + str(i) + "]CML: " + user_defined_name + " " + model_name
                               + "(" + str(strSerialNumber) + ")")
            elif mvcc_dev_info.nTLayerType == MV_GENTL_CXP_DEVICE:
                if show: print("\nCXP device: [%d]" % i)
                user_defined_name = cls.decoding_char(mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stCXPInfo.chUserDefinedName)
                model_name = cls.decoding_char(mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stCXPInfo.chModelName)
                if show: print("device user define name: " + user_defined_name)
                if show: print("device model name: " + model_name)

                strSerialNumber = ""
                for per in mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stCXPInfo.chSerialNumber:
                    if per == 0:
                        break
                    strSerialNumber = strSerialNumber + chr(per)
                if show: print("user serial number: " + strSerialNumber)
                devList.append("[" + str(i) + "]CXP: " + user_defined_name + " " + model_name
                               + "(" + str(strSerialNumber) + ")")
            elif mvcc_dev_info.nTLayerType == MV_GENTL_XOF_DEVICE:
                if show: print("\nXoF device: [%d]" % i)
                user_defined_name = cls.decoding_char(mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stXoFInfo.chUserDefinedName)
                model_name = cls.decoding_char(mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stXoFInfo.chModelName)
                if show: print("device user define name: " + user_defined_name)
                if show: print("device model name: " + model_name)

                strSerialNumber = ""
                for per in mvcc_dev_info.SpecialInfo.stXoFInfo.chSerialNumber:
                    if per == 0:
                        break
                    strSerialNumber = strSerialNumber + chr(per)
                if show: print("user serial number: " + strSerialNumber)
                devList.append("[" + str(i) + "]XoF: " + user_defined_name + " " + model_name
                               + "(" + str(strSerialNumber) + ")")

        return devList


================================================================================

# 转为16进制字符串
def To_hex_str(num):
    chaDic = {10: 'a', 11: 'b', 12: 'c', 13: 'd', 14: 'e', 15: 'f'}
    hexStr = ""
    if num < 0:
        num = num + 2 ** 32
    while num >= 16:
        digit = num % 16
        hexStr = chaDic.get(digit, str(digit)) + hexStr
        num //= 16
    hexStr = chaDic.get(num, str(num)) + hexStr
    return hexStr

import numpy as np

from .error import CameraReadError
from ..HK_MvImport.CameraParams_header import *
from ..HK_MvImport.MvCameraControl_class import *


class CameraIOMixin(object):
    _camera: MvCamera
    _stFrameInfo: MV_FRAME_OUT_INFO_EX
    _payload_size: c_uint

    def __init__(self):
        # 创建一个buff作为读取的缓存区, 避免频繁创建
        self._data_buf = None
        self._fram: np.ndarray = None

    def read_buff(self):
        """ 从buff中读取数据, 不负责解码"""
        stFrameInfo = self._stFrameInfo
        payload_size = self._payload_size
        # 避免频繁的创建
        if self._data_buf is None:
            self._data_buf = (c_ubyte * payload_size.value)()
            self._frame = np.frombuffer(self._data_buf, dtype=np.uint8)
        data_buf = self._data_buf
        ret = self._camera.MV_CC_GetOneFrameTimeout(
            # ret = self._camera.MV_CC_GetImageForBGR(
            byref(data_buf),
            payload_size.value,
            stFrameInfo,
            1000
        )
        return ret, data_buf

    def _read(self) -> np.ndarray:
        stFrameInfo = self._stFrameInfo
        payload_size = self._payload_size

        ret, data_buf = self.read_buff()

        if ret == 0:
            frame = self._frame  # 直接缓存中读取
            actual_width = stFrameInfo.nWidth
            actual_height = stFrameInfo.nHeight

            if stFrameInfo.enPixelType == 17301505:  # RGB8
                expected_size = actual_width * actual_height
                if len(frame) != expected_size:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"数据大小不匹配: 期望 {expected_size}, 实际 {len(frame)}")

                frame = frame.reshape((actual_height, actual_width))
            elif stFrameInfo.enPixelType == 17301514:  # Mono8
                expected_size = actual_width * actual_height
                if len(frame) != expected_size:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"数据大小不匹配: 期望 {expected_size}, 实际 {len(frame)}")

                frame = frame.reshape((actual_height, actual_width))
            elif stFrameInfo.enPixelType == 17301513:  # 可能是 Bayer 格式
                expected_size = actual_width * actual_height
                if len(frame) != expected_size:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"数据大小不匹配: 期望 {expected_size}, 实际 {len(frame)}")

                frame = frame.reshape((actual_height, actual_width))
            else:
                raise RuntimeError(f"不支持的像素格式: {stFrameInfo.enPixelType}")
        else:
            raise CameraReadError(f"获取图像失败，错误码: {ret}")
        return frame

# -- coding: utf-8 --

import sys
import copy
import os
import ctypes

from ctypes import *

from .PixelType_header import *
from .CameraParams_const import *
from .CameraParams_header import *
from .MvErrorDefine_const import *

# Python3.8版本修改Dll加载策略, 默认不再搜索Path环境变量, 同时增加winmode参数以兼容旧版本
# ctypes.windll.LoadLibrary(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'lib'))
# print(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'lib'))
dllname = "MvCameraControl.dll"
dllname = os.path.join('core', 'HK_MvImport', 'lib', 'Win64_x64', dllname)
if "winmode" in ctypes.WinDLL.__init__.__code__.co_varnames:
    try:
        MvCamCtrldll = WinDLL(dllname, winmode=0)
    except FileNotFoundError as e:
        MvCamCtrldll = WinDLL(dllname)
else:
    MvCamCtrldll = WinDLL(dllname)


# 用于回调函数传入相机实例
class _MV_PY_OBJECT_(Structure):
    pass


_MV_PY_OBJECT_._fields_ = [
    ('PyObject', py_object),
]
MV_PY_OBJECT = _MV_PY_OBJECT_


class MvCamera():

    def __init__(self):
        self._handle = c_void_p()  # 记录当前连接设备的句柄
        self.handle = pointer(self._handle)  # 创建句柄指针

    '''
    Part1 ch: 相机的控制和取流接口 | en: Camera control and streaming
    '''

    # ch:初始化SDK | en: Initialize SDK
    @staticmethod
    def MV_CC_Initialize():
        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_Initialize.restype = c_int
        # C原型：int __stdcall MV_CC_Initialize();
        return MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_Initialize()

    # ch:反初始化SDK | en: Finalize SDK
    @staticmethod
    def MV_CC_Finalize():
        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_Finalize.restype = c_int
        # C原型：int __stdcall MV_CC_Finalize();
        return MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_Finalize()

    # ch:获取SDK版本号 | en:Get SDK Version
    @staticmethod
    def MV_CC_GetSDKVersion():
        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_GetSDKVersion.restype = c_uint
        # C原型：unsigned int __stdcall MV_CC_GetSDKVersion();
        return MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_GetSDKVersion()

    # ch:获取支持的传输层 | en:Get supported Transport Layer
    @staticmethod
    def MV_CC_EnumerateTls():
        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_EnumerateTls.restype = c_uint
        # C原型：int __stdcall MV_CC_EnumerateTls();
        return MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_EnumerateTls()

    # ch:枚举设备 | en:Enumerate Device
    @staticmethod
    def MV_CC_EnumDevices(nTLayerType, stDevList):
        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_EnumDevices.argtype = (c_uint, c_void_p)
        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_EnumDevices.restype = c_uint
        # C原型:int __stdcall MV_CC_EnumDevices(unsigned int nTLayerType, MV_CC_DEVICE_INFO_LIST* pstDevList)
        return MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_EnumDevices(c_uint(nTLayerType), byref(stDevList))

    # ch:根据厂商名字枚举设备 | en:Enumerate device according to manufacture name
    @staticmethod
    def MV_CC_EnumDevicesEx(nTLayerType, stDevList, strManufacturerName):
        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_EnumDevicesEx.argtype = (c_uint, c_void_p, c_void_p)
        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_EnumDevicesEx.restype = c_uint
        # C原型:int __stdcall MV_CC_EnumDevicesEx(IN unsigned int nTLayerType, IN OUT MV_CC_DEVICE_INFO_LIST* pstDevList,
        #                                         IN const char* strManufacturerName);
        return MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_EnumDevicesEx(c_uint(nTLayerType), byref(stDevList),
                                                strManufacturerName.encode('ascii'))

    # ch:枚举设备扩展（可指定排序方式枚举、根据厂商名字过滤） | en: Enumerate device according to the specified ordering
    @staticmethod
    def MV_CC_EnumDevicesEx2(nTLayerType, stDevList, strManufacturerName, enSortMethod):
        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_EnumDevicesEx2.argtype = (c_uint, c_void_p, c_void_p, c_uint)
        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_EnumDevicesEx2.restype = c_uint
        # C原型:int __stdcall MV_CC_EnumDevicesEx2(IN unsigned int nTLayerType, IN OUT MV_CC_DEVICE_INFO_LIST* pstDevList,
        #                                          IN const char* strManufacturerName, IN MV_SORT_METHOD enSortMethod);
        return MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_EnumDevicesEx2(c_uint(nTLayerType), byref(stDevList),
                                                 strManufacturerName.encode('ascii'), c_uint(enSortMethod))

    # ch:设备是否可达 | en:Is the device accessible
    @staticmethod
    def MV_CC_IsDeviceAccessible(stDevInfo, nAccessMode):
        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_IsDeviceAccessible.argtype = (c_void_p, c_uint)
        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_IsDeviceAccessible.restype = c_uint
        # C原型：bool __stdcall MV_CC_IsDeviceAccessible(IN MV_CC_DEVICE_INFO* pstDevInfo, IN unsigned int nAccessMode);
        return MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_IsDeviceAccessible(byref(stDevInfo), nAccessMode)

    # ch:枚举采集卡 | en:Enumerate frame grabbers
    @staticmethod
    def MV_CC_EnumInterfaces(nTLayerType, stInterfaceInfoList):
        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_EnumInterfaces.argtype = (c_uint, c_void_p)

        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_CreateInterface.argtype = c_void_p

        return MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_CreateInterface(byref(self.handle), byref(stInterfaceInfo))

    # ch:通过采集卡ID创建采集卡句柄 | en:Create frame grabber handle by frame grabber ID
    def MV_CC_CreateInterfaceByID(self, InterfaceID):
        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_CreateInterfaceByID.argtype = c_void_p

        return MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_CreateInterfaceByID(byref(self.handle), InterfaceID.encode('ascii'))

        MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_OpenInterface.argtype = c_void_p

        return MvCamCtrldll.MV_CC_OpenInterface(self.handle, 0)

        return MvCamCtrldll.MV_USB_SetEventNodeNum(self.handle, c_uint(nEventNodeNum))

#!/usr/bin/env python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

MV_OK                                        = 0x00000000  # < \~chinese 成功，无错误             \~english Successed, no error

# 通用错误码定义:范围0x80000000-0x800000FF
MV_E_HANDLE                                  = 0x80000000  # < \~chinese 错误或无效的句柄         \~english Error or invalid handle
MV_E_SUPPORT                                 = 0x80000001  # < \~chinese 不支持的功能             \~english Not supported function
MV_E_BUFOVER                                 = 0x80000002  # < \~chinese 缓存已满                 \~english Buffer overflow
MV_E_CALLORDER                               = 0x80000003  # < \~chinese 函数调用顺序错误         \~english Function calling order error
MV_E_PARAMETER                               = 0x80000004  # < \~chinese 错误的参数               \~english Incorrect parameter
MV_E_RESOURCE                                = 0x80000006  # < \~chinese 资源申请失败             \~english Applying resource failed
MV_E_NODATA                                  = 0x80000007  # < \~chinese 无数据                   \~english No data
MV_E_PRECONDITION                            = 0x80000008  # < \~chinese 前置条件有误，或运行环境已发生变化       \~english Precondition error, or running environment changed
MV_E_VERSION                                 = 0x80000009  # < \~chinese 版本不匹配               \~english Version mismatches
MV_E_NOENOUGH_BUF                            = 0x8000000A  # < \~chinese 传入的内存空间不足       \~english Insufficient memory
MV_E_ABNORMAL_IMAGE                          = 0x8000000B  # < \~chinese 异常图像，可能是丢包导致图像不完整       \~english Abnormal image, maybe incomplete image because of lost packet
MV_E_LOAD_LIBRARY                            = 0x8000000C  # < \~chinese 动态导入DLL失败          \~english Load library failed
MV_E_NOOUTBUF                                = 0x8000000D  # < \~chinese 没有可输出的缓存         \~english No Avaliable Buffer
MV_E_ENCRYPT                                 = 0x8000000E  # < \~chinese 加密错误                 \~english Encryption error
MV_E_OPENFILE                                = 0x8000000F  # < \~chinese 打开文件出现错误         \~english open file error
MV_E_BUF_IN_USE                              = 0x80000010  # < \~chinese 缓存地址已使用           \~english Buffer already in use
MV_E_BUF_INVALID                             = 0x80000011  # < \~chinese 无效的缓存地址           \~english Buffer address invalid
MV_E_NOALIGN_BUF                             = 0x80000012  # < \~chinese 缓存对齐异常             \~english Buffer alignmenterror error
MV_E_NOENOUGH_BUF_NUM                        = 0x80000013  # < \~chinese 缓存个数不足             \~english Insufficient cache count
MV_E_PORT_IN_USE                             = 0x80000014  # < \~chinese 串口被占用               \~english Port is in use
MV_E_IMAGE_DECODEC                           = 0x80000015  # < \~chinese 解码错误(SDK校验图像异常)\~english Decoding error (SDK verification image exception)
MV_E_UNKNOW                                  = 0x800000FF  # < \~chinese 未知的错误               \~english Unknown error

# GenICam系列错误:范围0x80000100-0x800001FF
MV_E_GC_GENERIC                              = 0x80000100  # < \~chinese 通用错误                 \~english General error
MV_E_GC_ARGUMENT                             = 0x80000101  # < \~chinese 参数非法                 \~english Illegal parameters
MV_E_GC_RANGE                                = 0x80000102  # < \~chinese 值超出范围               \~english The value is out of range
MV_E_GC_PROPERTY                             = 0x80000103  # < \~chinese 属性                     \~english Property
MV_E_GC_RUNTIME                              = 0x80000104  # < \~chinese 运行环境有问题           \~english Running environment error
MV_E_GC_LOGICAL                              = 0x80000105  # < \~chinese 逻辑错误                 \~english Logical error
MV_E_GC_ACCESS                               = 0x80000106  # < \~chinese 节点访问条件有误         \~english Node accessing condition error
MV_E_GC_TIMEOUT                              = 0x80000107  # < \~chinese 超时                     \~english Timeout
MV_E_GC_DYNAMICCAST                          = 0x80000108  # < \~chinese 转换异常                 \~english Transformation exception
MV_E_GC_UNKNOW                               = 0x800001FF  # < \~chinese GenICam未知错误          \~english GenICam unknown error

# GigE_STATUS对应的错误码:范围0x80000200-0x800002FF
MV_E_NOT_IMPLEMENTED                         = 0x80000200  # < \~chinese 命令不被设备支持         \~english The command is not supported by device
MV_E_INVALID_ADDRESS                         = 0x80000201  # < \~chinese 访问的目标地址不存在     \~english The target address being accessed does not exist
MV_E_WRITE_PROTECT                           = 0x80000202  # < \~chinese 目标地址不可写           \~english The target address is not writable
MV_E_ACCESS_DENIED                           = 0x80000203  # < \~chinese 设备无访问权限           \~english No permission
MV_E_BUSY                                    = 0x80000204  # < \~chinese 设备忙，或网络断开       \~english Device is busy, or network disconnected
MV_E_PACKET                                  = 0x80000205  # < \~chinese 网络包数据错误           \~english Network data packet error
MV_E_NETER                                   = 0x80000206  # < \~chinese 网络相关错误             \~english Network error
MV_E_KEY_VERIFICATION                        = 0x8000020F  # < \~chinese 秘钥校验错误              \~english SwitchKey error
MV_E_IP_CONFLICT                             = 0x80000221  # < \~chinese 设备IP冲突               \~english Device IP conflict

# USB_STATUS对应的错误码:范围0x80000300-0x800003FF
MV_E_USB_READ                                = 0x80000300  # < \~chinese 读usb出错               \~english Reading USB error
MV_E_USB_WRITE                               = 0x80000301  # < \~chinese 写usb出错               \~english Writing USB error
MV_E_USB_DEVICE                              = 0x80000302  # < \~chinese 设备异常                \~english Device exception
MV_E_USB_GENICAM                             = 0x80000303  # < \~chinese GenICam相关错误         \~english GenICam error
MV_E_USB_BANDWIDTH                           = 0x80000304  # < \~chinese 带宽不足  该错误码新增   \~english Insufficient bandwidth, this error code is newly added
MV_E_USB_DRIVER                              = 0x80000305  # < \~chinese 驱动不匹配或者未装驱动   \~english Driver mismatch or unmounted drive
MV_E_USB_UNKNOW                              = 0x800003FF  # < \~chinese USB未知的错误           \~english USB unknown error

# 升级时对应的错误码:范围0x80000400-0x800004FF
MV_E_UPG_FILE_MISMATCH                       = 0x80000400  # < \~chinese 升级固件不匹配           \~english Firmware mismatches
MV_E_UPG_LANGUSGE_MISMATCH                   = 0x80000401  # < \~chinese 升级固件语言不匹配       \~english Firmware language mismatches
MV_E_UPG_CONFLICT                            = 0x80000402  # < \~chinese 升级冲突（设备已经在升级了再次请求升级即返回此错误）   \~english Upgrading conflicted (repeated upgrading requests during device upgrade)
MV_E_UPG_INNER_ERR                           = 0x80000403  # < \~chinese 升级时设备内部出现错误   \~english Camera internal error during upgrade
MV_E_UPG_UNKNOW                              = 0x800004FF  # < \~chinese 升级时未知错误          \~english Unknown error during upgrade

================================================================================
FILE: ./core\pipeline\detect_worker.py

import time, pickle
import cv2, sys,os


from fault_detection import detect,DetectOutput
from fault_detection.anaylsis import FaultEnum
import numpy as np
import multiprocessing.shared_memory as shm
import argparse
import onnxruntime as ort
from ultralytics import YOLO
from PIL import Image

WRITE_FINISHED_FLAG = 0
DETECT_FINISHED_FLAG = 1


def get_args():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Simple example of a training script.")
    parser.add_argument(f"--img_shm_name", type=str)
    parser.add_argument(f"--op_shm_name", type=str)
    parser.add_argument(f"--out_shm_name", type=str)
    parser.add_argument(f"--width", type=int)
    parser.add_argument(f"--height", type=int)

    args = parser.parse_args()
    return args

USE_YOLO = True
def main():
    args = get_args()
    width, height = args.width, args.height
    # 连接到已存在的共享内存块
    img_shm = shm.SharedMemory(name=args.img_shm_name)
    op_shm = shm.SharedMemory(name=args.op_shm_name)
    out_shm = shm.SharedMemory(name=args.out_shm_name)
    if USE_YOLO:
        model_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(__file__))),'_rc','yolo.onnx')
        yolo = YOLO(model_path)
    while True:
        if op_shm.buf[WRITE_FINISHED_FLAG] != 1:
            time.sleep(0.001)
            continue
        img = np.ndarray((height, width), dtype=np.uint8, buffer=img_shm.buf).copy()

        if USE_YOLO:
            result = yolo(np.repeat(img[:, :, np.newaxis], 3, axis=-1), imgsz=320, verbose=False)
            output = []
            boxes = result[0].boxes.xywh.cpu().numpy()  # 转为numpy数组
            confs = result[0].boxes.conf.cpu().numpy()
            classes = result[0].boxes.cls.cpu().numpy()

            for j, (box, conf, cls) in enumerate(zip(boxes, confs, classes)):
                # print(box, conf, cls)
                output.append(DetectOutput(x=float(box[0]), y=float(box[1]), w=float(box[2]), h=float(box[3]),
                                           ftype=(FaultEnum.Bubble,
                                                  FaultEnum.Particle,
                                                  FaultEnum.Tumor,
                                                  FaultEnum.Sticky_Line)[int(cls)], ))
        else:
            output = detect(img)

        # 回传
        data_bytes = pickle.dumps([o.to_dict() for o in output])
        out_shm.buf[:len(data_bytes)] = data_bytes
        # 传递数据大小
        data_length = len(data_bytes)
        # 3. 将整数转换为 bytes
        num_as_bytes = data_length.to_bytes(1024, byteorder='big')
        out_shm.buf[-1024:] = num_as_bytes

        # flag
        op_shm.buf[WRITE_FINISHED_FLAG] = 0
        op_shm.buf[DETECT_FINISHED_FLAG] = 1


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()


================================================================================
FILE: ./core\pipeline\error.py
================================================================================
class DeviceConnectError(Exception):
    """ 设备链接错误 """


class CameraConnectError(DeviceConnectError):
    """ 摄像头链接错误"""


class OpencvCameraConnectError(CameraConnectError):
    """ Hk摄像头链接错误"""


class HKCameraConnectError(CameraConnectError):
    """ Hk摄像头链接错误"""


class SerialConnectError(DeviceConnectError):
    """ 串口设备链接错误 """


class LightningSerialConnectError(SerialConnectError):
    """ 放电检测串口链接错误 """


class LightSourceControlerSerialConnectError(SerialConnectError):
    """ 光源控制器串口链接错误 """


class VSamplerSerialConnectError(SerialConnectError):
    """ 计米轮串口链接错误 """


================================================================================
FILE: ./core\pipeline\meta.py
================================================================================
import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple

import numpy as np
from PIL import Image

from core.pipeline.fault_detection import DetectOutput
from .device.camera import Camera


class PipelineType():
    exposureBatch = ["camera1", "camera2", "camera3", "camera4"]
    CAMERAS = ["camera1", "camera2", "camera3", "camera4"]
    CAMERA_LIST = CAMERAS
    CHANNELS = ['c1', 'c2', 'c3', 'c4']

    State_DetectDisable = '检测未使能'
    State_NoBacthName = '没有批次名称'
    State_Normal = '正常'


@dataclass
class ChannelInfo():
    oringe_image: np.ndarray = None
    detect_image: np.ndarray = None
    show_image: np.ndarray = None
    faults: List[DetectOutput] = None
    position:float = 0.0

    def clear(self):
        self.oringe_image = None
        self.detect_image = None
        self.show_image = None
        self.faults = []

    @property
    def camera(self) -> Camera:
        raise NotImplementedError



@dataclass
class FaultInfo():
    image: np.ndarray = None
    showImage: np.ndarray = None
    faults: List[DetectOutput] = None
    location: float = None
    batchName: str = None
    camera: str = None
    sampler: str = None
    worker: str = None

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, **kwargs):
        faults = kwargs.pop('faults')
        kwargs['faults'] = [DetectOutput(**f) for f in faults]
        return cls(**kwargs)

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            'faults': [f.to_dict() for f in self.faults],
            'location': self.location,
            'worker': self.worker,
        }

    def saveImage(self, path):
        if len(self.image.shape) == 2:
            image = Image.fromarray(self.image, mode='L')
        else:
            image = Image.fromarray(self.image[:, :, ::-1], mode='RGB')
        image.save(path)


@dataclass
class ModelConfig(object):
    """ 不同漆包线型号的单独配置 """
    rectification: Dict[str, Tuple[int, int, int, int]]
    mapper: Dict[str, int]
    enable: Dict[str, bool]
    exposureBatch: Dict[str, int]

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, _dict):
        return cls(
            rectification=_dict.get('rectification', {PipelineType.CHANNELS[0]: (0, 1, 0, 1),
                                                      PipelineType.CHANNELS[1]: (0, 1, 0, 1),
                                                      PipelineType.CHANNELS[2]: (0, 1, 0, 1),
                                                      PipelineType.CHANNELS[3]: (0, 1, 0, 1)}),
            mapper=_dict.get('mapper', {c1: c2 for c1, c2 in zip(PipelineType.CHANNELS, PipelineType.CAMERA_LIST)}),
            enable=_dict.get('enable', {PipelineType.CHANNELS[0]: True,
                                        PipelineType.CHANNELS[1]: True,
                                        PipelineType.CHANNELS[2]: True,
                                        PipelineType.CHANNELS[3]: True}),
            exposureBatch=_dict.get('exposureBatch', {PipelineType.CAMERA_LIST[0]: 0,
                                                      PipelineType.CAMERA_LIST[1]: 0,
                                                      PipelineType.CAMERA_LIST[2]: 0,
                                                      PipelineType.CAMERA_LIST[3]: 0}),
        )

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path):
        with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json_data = json.load(f)
        return cls.from_dict(json_data)

    def save(self, path):
        with open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(self.to_dict(), f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            'rectification': self.rectification,
            'mapper': self.mapper,
            'enable': self.enable,
            "exposureBatch": self.exposureBatch
        }


================================================================================
FILE: ./core\pipeline\mixin_camera.py
================================================================================
import threading
import time

from typing import Dict

from .device import LightSourceSerial, IOCard
from .device.camera import Camera, HK_Camera
from .error import CameraConnectError
from .meta import ChannelInfo, ModelConfig, PipelineType
from ..config import config


class ChannelInfo(ChannelInfo):
    def __init__(self, *args,
                 pipeline, cameras: Dict[str, Camera], ctx: str,
                 **kwargs):
        super(ChannelInfo, self).__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self._pipeline = pipeline
        self._ctx: str = ctx
        self._cameras: Dict[str, Camera] = cameras

    @property
    def camera(self) -> Camera:
        """ 摄像头 """
        mc: ModelConfig = self._pipeline.modelConfig()
        if mc is None:
            camera_ctx = {i2: i1 for i1, i2 in zip(PipelineType.CAMERAS, PipelineType.CHANNELS)}[self._ctx]
        else:
            camera_ctx = mc.mapper[self._ctx]
        return self._cameras.get(camera_ctx, None)


class CameraMixin():
    def __init__(self):
        # 可有的摄像头示例 (物理摄像头)
        self._cameras: Dict[str, Camera] = {}
        # 通道信息
        # 四个通道信息, 可以使用不同或相同的摄像头
        self.channel_bus = {ctx: ChannelInfo(pipeline=self,
                                             cameras=self._cameras,
                                             ctx=ctx) for ctx in PipelineType.CHANNELS}
        # 光源控制串口
        self._light_source: LightSourceSerial = None
        # pice io card, 控制光源和相机触发
        self._io_card = IOCard.getCard(number=0)
        # 摄像头是否使能
        self._is_camera_enabled = False
        # 光源循环的组
        self._light_group = ((0,), (1,), (2,), (3,))
        # 光源循环的线程
        self._light_loop_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._light_loop_thread_func)
        self._light_loop_thread.daemon = True
        self._light_loop_thread.start()

    def setCameraEnabled(self, enabled: bool) -> None:
        """ 使能摄像头 """
        if enabled:
            device_list = HK_Camera.device_list()
            self._cameras.clear()
            for number, (k, port, exposure) in enumerate(zip(
                    PipelineType.CAMERAS,
                    config.get(config.Camera_Prots),
                    config.get(config.Camera_Exposures))):
                # inti camera
                try:
                    camera = Camera(read_mode=config.get(config.Camera_ReadModes)[number],
                                    img_path=config.get(config.Camera_Imgs)[number],
                                    video_path=config.get(config.Camera_Videos)[number],
                                    exposureTime=exposure,
                                    port=port,
                                    device_list=device_list)
                    self._cameras[k] = camera
                except CameraConnectError:
                    self._cameras[k] = None
                    print(f"[System] {k}: Connect failed...")
                    continue
                except Exception as e:
                    raise e

                # 如果启动了光源控制器
                # 将摄像头改为触发模式
                camera.setTrigger(isTrigger=self._light_source is not None)
                # camera.op.Set_trigger_mode(self._light_source is not None)
                print(f"[System] {k}: Connect successfully...")
        else:
            for ctx in [k for k, c in self._cameras.items() if c is not None]:
                self._cameras[ctx].close()
                self._cameras[ctx] = None
                print(f"[System] Camera: Disconnect...")

        self._is_camera_enabled = enabled

    def setLightSourceEnable(self, enable: bool):
        """
        设置光源使能
        :param enable: 使能
        """
        if enable:
            baudrate = int(config.get(config.LightSource_Baudrate))
            port = str(config.get(config.LightSource_Port))
            print(f"[System] Connect Light Source (Baudrate:{baudrate}, Port:{port})...")
            self._light_source: LightSourceSerial = LightSourceSerial.open(baudrate=baudrate, port=port)
            # 更新光源控制器的曝光组
            modelconfig = self.modelConfig()
            if modelconfig:
                self.setLightGroup(*(modelconfig.exposureBatch[ctx] for ctx in PipelineType.CAMERA_LIST))
        else:
            print(f"[System] Disconnect Light Source...")
            self._light_source.close()
            self._light_source = None
        # 同步到相机
        for number, camera in self._cameras.items():
            if camera is None: continue
            camera.setTrigger(isTrigger=self._light_source is not None)

    def setLightGroup(self, ch1: str, ch2: str, ch3: str, ch4: str) -> None:
        """ 自动优化批次, 让一次循环可以尽量多的光源 """
        tags = (ch1, ch2, ch3, ch4)
        min_tag = tuple({ch1, ch2, ch3, ch4})
        self._light_group = tuple(tuple(i for i, t in enumerate(tags) if mt == t) for mt in min_tag)

    def _light_loop_thread_func(self):
        """ 当相机启动频闪, 触发对应的摄像头 """
        while True:
            if not self.isLightSourceEnabled():
                time.sleep(1)
                continue
            for g in self._light_group:
                chs = (0 in g, 1 in g, 2 in g, 3 in g)
                chs = tuple(1 if not c else 0 for c in chs)
                self._io_card.setOutPutData(*chs, *chs)
                time.sleep(0.033333)

    def channels(self, ctx: str) -> ChannelInfo:
        return self.channel_bus.get(ctx)

    def isCameraEnabled(self) -> bool:
        """ return True if camera is enabled """
        return self._is_camera_enabled

    def isLightSourceEnabled(self) -> bool:
        """ 光源是否使能 """
        return self._light_source is not None

================================================================================

from .device import IOCard
from .device import LightningOutput
from ..config import config


class LightningMixin():
    """ 放电检测 """

    def __init__(self):
        # io card
        self.__io_card = IOCard.getCard(number=0)
        self.__io_card.registerCallback(self._interrupt)
        self.__io_card.setFallInterrupEnbale(ch0=1)
        # 是否使能
        self._is_lightning_enabled = False
        # 结果缓存
        self._output = None

    def setLightningEnabled(self, enabled: bool) -> None:
        if enabled:
            baudrate = int(config.get(config.Lightning_Baudrate))
            port = str(config.get(config.Lightning_Port))
            # print(f"[System] Connect Lightning(Baudrate:{baudrate}, Port:{port})...")
            print(f"[System] Connect Lightning...")
        else:
            print(f"[System] Disconnect Lightning...")
        self._is_lightning_enabled = enabled

    def _interrupt(self):
        """ 中断函数 """
        if not self._is_lightning_enabled: return
        if self.__io_card.getInputData()[0] == 1:
            self._output = LightningOutput(fault=True)

    def lightningOutput(self) -> LightningOutput:
        """ 读取一次结果 """
        if not self._is_lightning_enabled:
            return LightningOutput()
        return self._output or LightningOutput()

    def clearLightningOutput(self):
        """ 清空 """
        self._output = None
    def isLightningEnabled(self):
        """ 放电检测使能 """
        return self._is_lightning_enabled


================================================================================
FILE: ./core\pipeline\mixin_prepare.py
================================================================================
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict
from .meta import ModelConfig
import cv2

clahe = cv2.createCLAHE(
    clipLimit=3.0,       # 对比度限制
    tileGridSize=(8,8)   # 分块大小
)

class PrepareMixin():
    def prepare(self, images: Dict[str, np.ndarray], modelConfig: ModelConfig) -> None:
        for v, image in images.items():
            # 空图片
            if image is None: continue

            h, w = image.shape[:2]
            rectification = modelConfig.rectification.get(v)
            top_lower, top_upper, bottom_lower, bottom_upper = rectification
            if rectification == (0, 1, 0, 1): continue

            # 透视变换
            # 原图上的四个点 (顺序很重要：左上, 右上, 左下, 右下)
            pts1 = np.float32([[top_lower * w, 0], [top_upper * w, 0],
                               [bottom_lower * w, h], [bottom_upper * w, h]])
            # 变换后的目标位置 (映射成一个规则矩形)
            new_width = max(top_upper - top_lower, bottom_upper - bottom_lower) * w
            pts2 = np.float32([[0, 0], [new_width, 0], [0, h], [new_width, h]])

            # cv2.line(image, (int(top_lower * w), 0), (int(bottom_lower * w), h), (255, 0, 0), 2)
            # cv2.line(image, (int(bottom_lower * w), 0), (int(bottom_upper * w), h), (255, 0, 0), 2)
            # cv2.imwrite('results.jpg', image)
            # 计算透视变换矩阵
            M = cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(pts1, pts2)
            # 应用透视变换
            dst = cv2.warpPerspective(image, M, (int(new_width), h))
            # 直方图均衡
            enhanced = clahe.apply(dst)
            images[v] = enhanced[int(h/3):int(2*h/3)]


================================================================================
FILE: ./core\pipeline\mixin_save.py
================================================================================
import json
import time
import threading
import os
from typing import List
from .meta import FaultInfo
from ..const import Path


class SaveMixin():
    _save_thread = None
    _save_pipe: List[FaultInfo] = []

    def save(self, fault: FaultInfo):
        assert isinstance(fault, FaultInfo), f'Expected {FaultInfo.__name__}, got {type(fault)}'
        if self._save_thread is None:
            self._save_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._saveThread)
            self._save_thread.daemon = True
            self._save_thread.start()
            self._save_pipe = []
        self._save_pipe.append(fault)

    def _saveThread(self):
        while True:
            if len(self._save_pipe) == 0:
                time.sleep(0.1)
                continue

            fault = self._save_pipe.pop(0)
            # 型号
            if fault.batchName is None or fault.batchName == '':
                continue
            save_path = os.path.join(Path.OUTPUT_DIR, fault.batchName)
            if not os.path.exists(save_path):
                os.makedirs(save_path)

            # 单次识别的保存路径
            save_path = os.path.join(save_path, f'{fault.location:.2f}', fault.camera)
            if os.path.exists(save_path):
                continue  # 已经保存过了
            else:
                os.makedirs(save_path)


            with open(os.path.join(save_path, f'fault.json'), 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                print(fault.to_dict())
                json.dump(fault.to_dict(), f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=4)

            fault.saveImage(os.path.join(save_path, f'image.jpg'))
