from typing import Callable
import os
from .meta import PipelineType, ModelConfig


class SettingMixin(PipelineType):
    LightningOutput = None

    _batch_name = None  # 批次名称
    _worker_name = None  # 批次的负责人
    _detect_enable = False  # 检测使能
    _pipeline_state = ''

    # 型号配置
    _modelConfig = None

    def setCallback(self, callback):
        """" setter """
        assert callable(callback)
        self._callback = callback

    def setState(self, state: str):
        """" setter """
        # 描述pipeline的状态
        self._pipeline_state = state

    def setModelConfig(self, modelConfig: 'ModelConfig' or str):
        """" setter """
        if isinstance(modelConfig, str):
            modelConfig = ModelConfig.load(modelConfig)
        self._modelConfig = modelConfig

    def setDetectEnable(self, enable: bool):
        """" setter """
        self._detect_enable = enable

    def setBatchName(self, batch_name: str):
        """" setter """
        self._batch_name = batch_name

    def setWorkerName(self, worker_name: str):
        """" setter """
        self._worker_name = worker_name

    def state(self) -> str:
        return self._pipeline_state

    def isDetectEnabled(self) -> bool:
        return self._detect_enable

    def batchName(self):
        """ 批次名 """
        return self._batch_name

    def workerName(self):
        """ 批次的负责人 """
        return self._worker_name

    def callback(self) -> Callable:
        return self._callback

    def modelConfig(self) -> 'ModelConfig':
        return self._modelConfig


================================================================================
FILE: ./core\pipeline\mixin_vsampler.py
================================================================================
import threading
import time

from .device import VSamplerSerial
from ..config import config


class VSamplerMixin():
    def __init__(self):
        self._vsampler = None
        self._location_timer = threading.Thread(target=self._locationThread)
        self._location_timer.daemon = True

        # 计米轮累加
        self._accumulated_distance = 0
        self._v_last_timestep = None
        self._is_vsampler_enabled = False

    def setVSamplerEnabled(self, enabled: bool) -> None:

        if enabled:
            baudrate = int(config.get(config.VSampler_Baudrate))
            port = str(config.get(config.VSampler_Port))
            print(f"[System] Connect VSampler(Baudrate:{baudrate}, Port:{port})...")
            self._vsampler: VSamplerSerial = VSamplerSerial.open(baudrate=baudrate, port=port)
        else:
            print(f"[System] Disconnect VSampler...")
            self._vsampler.close()
            self._vsampler = None

        self._is_vsampler_enabled = enabled

    def _locationThread(self):
        while True:
            st = time.time()
            if self._v_last_timestep is None:
                self._v_last_timestep = time.time()
            else:
                v_now_time = time.time()
                # 读取位置
                v = self.velocity()  # 速度 m/s
                timestep = v_now_time - self._v_last_timestep
                self._accumulated_distance += v * timestep
                # cache
                self._v_last_timestep = v_now_time
            # 30FPS
            if (sleep_time := time.time() - st) < 1 / 30:
                time.sleep(sleep_time)

    def velocity(self) -> float:
        ''' 速度: m/min '''
        return self.angularVelocity() * config.get(config.VSampler_Perimeter)

    def angularVelocity(self) -> float:
        ''' 速度: 转/s '''
        if self._vsampler is None:
            return 0.0
        # 编码器旋转速度 = 编码器角速度值 / 单圈分辨率 / 转速计算时间（单位：转 / 分钟）
        # 采样时间 100ms
        av = self._vsampler.frequency() / 32678 / 0.1 * 60
        # conver 单位: 转 / 秒
        av = av / 60
        return av * (-1 if config.get(config.VSampler_Direction) else 1)

    def accumulatedDistance(self) -> float:
        """ 检测的累计距离 """
        return self._accumulated_distance

    def isVSamplerEnabled(self):
        """ 计米轮使能 """
        return self._is_vsampler_enabled


================================================================================
FILE: ./core\pipeline\pipeline.py
================================================================================
import os.path
import pickle
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
from multiprocessing import shared_memory
from os import times_result
from typing import List,Iterable

import numpy as np

from .fault_detection import DetectOutput
from .meta import FaultInfo
from .mixin_camera import CameraMixin
from .mixin_lightning import LightningMixin
from .mixin_prepare import PrepareMixin
from .mixin_save import SaveMixin
from .mixin_setting import SettingMixin
from .mixin_vsampler import VSamplerMixin

WRITE_FINISHED_FLAG = 0
DETECT_FINISHED_FLAG = 1


class WorkerOutput():
    def get(self) -> List[DetectOutput]:
        raise NotImplementedError()


work_cache = {}


def init_work(width, height, imgsize, ctx: str):
    """ 初始化一个 work"""
    if ctx in work_cache:
        task, img_shm, op_shm, out_shm, _width, _height = work_cache[ctx]
        if _width == width and _height == height and img_shm.size == imgsize:
            return img_shm, op_shm, out_shm
        else:
            print('[System][Pipeline] 原 Worker 参数不匹配, 重新启动...')
            task.kill()

    # 共享内存
    print('[System][Pipeline] 分配共享内存.')
    img_shm = shared_memory.SharedMemory(create=True, size=imgsize)  # 只要 1 个字节
    op_shm = shared_memory.SharedMemory(create=True, size=2)
    out_shm = shared_memory.SharedMemory(create=True, size=2 ** 16)

    print('[System][Pipeline] 解析器路径:', sys.executable)

    if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
        # 用户环境
        print('[System][Pipeline] 用户环境...')
        exe_path = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "detect_worker", "detect_worker.exe")
        print('[System][Pipeline] exe路径:', exe_path)
        exe_path = [exe_path]
    else:
        # 开发环境
        print('[System][Pipeline] 用户环境...')
        print('[System][Pipeline] 解析器路径:', sys.executable)
        assert str(sys.executable).endswith('python.exe'), ''
        start_path = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), 'core', 'pipeline', 'detect_worker.py')
        exe_path = [sys.executable, start_path, ]

    task = subprocess.Popen(exe_path + ['--width', str(width),
                                        '--height', str(height),
                                        '--img_shm_name', img_shm.name,
                                        '--op_shm_name', op_shm.name,
                                        '--out_shm_name', out_shm.name,
                                        ],
                            creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP)
    work_cache[ctx] = (task, img_shm, op_shm, out_shm, width, height)
    return img_shm, op_shm, out_shm


def detect_by_work(img: np.ndarray, ctx) -> WorkerOutput:
    """ 使用 worker 进程 异步处理, 加快速度"""
    img_shm, op_shm, out_shm = init_work(width=img.shape[1], height=img.shape[0], imgsize=img.nbytes, ctx=ctx)

    # 将 img 拷贝到共享内存
    b = np.ndarray(img.shape, dtype=img.dtype, buffer=img_shm.buf)
    b[:] = img[:]
    # reset : wait detect finish
    op_shm.buf[DETECT_FINISHED_FLAG] = 0
    op_shm.buf[WRITE_FINISHED_FLAG] = 1

    def wapper():
        while op_shm.buf[DETECT_FINISHED_FLAG] != 1:
            time.sleep(0.01)
        # 先获取数据大小, 然后读取数据
        data_length = int.from_bytes(out_shm.buf[-1024:], byteorder='big')
        output: List[DetectOutput] = pickle.loads(out_shm.buf[:data_length])

        # reset
        op_shm.buf[DETECT_FINISHED_FLAG] = 0
        op_shm.buf[WRITE_FINISHED_FLAG] = 0

        return [DetectOutput(**o) for o in output]

    output = WorkerOutput()
    output.get = wapper
    return output


class FaultDetectionPipeline(SettingMixin, PrepareMixin, SaveMixin, VSamplerMixin, CameraMixin, LightningMixin):

    def __init__(self):
        VSamplerMixin.__init__(self)
        CameraMixin.__init__(self)
        LightningMixin.__init__(self)

        # 当前批次开启时的累计距离
        self._accumulatedDistance_for_batch = 0
        # 处理的fps
        self._process_time = None
        # 最后处理的缺陷
        self._last_faults = None

    def start(self):
        """ start detection pipeline """
        self._location_timer.start()
        for thread_func in (self._image_detect_thread, self._lightning_detect_thread):
            t = threading.Thread(target=thread_func)
            t.daemon = True
            t.start()

    def swapBatch(self, batch_name: str,woker_name:str):
        super(FaultDetectionPipeline, self).setBatchName(batch_name)
        super(FaultDetectionPipeline, self).setWorkerName(woker_name)
        self._accumulatedDistance_for_batch = self.accumulatedDistance()

    def setModelConfig(self, modelConfig: 'ModelConfig' or str):
        super(FaultDetectionPipeline, self).setModelConfig(modelConfig=modelConfig)
        config = self.modelConfig()
        # 更新光源控制器的曝光组
        self.setLightGroup(*(config.exposureBatch[ctx] for ctx in self.CAMERA_LIST))

    def _image_detect_thread(self):
        """ 检测线程: 对于四个通道的摄像头数据进行图像处理"""
        def detect_image():
            # ===========================
            # 读取图片, 图像识别
            # ==========================
            imgs = {k: None if info.camera is None else info.camera.read() for k, info in self.channel_bus.items()}

            # 预处理图像
            # 同时判断改管线是否需要处理
            if self.modelConfig() is not None:
                # 如果不使能, 则置空后续会掉过处理
                imgs = {k: v if self.modelConfig().enable[k] else None for k, v in imgs.items()}
                self.prepare(images=imgs, modelConfig=self.modelConfig())
            # =========================================
            # 处理图片
            # 多进程, 需要异步获取结果
            # =========================================
            worker, is_exist_fault = {}, False
            for k, img in imgs.items():
                if img is None:
                    self.channel_bus[k].clear()
                else:
                    is_exist_fault = True
                    worker[k] = detect_by_work(img, ctx=k)
                    self.channel_bus[k].detect_image = img
                    self.channel_bus[k].oringe_image = img
            for k, output in worker.items(): self.channel_bus[k].faults = output.get()
            if is_exist_fault and self._callback is not None: self._callback()

            # save
            # 如果没有设置批次则不保存
            result = []
            for k, v in self.channel_bus.items():
                info = self.channel_bus[k]
                if len(info.faults) == 0: continue
                result.append(FaultInfo(image=info.detect_image,
                                        faults=info.faults,
                                        location=self.accumulatedDistance(obj='batch'),
                                        batchName=self.batchName(),
                                        worker=self.workerName(),
                                        sampler=k,
                                        camera=k, ))


            return result


        def detect_lightning():
            # 读取结果
            output = self.lightningOutput()
            output.distance = self.accumulatedDistance(obj='batch')
            result = []
            if output.fault:  # <--存在缺陷
                result.append(FaultInfo(camera='lightning',
                                        faults=[DetectOutput(0, 0, 0, 0, ftype='针孔缺陷')],
                                        location=output.distance,
                                        batchName=self.batchName(),
                                        sampler="放电检测",
                                        worker=self.workerName()))
                # sleep
                time.sleep(0.3)
                self.clearLightningOutput()
            return result

        while True:
            # ------
            # 是否启动了检测功能
            # ----
            if not self.isDetectEnabled():
                self.setState(state=self.State_DetectDisable)
                time.sleep(0.1)
                continue
            st = time.time()
            # -----  core ----
            result = []
            result += detect_image()
            result += detect_lightning()
            self._last_faults = result
            # 回调
            if len(result) != 0:
                self.callback()()

            # 保存
            post_fix = f', 无保存'
            if self.batchName() is not None and self.batchName() != '':
                for f in result: self.save(fault=f)
                post_fix = f', 保存至{self.batchName()}'
            self.setState(state=SettingMixin.State_Normal + post_fix)

            # 如果没有读取到数据,睡眠, 不要阻塞UI线程
            interval = time.time() - st
            if (sleep_time := 0.03 - (interval)) > 0: time.sleep(sleep_time)
            # 记录处理需要的时间, 使用指数平滑
            self._process_time = (self._process_time or interval) * 0.1 + 0.9 * interval

    def _lightning_detect_thread(self):
        while True:
            # ------
            # 是否启动了检测功能
            # ----
            if not self.isDetectEnabled():
                # self.setState(state=self.State_DetectDisable)
                time.sleep(0.1)
                continue
            # 没有启动放电检测, 不用进行读取
            st = time.time()


            # 如果没有读取到数据,睡眠, 不要阻塞UI线程
            if (sleep_time := 0.03 - (time.time() - st)) > 0: time.sleep(sleep_time)

    def accumulatedDistance(self, obj='all') -> float:
        """ 检测的累计距离 """
        # 总的累计距离
        if obj == 'all':
            return VSamplerMixin.accumulatedDistance(self)
        # 只统计当前批次的累计距离
        elif obj == 'batch':
            return VSamplerMixin.accumulatedDistance(self) - self._accumulatedDistance_for_batch
        else:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Not researchable: obj={obj}")

    def processTime(self):
        """ 处理一次需要的时间 """
        return self._process_time or 0

    def pipelineState(self) -> str:
        """ 管线的当前状态 """
        return self._pipeline_state

    def lastFault(self)->Iterable[FaultInfo]:
        return self._last_faults



faultDetectionPipeline = None
faultDetectionPipeline = FaultDetectionPipeline()

class CameraAbstract():
    _isTrigger = False

    def setExposure(self, exposure: int):
        pass

    def setTrigger(self, isTrigger: bool):
        """ 设置触发模式 """
        self._isTrigger = isTrigger

    def trigger(self) -> None:
        """ 触发 """
        pass

    def isTrigger(self):
        return self._isTrigger

    def close(self):
        pass


class CaptrueCamera(CameraAbstract):
    def __init__(self, idx: int):
        cap = cv2.Capture(idx)

        if not cap.isOpened():
            raise OpencvCameraConnectError("摄像头path打开失败")


class VedioCamera(CameraAbstract):
    def __init__(self, path: str):
        cap = cv2.VideoCapture()
        cap.open(path, cv2.CAP_FFMPEG)

        if not cap.isOpened():
            raise OpencvCameraConnectError("摄像头path打开失败")


class ImageCamera(CameraAbstract):
    def __init__(self, path: str):
        data = np.fromfile(path, dtype=np.uint8)
        img = cv2.imdecode(data, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
        self._img = cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        cv2.imwrite(os.path.basename(path), self._img)

    def read(self):
        return self._img


class Camera():
    ReadMode_Hk = 'Mode_Hk'
    ReadMode_Capture = 'ReadMode_Capture'
    ReadMode_Vedio = 'ReadMode_OpenCV'
    ReadMode_Img = 'Mode_img'

    @classmethod
    def HK_deivces(cls):
        """ get all HK camera deivces"""
        return HK_Camera.enum_devices()

    def __init__(self,
                 port=None,
                 capture=None,
                 device_list=None,
                 img_path=None,
                 video_path=None,
                 exposureTime=None,
                 read_mode=ReadMode_Hk):

        if read_mode == self.ReadMode_Hk:
            self._capture = HKCamera(port=port, device_list=device_list)
            if exposureTime:
                self._capture.setExposure(exposureTime=exposureTime)
        elif read_mode == self.ReadMode_Capture:
            self._capture = CaptrueCamera(idx=capture)
        elif read_mode == self.ReadMode_Vedio:
            self._capture = VedioCamera(path=video_path)
        elif read_mode == self.ReadMode_Img:
            self._capture = ImageCamera(path=img_path)

    def read(self):
        """ get a frame"""
        return self._capture.read()

    def setExposure(self, exposure: int) -> None:
        """ set exposure """
        self._capture.setExposure(exposure)

    def setTrigger(self, isTrigger: bool):
        self._capture.setTrigger(isTrigger=isTrigger)

    def trigger(self) -> None:
        self._capture.trigger()

    def isTrigger(self) -> bool:
        return self._capture.isTrigger()

    def close(self):
        return self._capture.close()

import ctypes
import time
from typing import Callable, List

lib_path = "..\\..\\..\\_rc\\WENYU_EIO16P.dll" if __name__ == "__main__" else "_rc\\WENYU_EIO16P.dll"
lib = ctypes.CDLL(lib_path)  # Windows 用 add.dll

# callback
INTERRUP_CALLBACK = ctypes.CFUNCTYPE(None)

# WY_Open
lib.WY_Open.argtypes = []
lib.WY_Open.restype = ctypes.c_int
# WY_Close
lib.WY_Close.argtypes = []
lib.WY_Close.restype = ctypes.c_int
# int WY_SetOutPutData(int CardNo,unsigned char OutPutData);
lib.WY_SetOutPutData.argtypes = [
    ctypes.c_int,
    ctypes.c_char
]
lib.WY_SetOutPutData.restype = ctypes.c_int
# int WY_GetInPutData (int CardNo,unsigned char &Data);
lib.WY_GetInPutData.argtypes = [
    ctypes.c_int,  # CardNo
    ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_ubyte)  # unsigned char*
]
lib.WY_GetInPutData.restype = ctypes.c_int
# int WY_SetInterruptFun(int CardNo, EventInterrupt InterruptFun);
lib.WY_SetInterruptFun.argtypes = [
    ctypes.c_int,
    INTERRUP_CALLBACK]
lib.WY_SetInterruptFun.restype = ctypes.c_int
# int WY_SetInterruptPortEnable(int CardNo, byte RiseEnable, byte FallEnable);
lib.WY_SetInterruptPortEnable.argtypes = [
    ctypes.c_int,  # CardNo
    ctypes.c_ubyte,  # RiseEnable
    ctypes.c_ubyte  # FallEnable
]
lib.WY_SetInterruptPortEnable.restype = ctypes.c_int
# int WY_ResetInterrupt(int CardNo);
lib.WY_ResetInterrupt.argtypes = [
    ctypes.c_int,  # CardNo
]
lib.WY_ResetInterrupt.restype = ctypes.c_int
# int WY_SetInterruptEnable(int CardNo, int Enable);
lib.WY_SetInterruptEnable.argtypes = [
    ctypes.c_int,  # CardNo
    ctypes.c_int,  # enable
]
lib.WY_SetInterruptEnable.restype = ctypes.c_int
# int WY_ClrInterruptRisePort0(int CardNo);
lib.WY_ClrInterruptRisePort0.argtypes = [
    ctypes.c_int,  # CardNo
]
lib.WY_ClrInterruptRisePort0.restype = ctypes.c_int
# int WY_ClrInterruptFallPort0(int CardNo);
lib.WY_ClrInterruptFallPort0.argtypes = [
    ctypes.c_int,  # CardNo
]
lib.WY_ClrInterruptFallPort0.restype = ctypes.c_int


class IOCard():
    __cards = {}

    @staticmethod
    def to8bit(ch0: int = None, ch1: int = None, ch2: int = None, ch3: int = None,
               ch4: int = None, ch5: int = None, ch6: int = None, ch7: int = None,
               revise=False):
        chs = (ch7, ch6, ch5, ch4, ch3, ch2, ch1, ch0)
        if revise:
            string = int("".join(['0' if c == 1 else '1' for c in chs]), 2)
        else:
            string = int("".join([str(c) for c in chs]), 2)
        return string

    @classmethod
    def getCard(cls, number: int) -> 'IOCard':
        if number not in cls.__cards:
            IOCard.open()
            cls.__cards[number] = cls(number=number)
        return cls.__cards[number]

    @classmethod
    def open(self):
        return lib.WY_Open()

    @classmethod
    def close(self):
        return lib.WY_Close()

    def __init__(self, number):
        self._enable = self.open() > number
        # assert self.open() > number
        self._number = number
        self._output_chs = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]
        self._rise_interrupt_enable = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]
        self._fall_interrupt_enable = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]
        self._interrupt_callback_pool: List[Callable] = []
        self.setOutPutData()

        @INTERRUP_CALLBACK
        def _interrupt_callback():
            for c in self._interrupt_callback_pool:
                c()
            lib.WY_ClrInterruptFallPort0(self._number)
            lib.WY_ResetInterrupt(self._number)

        self._interrupt_callback = _interrupt_callback

        lib.WY_SetInterruptEnable(self._number, 1)
        ret = lib.WY_SetInterruptFun(self._number, self._interrupt_callback)
        assert ret == 0, '中断函数设置失败'

    def registerCallback(self, callback: Callable):
        self._interrupt_callback_pool.append(callback)

    def setOutPutData(self,
                      ch0: int = None, ch1: int = None, ch2: int = None, ch3: int = None,
                      ch4: int = None, ch5: int = None, ch6: int = None, ch7: int = None):
        if not self._enable: return -1
        chs = [c1 if c2 is None else c2 for c1, c2 in zip(self._output_chs, (ch0, ch1, ch2, ch3, ch4, ch5, ch6, ch7,))]
        self._output_chs = chs
        string = self.to8bit(*chs, revise=True)
        ret = lib.WY_SetOutPutData(self._number, string)
        assert ret == 0, 'ret'
        return ret

    def getInputData(self):
        data = ctypes.c_ubyte()  # 分配 1 字节
        ret = lib.WY_GetInPutData(self._number, ctypes.byref(data))
        assert ret == 0
        mask = data.value
        return [(mask >> i) & 1 for i in range(8)]

    def setRiseInterrupEnbale(self, ch0: int = None, ch1: int = None, ch2: int = None, ch3: int = None,
                              ch4: int = None, ch5: int = None, ch6: int = None, ch7: int = None):
        enable = [c1 if c2 is None else c2 for c1, c2 in zip(self._rise_interrupt_enable,
                                                             (ch0, ch1, ch2, ch3, ch4, ch5, ch6, ch7,))]
        self._rise_interrupt_enable = enable
        ret = lib.WY_SetInterruptPortEnable(self._number,
                                            self.to8bit(*self._rise_interrupt_enable, revise=False),
                                            self.to8bit(*self._fall_interrupt_enable, revise=False), )
        assert ret == 0

    def setFallInterrupEnbale(self, ch0: int = None, ch1: int = None, ch2: int = None, ch3: int = None,
                              ch4: int = None, ch5: int = None, ch6: int = None, ch7: int = None):
        enable = [c1 if c2 is None else c2 for c1, c2 in zip(self._fall_interrupt_enable,
                                                             (ch0, ch1, ch2, ch3, ch4, ch5, ch6, ch7,))]
        self._fall_interrupt_enable = enable
        ret = lib.WY_SetInterruptPortEnable(self._number,
                                            self.to8bit(*self._rise_interrupt_enable, revise=False),
                                            self.to8bit(*self._fall_interrupt_enable, revise=False), )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # 打开 IO card
    card_num = IOCard.open()
    print(f'卡片数量:{card_num}')

    card = IOCard(0)

    for _ in range(1000):
        time.sleep(2)

        print(2)
        card.setOutPutData(ch0=0, ch1=0, ch2=1, ch3=1, ch4=0, ch5=0, ch6=0, ch7=0)

import time, json

from dataclasses import dataclass
import serial
import threading
from ..._serial import Serial
from ..error import LightningSerialConnectError

@dataclass
class LightningOutput():
    fault: bool = False
    distance: float = 0.0

    @classmethod
    def dumps(cls, string: str):
        cfg = json.loads(string)
        return cls(
            fault=bool(cfg['fault']),
        )


class LightningSerial(Serial):
    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super(LightningSerial, self).__init__(*args, **kwargs)

        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._monitor)
        self._thread.daemon = True
        self._close_lock = threading.Lock()
        # 读取缓存
        self._cache: LightningOutput = LightningOutput()
        self._is_contiune = False

    def setSerial(self, serial: serial.Serial) -> None:
        super(LightningSerial, self).setSerial(serial=serial)
        # 检测是否是用于放电检测的串口
        serial.write('enable(False)\n'.encode())# 用于停止终端发送消息
        time.sleep(0.1)
        serial.reset_input_buffer()
        serial.write('machine()\n'.encode())
        time.sleep(0.1)
        result = serial.readline().decode()
        if result.startswith('Lightning-Moniter'):
            # 测试通过
            pass
        else:
            raise LightningSerialConnectError("该串口不能用于放电检测")
        serial.write('enable(True)\n'.encode())  # 通过测试开始监控
        self._thread.start() # 启动监控, 循环读取放电检测消息

    def close(self):
        """ 关闭, 防止线程冲突 """
        self._is_contiune = False
        self._serial.write('emptyOutput()\n'.encode())  # 用于停止终端发送消息
        time.sleep(0.1)
        r = super(LightningSerial, self).close()
        return r

    def _monitor(self):

        self._is_contiune = True
        # 之前的检测结果缓存与缓冲
        time.sleep(0.5)
        if self.isOpen():
            self._serial.reset_input_buffer()
        while self._is_contiune:
            if self.isOpen():
                with self._close_lock:
                    string = self._serial.readline().decode()
            else:
                continue
            self._cache = LightningOutput.dumps(string)

    def read(self) -> LightningOutput:
        return self._cache


================================================================================
FILE: ./core\pipeline\device\light_source.py
================================================================================
import threading
import time
from functools import lru_cache

import serial
from typing import Callable, Optional

from core._serial import Serial
from ..error import LightSourceControlerSerialConnectError


@lru_cache(maxsize=100)
def commandTamplate(number: int, level: int, closeOther=False, closeFace=False, state=(0, 0, 0, 0)):
    ch = ["A", 'B', "C", "D"]
    if closeOther:
        command = (f'S{ch[0]}{level if number == 0 else 0:0>4}#',
                   f'S{ch[1]}{level if number == 1 else 0:0>4}#',
                   f'S{ch[2]}{level if number == 2 else 0:0>4}#',
                   f'S{ch[3]}{level if number == 3 else 0:0>4}#')

        target = [0, 0, 0, 0]
        target[number] = level
        command = ''.join([c for c, l, s in zip(command, target, state) if l != s])
    elif closeFace:
        target = [0, 0, 0, 0]
        target[number] = level
        target[{0: 1, 1: 0, 2: 3, 3: 2}[number]] = level
        command = ''.join([f'S{ch[i]}{target[i]:0>4}#' for i in range(4)])
    else:
        command = f'S{ch[number]}{level:0>4}#'
    return command.encode('utf8'), tuple(target)

@lru_cache(maxsize=100)
def getLevelTamplate(number: int):
    ch = ["A", 'B', "C", "D"]
    command = f'S{ch[number]}#'
    return command.encode('utf8')

@lru_cache(maxsize=100)
def switchamplate(ch1: bool, ch2: bool, ch3: bool, ch4: bool):
    """开关光源 (响应速度优先)"""
    ch1 = "1" if ch1 else "0"
    ch2 = "1" if ch2 else "0"
    ch3 = "1" if ch3 else "0"
    ch4 = "1" if ch4 else "0"
    return f'switch({ch1},{ch2},{ch3},{ch4})\n'.encode()


class LightSourceSerial_(Serial):
    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self._single_cycle_resolution = None
        self._state = (0, 0, 0, 0)
        self._is_loop = False
        self._group = ((0,), (1,), (2,), (3,))  # 同批次发光的组
        super(LightSourceSerial, self).__init__(*args, **kwargs)

        self._rw_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._close_lock = threading.Lock()
        self.ch_lock = (threading.Lock(), threading.Lock(), threading.Lock(), threading.Lock())

    def setSerial(self, serial: serial.Serial) -> None:
        super(LightSourceSerial, self).setSerial(serial=serial)
        # 检测是否是用于放电检测的串口
        serial.reset_input_buffer()
        serial.write('machine()\n'.encode())
        time.sleep(0.1)
        result = serial.readline().decode()
        if result.startswith('Light-Source-Pico'):
            pass  # 测试通过
        else:
            raise LightSourceControlerSerialConnectError(f"设备标识为:{result},该串口不能用于光源控制")

    def setGroup(self, ch1: str, ch2: str, ch3: str, ch4: str) -> None:
        """ 自动优化批次, 让一次循环可以尽量多的光源 """
        tags = (ch1, ch2, ch3, ch4)
        min_tag = tuple({ch1, ch2, ch3, ch4})
        self._group = tuple(tuple(i for i, t in enumerate(tags) if mt == t) for mt in min_tag)

    def close(self):
        """ 关闭, 防止线程冲突 """
        # 先关闭循环
        if self._is_loop:
            self._is_loop = False
            time.sleep(0.1)
        with self._close_lock:
            r = super(LightSourceSerial, self).close()
            for lock in self.ch_lock:
                if lock.locked():
                    lock.release()
        return r

    def startLoop(self, callback: Optional[Callable] = None):
        """启动循环"""
        self._t = threading.Thread(target=self.loop)
        self._t.daemon = True
        self._is_loop = True
        self._callback = callback
        self._t.start()

    def loop(self):
        """循环屏闪"""

        def release(idx):
            if self.ch_lock[idx].locked():
                self.ch_lock[idx].release()

        # 全部上锁, 只有亮才解锁
        for lock in self.ch_lock:
            lock.acquire()
        # ============= 频闪 ==============
        # 使用switch进行频闪, 这样的响应速度最快
        # 1.光源开关
        # 2.释放线程锁, 让摄像头读取线程更新图片
        # ================================
        while self._serial.isOpen() and self._is_loop:
            for g in self._group:
                # self.switch(ch1=0 in g, ch2=1 in g, ch3=2 in g, ch4=3 in g, )
                time.sleep(0.0333)
                # for i in g: release(i)
                if self._callback is not None: self._callback(ch1=0 in g, ch2=1 in g, ch3=2 in g, ch4=3 in g)
                # time.sleep(0.03)  # 释放锁,让其他线程有时间接管程序
                # for i in g: self.ch_lock[i].acquire()

    def switch(self, ch1: bool, ch2: bool, ch3: bool, ch4: bool):
        """开关光源 (响应速度优先)"""
        s = switchamplate(ch1=ch1, ch2=ch2, ch3=ch3, ch4=ch4)

        self._serial.write(s)
        while self._serial.out_waiting > 0:
            time.sleep(0.001)

    def setLightLevel(self, number: int, level: int, closeOther=False, closeFace=False) -> None:
        """
        调整光源的亮度
        :param number: 第几路光源
        :param level:亮度
        :param closeOther:其他路型号自动关闭
        """
        with self._close_lock:
            # print(command)
            if self._serial.isOpen():
                command, state = commandTamplate(number=number, level=level, closeOther=closeOther, closeFace=closeFace,
                                                 state=self._state)
                # self._serial.reset_input_buffer()
                self._serial.write(command)
                self._serial.reset_input_buffer()
                self._state = state


class LightSourceSerial(Serial):
    @classmethod
    def open(cls, port: str, baudrate: int) -> 'Serial':
        _instance = cls()
        # 延时100ms
        _instance.setSerial(serial=serial.Serial(port=port,
                                                 baudrate=baudrate,
                                                 timeout=1))
        return _instance

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self._single_cycle_resolution = None
        self._state = (0, 0, 0, 0)
        self._is_loop = False
        self._group = ((0,), (1,), (2,), (3,))  # 同批次发光的组
        super(LightSourceSerial, self).__init__(*args, **kwargs)

        self._rw_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._close_lock = threading.Lock()


    def setSerial(self, serial: serial.Serial) -> None:
        super(LightSourceSerial, self).setSerial(serial=serial)
        # 检测是否是用于放电检测的串口
        serial.reset_input_buffer()
        serial.write(getLevelTamplate(number=0))
        time.sleep(0.1)
        result = serial.readline().decode()
        if result.startswith('a'):
            pass  # 测试通过
        else:
            raise LightSourceControlerSerialConnectError(f"设备标识为:{result},该串口不能用于光源控制")

    def close(self):
        """ 关闭, 防止线程冲突 """
        # 先关闭循环
        if self._is_loop:
            self._is_loop = False
            time.sleep(0.1)
        with self._close_lock:
            r = super(LightSourceSerial, self).close()
        return r

    def setLightLevel(self, number: int, level: int, closeOther=False, closeFace=False) -> None:
        """
        调整光源的亮度
        :param number: 第几路光源
        :param level:亮度
        :param closeOther:其他路型号自动关闭
        """
        with self._close_lock:
            # print(command)
            if self._serial.isOpen():
                command, state = commandTamplate(number=number, level=level, closeOther=closeOther, closeFace=closeFace,
                                                 state=self._state)
                # self._serial.reset_input_buffer()
                self._serial.write(command)
                self._serial.reset_input_buffer()
                self._state = state

    def getLightLevel(self, number):
        """ get level """
        command = getLevelTamplate(number=number)
        self._serial.write(command)
        self._serial.reset_input_buffer()
        result = serial.readline().decode()
        return result

================================================================================
FILE: ./core\pipeline\device\v_sampler.py
================================================================================
import serial
import binascii
import threading
import struct
from typing import List

from core._serial import Serial
from typing import overload


class VSamplerSerial(Serial):
    TX_ANGLE = bytes.fromhex('01 03 00 00 00 01 84 0A')
    RX_ANGLE = 7
    TX_VELOCITY = bytes.fromhex('01 03 00 03 00 01 74 0A')
    RX_VELOCITY = 7
    TX_SINGLE_CYCLE_RESOLUTION = bytes.fromhex('01 03 00 88 00 01 84 0A')
    RX_SINGLE_CYCLE_RESOLUTION = 7

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self._frequency = 0
        self._single_cycle_resolution = None
        super(VSamplerSerial, self).__init__(*args, **kwargs)

        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._monitor)
        self._thread.daemon = True
        self._rw_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._close_lock = threading.Lock()

    def setSerial(self, serial: serial.Serial) -> None:
        super(VSamplerSerial, self).setSerial(serial=serial)
        self._thread.start()

    def close(self):
        """ 关闭, 防止线程冲突"""
        with self._close_lock:
            r = super(VSamplerSerial, self).close()
        return r

    def _monitor(self):
        while self.isOpen():
            with self._close_lock:
                if not self.isOpen(): break
                hex_list = self._write_and_read(tx=self.TX_VELOCITY, rx=self.RX_VELOCITY)
                # Rx:01 03 02 02 7A (D8 C6)
                # 校验位已删除
                hex_list = hex_list[-4:-2]
                self._frequency = self.hex2int(string=''.join(hex_list), bin=16, signed=True)

    def _write_and_read(self, tx: bytes, rx: int) -> List[str]:
        with self._rw_lock:
            self._serial.write(tx)
            data = self._serial.read(rx)
            self._serial.flushInput()

        hex_list = [format(byte, '02X') for byte in data]
        return hex_list

    def frequency(self) -> int:
        return self._frequency

import numba

import cv2, math
import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass
from . import numba_jit


class FaultEnum():
    Sticky_Line = '毛粘线'
    Bubble = '汽包'
    Particle = '粒子'
    Tumor= '漆瘤'


@dataclass
class AnaylsisOutput:
    has_fault: bool
    fault_type: str = ''


@numba.njit
def _jit_std_with_mask(arr, mean, mask):
    """ 计算 mask区域中的方差 """
    std_outside = np.sum((arr - mean) ** 2 * mask) / np.sum(mask)
    return std_outside


@numba.njit
def _jit_mean_with_mask(arr, mask):
    """ 计算 mask区域中的方差 """
    mean = np.sum(arr * mask) / np.sum(mask)
    return mean


@numba.njit
def _jit_feature_with_gxy_residual(gxy, residual, mask):
    """ 计算 mask区域中的方差 """
    num = np.sum(mask)
    feature_gxy = np.sum(gxy * mask) / num
    feature_residual = np.sum(residual * mask) / num
    return feature_gxy, feature_residual


@numba.njit
def _jit_mask_and_crop(img, x, y, w, h):
    height, width = img.shape[:2]
    edge_w, edge_h = int(w * 0.2), int(h * 0.2)
    edge_left, edge_right = edge_w, edge_w
    edge_top, edge_bottom = edge_h, edge_h
    x1, y1, x2, y2 = int(x - edge_left), int(y - edge_top), int(x + w + edge_right), int(y + h + edge_bottom)
    _x1, _y1, _x2, _y2 = max(x1, 0), max(y1, 0), min(x2, width), min(y2, height)
    edge_left, edge_right = edge_left - abs(x1 - _x1), edge_w - abs(x2 - _x2)
    edge_top, edge_bottom = edge_h - abs(y1 - _y1), edge_h - abs(y2 - _y2)

    crop_img = img[_y1:_y2, _x1:_x2]
    mask = np.ones(shape=crop_img.shape[:2])
    mask[edge_top:-edge_bottom, edge_left:- edge_right] = 0
    return crop_img, mask, _x1, _y1


def anaylsis(cnt: np.ndarray,
             img: np.ndarray,
             gxy: np.ndarray,
             residual: np.ndarray,
             threshold: float, ) -> AnaylsisOutput:
    # # crop
    x, y, w, h = cv2.boundingRect(cnt)

    crop_img, mask, x1, y1 = _jit_mask_and_crop(img=img, x=x, y=y, w=w, h=h)
    mean_outside = _jit_mean_with_mask(arr=crop_img, mask=mask)

    mask[:, :] = 0
    _cnt = numba_jit.add(cnt, np.array((-x1, -y1)))
    cv2.drawContours(mask, [_cnt], -1, 1, -1)

    # 方差
    std_outside = _jit_std_with_mask(arr=crop_img, mean=mean_outside, mask=mask)
    # std_outside = np.sum((crop_img - mean_outside) ** 2 * mask_cnt) / np.sum(mask_cnt)

    # 计算梯度方向
    gx = cv2.Sobel(crop_img, cv2.CV_32F, 1, 0, ksize=3)
    gy = cv2.Sobel(crop_img, cv2.CV_32F, 0, 1, ksize=3)
    # angle = np.arctan2(gy, gx)
    # 转成向量（关键！避免角度跳变）
    # cos_a = np.cos(angle)
    # sin_a = np.sin(angle)
    # 在候选区域内计算“方向一致性”
    # mean_cos = np.mean(cos_a)
    # mean_sin = np.mean(sin_a)
    # coherence = np.sqrt(mean_cos ** 2 + mean_sin ** 2)
    # print(coherence.max(), coherence.min())
    Jxx = cv2.GaussianBlur(gx * gx, (9, 9), 0)
    Jyy = cv2.GaussianBlur(gy * gy, (9, 9), 0)
    Jxy = cv2.GaussianBlur(gx * gy, (9, 9), 0)
    tmp = np.sqrt((Jxx - Jyy) ** 2 + 4 * Jxy * Jxy)
    lambda1 = (Jxx + Jyy + tmp) / 2
    lambda2 = (Jxx + Jyy - tmp) / 2
    coherence = (lambda1 - lambda2) / (lambda1 + lambda2 + 1e-5)

    print('coherence:', coherence.min())

    # mask = 255 - mask.astype(np.uint8) * 255
    # result_ns = cv2.inpaint(crop_img, mask, 7, cv2.INPAINT_TELEA)  # Navier-Stokes
    # ssim_index = compute_ssim(crop_img, result_ns)
    # ssim = cv2.compare_ssim(crop_img, mask, multichannel=True)
    # print(u, std_outside, ssim)
    # print()
    # cv2.imwrite(f'cache/anaylsis-crop-{u}.jpg', crop_img)
    # u += 1
    has_fault = std_outside > threshold and coherence.min() < 0.45

    # 没有缺陷
    if not has_fault:
        return AnaylsisOutput(
            has_fault=has_fault
        )

    # ===================
    # 分析缺陷类型
    # ===================
    crop_h, crop_w = crop_img.shape[:2]
    crop_gxy = gxy[y1:y1 + crop_h, x1:x1 + crop_w]
    crop_residual = residual[y1:y1 + crop_h, x1:x1 + crop_w]
    feature_gxy, feature_residual = _jit_feature_with_gxy_residual(gxy=crop_gxy, residual=crop_residual, mask=mask)
    area = cv2.contourArea(cnt)  # 计算面积
    perimeter = cv2.arcLength(cnt, True)  # 计算周长
    a_p_r = area * math.pi * 4 / perimeter # 圆度
    # print(feature_gxy, feature_residual, area, perimeter, a_p_r)

    if a_p_r > 100:
        fault_type = FaultEnum.Sticky_Line
    elif feature_residual < 0:
        fault_type = FaultEnum.Particle
    elif feature_residual >= 0:
        fault_type = FaultEnum.Bubble
    else:
        fault_type = FaultEnum.Particle

    return AnaylsisOutput(
        has_fault=has_fault,
        fault_type=fault_type
    )
    # raise Exception


================================================================================

import numpy as np


def iou(box1, box2):
    # box: [x, y, w, h]
    xi1 = max(box1[0], box2[0])
    yi1 = max(box1[1], box2[1])
    xi2 = min(box1[0] + box1[2], box2[0] + box2[2])
    yi2 = min(box1[1] + box1[3], box2[1] + box2[3])
    inter_area = max(0, xi2 - xi1) * max(0, yi2 - yi1)

    box1_area = (box1[2] - box1[0]) * (box1[3] - box1[1])
    box2_area = (box2[2] - box2[0]) * (box2[3] - box2[1])
    union_area = box1_area + box2_area - inter_area
    return inter_area / union_area


def merge_box(boxes, iou_threshold=0.5):
    while True:
        new_boxes = []
        is_break = False
        for i, box_1 in enumerate(boxes):
            for j, box_2 in enumerate(boxes[i + 1:]):
                if iou(box_1, box_2) > iou_threshold:
                    new_boxes.append(box_1)
                    is_break = True
                    break
            if is_break:
                break



def fuse_boxes(boxes, scores=None, iou_thresh=0.5):
    """
    boxes: N x 4 array
    scores: N array, optional, 用于加权融合
    """
    boxes = boxes.copy()
    scores = scores if scores is not None else np.ones(len(boxes))
    fused = []

    while len(boxes) > 0:
        # 取分数最高的 box
        idx = np.argmax(scores)
        base_box = boxes[idx]
        base_score = scores[idx]

        # 找出 IoU 大于阈值的 box
        ious = np.array([iou(base_box, b) for b in boxes])
        merge_idx = np.where(ious > iou_thresh)[0]

        # 加权平均融合
        merge_boxes = boxes[merge_idx]
        merge_scores = scores[merge_idx]
        fused_box = np.sum(merge_boxes.T * merge_scores, axis=1) / np.sum(merge_scores)
        fused.append(fused_box.tolist())

        # 删除已经融合的 box
        boxes = np.delete(boxes, merge_idx, axis=0)
        scores = np.delete(scores, merge_idx, axis=0)

    return fused


if __name__ == '__main__':
    # 示例
    boxes = np.array([[10, 10, 50, 50], [12, 12, 48, 48], [100, 100, 150, 150]])
    scores = np.array([0.9, 0.8, 0.95])
    fused = fuse_boxes(boxes, scores, iou_thresh=0.5)
    print(fused)

import numpy as np
from functools import cache


@cache
def cache_kenel(w, h) -> np.ndarray:
    return np.ones(shape=(h, w),dtype=np.uint8)


================================================================================
FILE: ./core\pipeline\fault_detection\canny.py

import cv2, numba
import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass
from .cache import cache_kenel
from . import numba_jit

@dataclass
class Output:
    gx: np.ndarray
    gy: np.ndarray
    gxy: np.ndarray
    residual: np.ndarray
    # processed
    processed_gxy: np.ndarray
    processed_residual: np.ndarray


@numba.jit(nopython=True)
def gamma_format(img, gamma=2.) -> np.ndarray:
    img = ((img.astype(np.float32) - img.min()) / (img.max()-img.min()))
    img = img ** gamma
    return (img * 255).astype(np.uint8)


@numba.jit(nopython=True)
def _jit_gxy(gx, gy):
    gxy = ( gx ** 2 +
            gy ** 2) ** 0.5
    return gxy


#@numba.jit(nopython=True)
def _jit_uniform(a):
    b = ((a - a.min()) / (a.max() - a.min()) * 255).astype(np.uint8)
    return b


@numba.jit(nopython=True)
def _jit_get_residual(gray, mean):
    residual = gray - mean
    return residual


def _jit_weighted_canny(gray, low_thresh=50, high_thresh=150) -> Output:
    # Sobel 梯度
    _gx: np.ndarray = cv2.Sobel(gray, cv2.CV_32F, 1, 0, ksize=3)
    _gy: np.ndarray = cv2.Sobel(gray, cv2.CV_32F, 0, 1, ksize=3)
    _gxy = _jit_gxy(gx=_gx, gy=_gy)
    # gx, gy = np.gradient(gray)  # .astype(np.uint8)
    # gxy = ((gxy - gxy.min()) / gxy.ptp() * 255).astype(np.uint8)

    # 腐蚀膨胀
    gxy = _jit_uniform(_gxy.astype(np.float32))
    for _ in range(0):
        # gxy = cv2.dilate(gxy, np.ones((3, 15), np.uint8), iterations=1)
        # gxy = cv2.erode(gxy, np.ones((7, 7), np.uint8), iterations=1)
        gxy = cv2.blur(gxy, (5, 5))
        gxy = gamma_format(gxy, gamma=1.1)
        # gxy = cv2.dilate(gxy, cache_kenel(w=7, h=7), iterations=1)
        # gxy = cv2.erode(gxy, cache_kenel(w=7, h=7), iterations=1)
        gxy = cv2.morphologyEx(gxy, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, cache_kenel(w=7, h=7))
        # print(np.bincount(gxy.reshape((gxy.shape[0] * gxy.shape[1]))))
        # print(gxy.max())

    # residual
    mean = np.mean(gray, axis=0)
    _residual = _jit_get_residual(gray=gray, mean=mean[np.newaxis])
    residual = _jit_uniform(numba_jit.abs(_residual))

    for _ in range(1):
        # residual = cv2.dilate(residual, np.ones((7, 7), np.uint8), iterations=1)
        # residual = cv2.erode(residual, np.ones((7, 7), np.uint8), iterations=1)
        residual = cv2.blur(residual, (5, 5))
        residual = gamma_format(residual, gamma=1.1)
        # residual = cv2.dilate(residual, cache_kenel(w=7, h=7), iterations=1)
        # residual = cv2.erode(residual, cache_kenel(w=7, h=7), iterations=1)
        # dilate -> erode = close
        residual = cv2.morphologyEx(residual, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, cache_kenel(w=7, h=7))
        # residual
        # print(np.bincount(residual.flatten()))
        # print(np.where(residual == 255,))


    # cv2.imwrite('cache/gx.jpg', gx)
    # cv2.imwrite('cache/gy.jpg', gy)
    # cv2.imwrite('cache/gxy.jpg', gxy)
    #cv2.imwrite('cache/residual.jpg', _jit_uniform_residual))

    return {
        "gx": _gx,
        "gy": _gy,
        "gxy": _gxy,
        "residual": _residual,
        "processed_gxy": gxy,
        "processed_residual": residual,
    }


def weighted_canny(img, low_thresh=50, high_thresh=150):
    # canny = cv2.Canny(gxy, low_thresh, high_thresh)
    return Output(**_jit_weighted_canny(img))

@dataclass
class DetectOutput:
    x: int or float
    y: int or float
    w: int or float
    h: int or float
    ftype: str

    def to_tuple(self):
        return (self.x,
                self.y,
                self.w,
                self.h,
                self.ftype)

    def to_dict(self):
        return {'x': self.x,
                'y': self.y,
                'w': self.w,
                'h': self.h,
                'ftype': self.ftype}


def detect(img: np.ndarray) -> List[DetectOutput]:
    # print(img.shape,img.max(),img.min())
    # rgb or gray
    if len(img.shape) == 2:
        gray = img.astype(np.uint8)
        # gray = np.tile(gray[:, :, np.newaxis], (1, 1, 3))
    elif len(img.shape) == 3:
        assert img.shape[2] == 3, 'the channel should be 3'
        img = img.astype(np.uint8)
        gray = cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)

    # height and width
    scale = 0.5
    gray = cv2.resize(gray, None, fx=scale, fy=scale, interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
    height, width = gray.shape[:2]

    # 裁剪出需要识别的区域
    #crop_output = crop_detect_area(gray)
    # fft 过滤纹理
    gray = fft_remove_texture(gray)

    # 提取特征
    canny_output = weighted_canny(gray, low_thresh=60, high_thresh=110)


    # 腐蚀膨胀
    feature = cv2.bitwise_or(canny_output.processed_residual, canny_output.processed_gxy)
    # cv2.imshow('feature', gray)

    for _ in range(10):
        feature = cv2.erode(feature, np.ones((3, 3), np.uint8), iterations=1)
        feature = cv2.dilate(feature, np.ones((3, 3), np.uint8), iterations=1)
        #     feature = cv2.erode(feature, np.ones((32, 24), np.uint8), iterations=1)

    # Step 1：去背景（保留局部差异）
    # bg = cv2.GaussianBlur(feature, (1, 1), 0)
    # feature = cv2.subtract(bg, feature)

    clahe = cv2.createCLAHE(2.0, (8, 8))

    # params = cv2.SimpleBlobDetector_Params()
    # # 设置阈值范围
    # params.minThreshold = 0

    # # 按面积筛选
    # params.filterByArea = False
    # params.minArea = 50
    # # 按圆度筛选
    # params.filterByCircularity = False
    # # 创建检测器
    # detector = cv2.SimpleBlobDetector_create(params)
    # # 检测
    # keypoints = detector.detect(feature)
    # print(keypoints)
    # 绘制
    # out = cv2.drawKeypoints(feature, keypoints, None, (0, 0, 255),
    #                         cv2.DRAW_MATCHES_FLAGS_DRAW_RICH_KEYPOINTS)

    #kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (21, 21))
    #tophat = cv2.morphologyEx(feature, cv2.MORPH_TOPHAT, kernel)
    # feature_f = feature.astype(np.float32)
    # mean = cv2.blur(feature_f, (15, 15))
    # sqmean = cv2.blur(feature_f ** 2, (15, 15))** 0.5
    # std = np.sqrt(sqmean - mean ).astype(np.uint8)


    # cv2.imshow("blob", out)
    # cv2.imshow("feature", feature)
    # cv2.imshow("sqmean", sqmean.astype(np.uint8))
    # cv2.imwrite("feature.jpg", feature)
    # cv2.waitKey(0)
    #
    # feature = std
    # feature = clahe.apply(feature)
    # th2 = cv2.adaptiveThreshold(
    #     feature,
    #     255,
    #     cv2.ADAPTIVE_THRESH_GAUSSIAN_C,
    #     cv2.THRESH_BINARY,
    #     11,
    #     5
    # )
    # feature = th2

    # 找轮廓(可能的缺陷)
    _, binary = cv2.threshold(feature, 75, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY)
    binary = cv2.dilate(binary, np.ones((3, 3), np.uint8), iterations=3)
    #binary = cv2.erode(binary, np.ones((3, 3), np.uint8), iterations=3)
    # cv2.imshow('feature', feature)
    #

    # while True:
    #     cv2.imshow('canny_output', feature)
    #     cv2.imwrite('canny_output.png', feature)
    #     cv2.waitKey(1)
    contours, _ = cv2.findContours(binary, cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL, cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)

    # print(contours)
    _contours = []
    for cnt in contours:
        #if cv2.contourArea(cnt) < 0: continue  # 过滤太小的色块
        x, y, w, h = cv2.boundingRect(cnt)
        if x == 0 or x + w == width or y == 0 or y + h == height:
            continue

        # if w < width * 0.01 or h < height * 0.01: continue
        if h > height * 0.5: continue
        _contours.append(cnt)

    # 进一步分析是否是缺陷
    outputs = []
    for cnt in _contours:
        anaylsis_output = anaylsis(cnt=cnt, img=gray,
                                   gxy=canny_output.gxy,
                                   residual=canny_output.residual,
                                   threshold=30)
        if not anaylsis_output.has_fault:
            continue
        x, y, w, h = cv2.boundingRect(cnt)
        # offset_x, offset_y, offset_w, offset_h = crop_output.offset
        # x, y = x + offset_x, y + offset_y
        outputs.append(
            DetectOutput(
                x=x / scale, y=y / scale, w=w / scale, h=h / scale, ftype=anaylsis_output.fault_type
            ))
    # 绘制

    # cv2.imwrite('cache/gray.jpg', gray)

    # cv2.imwrite('cache/binary.jpg', binary)
    # cv2.imwrite('cache/feature.jpg', feature)
    # print(time.time() - st)
    return outputs

import cv2, math
import numpy as np
from typing import Tuple
import cv2
import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass

line_color = (0, 255, 0)
box_color = (0, 0, 255)


@dataclass
class Output:
    crop: np.ndarray
    offset: Tuple[int, int, int, int,]


def distance_origin_from_line(k, x0, y0):
    """
    k: 斜率；若为 None 或 math.isinf(k) 表示竖直线
    (x0, y0): 直线上一点
    返回: 到原点的距离
    """
    if k is None or math.isinf(k):
        return abs(x0)  # x = x0
    return abs(y0 - k * x0) / math.sqrt(k ** 2 + 1)


def crop_detect_area(gray: np.ndarray):
    def cluster_by_distance(data, threshold):
        data = sorted(data)
        clusters = []
        cluster = [data[0]]

        for i in range(1, len(data)):
            if data[i] - data[i - 1] <= threshold:
                cluster.append(data[i])
            else:
                clusters.append(cluster)
                cluster = [data[i]]
        clusters.append(cluster)
        return clusters

    # 梯度图裁剪
    gx = cv2.Sobel(gray, cv2.CV_64F, 1, 0, ksize=3)
    gx = np.abs(gx)
    gx = ((gx - gx.min()) / gx.ptp() * 255).astype(np.uint8)
    _, gx = cv2.threshold(gx, 100, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY)
    img = np.tile(gray[:, :, np.newaxis], (1, 1, 3))

    # 霍夫变换
    _lines = cv2.HoughLinesP(gx, 2, np.pi / 180, 180, minLineLength=100, maxLineGap=128)

    if _lines is not None:
        # 坐标 -> 角度
        lines = []
        for line in _lines:
            x1, y1, x2, y2 = line[0]
            k = abs(y1 - y2) / abs(x1 - x2) if x1 != x2 else None
            distance = distance_origin_from_line(k=k, x0=x1, y0=y1)
            lines.append((distance, k))

        # 聚类
        kmeans = cluster_by_distance(data=[l[0] for l in lines], threshold=10)
    else:
        kmeans = []

    # 有两个以上的簇
    if len(kmeans) >= 2:
        left, right = max(kmeans[0]) + 10, min(kmeans[-1]) - 10
        left, right = int(left), int(right)
    else:
        left, right = 0, gray.shape[1]


    # cv2.imwrite('cache/crop.jpg', gx)

    if _lines is not None:
        for line in (_lines):
            x1, y1, x2, y2 = line[0]
            cv2.line(img, (x1, y1), (x2, y2), line_color, 2)
    # cv2.imwrite('cache/crop-img.jpg', img)
    return Output(
        crop=gray[:, left:right],
        offset=(left, 0, right - left, gray.shape[0]),
    )

import pyfftw
import numpy as np

from functools import cache
from . import numba_jit


@cache
def _create_mask(shape, k1, k2):
    rows, cols = shape
    crow, ccol = rows // 2, cols // 2
    mask = np.ones(shape)
    k1, k2 = 0.492, 0.49999999999999999999999999
    mask[int(rows * k1):-int(rows * k1), int(ccol * k1):-int(ccol * k1)] = 0
    mask[int(rows * k2):-int(rows * k2), int(ccol * k2):-int(ccol * k2)] = 1
    mask[crow, ccol] = 1
    return np.fft.ifftshift(mask)


@cache
def fft2_plan(shape):
    in_array = pyfftw.empty_aligned(shape, dtype=np.complex64)
    out_array = pyfftw.empty_aligned(shape, dtype=np.complex64)
    fft_object = pyfftw.FFTW(in_array, out_array, axes=(0, 1), threads=4)
    return in_array, out_array, fft_object


@cache
def ifft2_plan(shape):
    in_array = pyfftw.empty_aligned(shape, dtype=np.complex64)
    out_array = pyfftw.empty_aligned(shape, dtype=np.complex64)
    fft_object = pyfftw.FFTW(in_array, out_array, axes=(0, 1), direction='FFTW_BACKWARD', threads=4)
    return in_array, out_array, fft_object


def fft2_fftw(a, threads=1):
    """
    用 pyFFTW 实现 numpy.fft.fft2 的功能
    :param a: 输入 numpy.ndarray
    :param threads: 使用的线程数
    :return: 复数结果 ndarray
    """
    # 创建计划, 缓存
    in_array, out_array, fft_object = fft2_plan(a.shape)
    in_array[:] = a[:]
    # numba_jit.setter(a=in_array, b=a)
    fft_object()
    return out_array  # .copy()


def ifft2_fftw(a, threads=1):
    """
    用 pyFFTW 实现 numpy.fft.ifft2 的功能
    :param a: 输入复数 ndarray
    :param threads: 使用线程数
    :return: 复数结果 ndarray
    """
    # 创建计划, 缓存
    in_array, out_array, fft_object = ifft2_plan(a.shape)
    in_array[:] = a[:]
    # numba_jit.setter(a=in_array, b=a)
    fft_object()
    return out_array  # .copy()


# @numba.njit
def _jit_fft_remove_texture(img, mask):
    # f = np.fft.fft2(img)
    f = fft2_fftw(img)
    # fshift = np.fft.fftshift(f)

    # 应用 mask
    _fshift = numba_jit.multiplicate(f, mask)

    # 反变换
    # f_ishift = np.fft.ifftshift(_fshift)
    # img_back = np.fft.ifft2(_fshift)
    img_back = ifft2_fftw(_fshift)
    img_back = numba_jit.astype(numba_jit.abs(img_back), dtype=np.uint8)

    return img_back


def fft_remove_texture(img):
    # 手工制作一个mask, 低通，去除高频）
    mask = _create_mask(img.shape, k1=0.492, k2=0.49999999999999999999999999)
    return _jit_fft_remove_texture(img, mask)


================================================================================
FILE: ./core\pipeline\fault_detection\line.py
================================================================================
def line_by_two_points(p1, p2, x=None, y=None):
    """
    已知两点 p1, p2，求直线上第三点坐标
    输入:
        p1, p2 : (x, y)
        x      : 给定x (可选)
        y      : 给定y (可选)
    返回:
        (x, y) : 第三点坐标
    """
    x1, y1 = p1
    x2, y2 = p2

    if x1 == x2:  # 垂直线
        if x is not None:
            return (x1, None)  # x固定，y不唯一
        elif y is not None:
            return (x1, y)
        else:
            raise ValueError("必须提供x或y")

    k = (y2 - y1) / (x2 - x1)

    if x is not None:
        y = y1 + k * (x - x1)
        return (x, y)
    elif y is not None:
        x = x1 + (y - y1) / k
        return (x, y)
    else:
        raise ValueError("必须提供x或y")

from numba import jit
import numpy as np


@jit(nopython=True)
def multiplicate(a, b):
    return a * b


@jit(nopython=True)
def add(a, b):
    return a + b


@jit(nopython=True)
def sum(a):
    return np.sum(a)


@jit(nopython=True)
def abs(a):
    return np.abs(a)


@jit(nopython=True)
def astype(a, dtype):
    return a.astype(dtype)


@jit(nopython=True)
def setter(a, b):
    a[:] = b[:]

# coding:utf-8
import json
from copy import deepcopy
from enum import Enum
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List

import darkdetect
from PyQt5.QtCore import QObject, pyqtSignal
from PyQt5.QtGui import QColor





class Theme(Enum):
    """ Theme enumeration """

    LIGHT = "Light"
    DARK = "Dark"
    AUTO = "Auto"


class ConfigValidator:
    """ Config validator """

    def validate(self, value):
        """ Verify whether the value is legal """
        return True

    def correct(self, value):
        """ correct illegal value """
        return value


class RangeValidator(ConfigValidator):
    """ Range validator """

    def __init__(self, min, max):
        self.min = min
        self.max = max
        self.range = (min, max)

    def validate(self, value):
        return self.min <= value <= self.max

    def correct(self, value):
        return min(max(self.min, value), self.max)


class OptionsValidator(ConfigValidator):
    """ Options validator """

    def __init__(self, options):
        if not options:
            raise ValueError("The `options` can't be empty.")

        if isinstance(options, Enum):
            options = options._member_map_.values()

        self.options = list(options)

    def validate(self, value):
        return value in self.options

    def correct(self, value):
        return value if self.validate(value) else self.options[0]


class BoolValidator(OptionsValidator):
    """ Boolean validator """

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__([True, False])


class FolderValidator(ConfigValidator):
    """ Folder validator """

    def validate(self, value):
        return Path(value).exists()

    def correct(self, value):
        path = Path(value)
        path.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
        return str(path.absolute()).replace("\\", "/")


class FolderListValidator(ConfigValidator):
    """ Folder list validator """

    def validate(self, value):
        return all(Path(i).exists() for i in value)

    def correct(self, value: List[str]):
        folders = []
        for folder in value:
            path = Path(folder)
            if path.exists():
                folders.append(str(path.absolute()).replace("\\", "/"))

        return folders


class ColorValidator(ConfigValidator):
    """ RGB color validator """

    def __init__(self, default):
        self.default = QColor(default)

    def validate(self, color):
        try:
            return QColor(color).isValid()
        except:
            return False

    def correct(self, value):
        return QColor(value) if self.validate(value) else self.default


class ConfigSerializer:
    """ Config serializer """

    def serialize(self, value):
        """ serialize config value """
        return value

    def deserialize(self, value):
        """ deserialize config from config file's value """
        return value


class EnumSerializer(ConfigSerializer):
    """ enumeration class serializer """

    def __init__(self, enumClass):
        self.enumClass = enumClass

    def serialize(self, value):
        return value.value

    def deserialize(self, value):
        return self.enumClass(value)


class ColorSerializer(ConfigSerializer):
    """ QColor serializer """

    def serialize(self, value: QColor):
        return value.name(QColor.HexArgb)

    def deserialize(self, value):
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return QColor(*value)

        return QColor(value)


class ConfigItem(QObject):
    """ Config item """

    valueChanged = pyqtSignal(object)

    def __init__(self, group, name, default, validator=None, serializer=None, restart=False):
        """
        Parameters
        ----------
        group: str
            config group name

        name: str
            config item name, can be empty

        default:
            default value

        options: list
            options value

        serializer: ConfigSerializer
            config serializer

        restart: bool
            whether to restart the application after updating value
        """
        super().__init__()
        self.group = group
        self.name = name
        self.validator = validator or ConfigValidator()
        self.serializer = serializer or ConfigSerializer()
        self.__value = default
        self.value = default
        self.restart = restart
        self.defaultValue = self.validator.correct(default)

    @property
    def value(self):
        """ get the value of config item """
        return self.__value

    @value.setter
    def value(self, v):
        v = self.validator.correct(v)
        ov = self.__value
        self.__value = v
        if ov != v:
            self.valueChanged.emit(v)

    @property
    def key(self):
        """ get the config key separated by `.` """
        return self.group+"."+self.name if self.name else self.group

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.__class__.__name__}[value={self.value}]'

    def serialize(self):
        return self.serializer.serialize(self.value)

    def deserializeFrom(self, value):
        self.value = self.serializer.deserialize(value)


class RangeConfigItem(ConfigItem):
    """ Config item of range """

    @property
    def range(self):
        """ get the available range of config """
        return self.validator.range

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.__class__.__name__}[range={self.range}, value={self.value}]'


class OptionsConfigItem(ConfigItem):
    """ Config item with options """

    @property
    def options(self):
        return self.validator.options

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.__class__.__name__}[options={self.options}, value={self.value}]'


class ColorConfigItem(ConfigItem):
    """ Color config item """

    def __init__(self, group, name, default, restart=False):
        super().__init__(group, name, QColor(default), ColorValidator(default),
                         ColorSerializer(), restart)

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.__class__.__name__}[value={self.value.name()}]'


class QConfig(QObject):
    """ Config of app """

    appRestartSig = pyqtSignal()
    themeChanged = pyqtSignal(Theme)
    themeChangedFinished = pyqtSignal()
    themeColorChanged = pyqtSignal(QColor)

    themeMode = OptionsConfigItem(
        "QFluentWidgets", "ThemeMode", Theme.LIGHT, OptionsValidator(Theme), EnumSerializer(Theme))
    themeColor = ColorConfigItem("QFluentWidgets", "ThemeColor", '#009faa')
    fontFamilies = ConfigItem("QFluentWidgets", "FontFamilies", ['Segoe UI', 'Microsoft YaHei', 'PingFang SC'])

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.file = Path("config/config.json")
        self._theme = Theme.LIGHT
        self._cfg = self

    def get(self, item):
        """ get the value of config item """
        return item.value

    def set(self, item, value, save=True, copy=True):
        """ set the value of config item

        Parameters
        ----------
        item: ConfigItem
            config item

        value:
            the new value of config item

        save: bool
            whether to save the change to config file

        copy: bool
            whether to deep copy the new value
        """
        if item.value == value:
            return

        # deepcopy new value
        try:
            item.value = deepcopy(value) if copy else value
        except:
            item.value = value

        if save:
            self.save()

        if item.restart:
            self._cfg.appRestartSig.emit()

        if item is self._cfg.themeMode:
            self.theme = value
            self._cfg.themeChanged.emit(value)

        if item is self._cfg.themeColor:
            self._cfg.themeColorChanged.emit(value)

    def toDict(self, serialize=True):
        """ convert config items to `dict` """
        items = {}
        for name in dir(self._cfg.__class__):
            item = getattr(self._cfg.__class__, name)
            if not isinstance(item, ConfigItem):
                continue

            value = item.serialize() if serialize else item.value
            if not items.get(item.group):
                if not item.name:
                    items[item.group] = value
                else:
                    items[item.group] = {}

            if item.name:
                items[item.group][item.name] = value

        return items

    def save(self):
        """ save config """
        self._cfg.file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with open(self._cfg.file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(self._cfg.toDict(), f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=4)

   
    def load(self, file=None, config=None):
        """ load config

        Parameters
        ----------
        file: str or Path
            the path of json config file

        config: Config
            config object to be initialized
        """
        if isinstance(config, QConfig):
            self._cfg = config
            self._cfg.themeChanged.connect(self.themeChanged)

        if isinstance(file, (str, Path)):
            self._cfg.file = Path(file)

        try:
            with open(self._cfg.file, encoding="utf-8") as f:
                cfg = json.load(f)
        except:
            cfg = {}

        # map config items'key to item
        items = {}
        for name in dir(self._cfg.__class__):
            item = getattr(self._cfg.__class__, name)
            if isinstance(item, ConfigItem):
                items[item.key] = item

        # update the value of config item
        for k, v in cfg.items():
            if not isinstance(v, dict) and items.get(k) is not None:
                items[k].deserializeFrom(v)
            elif isinstance(v, dict):
                for key, value in v.items():
                    key = k + "." + key
                    if items.get(key) is not None:
                        items[key].deserializeFrom(value)

        self.theme = self.get(self._cfg.themeMode)

    @property
    def theme(self):
        """ get theme mode, can be `Theme.Light` or `Theme.Dark` """
        return self._cfg._theme

    @theme.setter
    def theme(self, t):
        """ chaneg the theme without modifying the config file """
        if t == Theme.AUTO:
            t = darkdetect.theme()
            t = Theme(t) if t else Theme.LIGHT

        self._cfg._theme = t


qconfig = QConfig()


def isDarkTheme():
    """ whether the theme is dark mode """
    return qconfig.theme == Theme.DARK

def theme():
    """ get theme mode """
    return qconfig.theme

def isDarkThemeMode(theme=Theme.AUTO):
    """ whether the theme is dark mode """
    return theme == Theme.DARK if theme != Theme.AUTO else isDarkTheme()

import numpy as np
from PyQt5.QtGui import QImage, QPixmap

def np_2_pixmap(arr: np.ndarray) -> QPixmap:
    """将 NumPy 数组转为 QPixmap"""
    if arr.ndim == 2:  # 灰度图
        h, w = arr.shape
        bytes_per_line = w
        qimg = QImage(arr.data, w, h, bytes_per_line, QImage.Format_Grayscale8)
    elif arr.ndim == 3:
        h, w, ch = arr.shape
        if ch == 3:  # RGB
            bytes_per_line = ch * w
            qimg = QImage(arr.data, w, h, bytes_per_line, QImage.Format_RGB888)
        elif ch == 4:  # RGBA
            bytes_per_line = ch * w
            qimg = QImage(arr.data, w, h, bytes_per_line, QImage.Format_RGBA8888)
        else:
            raise ValueError("通道数必须是 1, 3 或 4")
    else:
        raise ValueError("输入必须是 2D 灰度图或 3D 彩色图")

    # 注意：arr 必须是连续内存，否则 QImage 会显示错乱
    if not arr.flags['C_CONTIGUOUS']:
        arr = np.ascontiguousarray(arr)

    return QPixmap.fromImage(qimg)

# machine_id.py
import sys
import subprocess
import uuid
import hashlib
import platform
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric import rsa, padding
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import hashes, serialization
import base64
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
def _get_mac():
    # uuid.getnode() usually returns MAC (may be random if cannot find)
    node = uuid.getnode()
    mac = ':'.join(("%012X" % node)[i:i+2] for i in range(0, 12, 2))
    return mac

def _run_cmd(cmd):
    try:
        out = subprocess.check_output(cmd, shell=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, universal_newlines=True)
        return out.strip()
    except Exception:
        return ""

def get_windows_serials():
    # 需要在 Windows 上运行
    bios = _run_cmd('wmic bios get serialnumber').splitlines()
    mb = _run_cmd('wmic baseboard get serialnumber').splitlines()
    def pick(lines):
        for l in lines:
            s = l.strip()
            if s and not s.lower().startswith('serial'):
                return s
        return ""
    return pick(bios), pick(mb)

def get_unix_serials():
    # Linux / macOS 尝试读取一些常见位置
    cpu = _run_cmd("cat /proc/cpuinfo | grep 'Serial' | head -n 1 | awk -F': ' '{print $2}'") or \
          _run_cmd("cat /proc/cpuinfo | grep -m1 'model name' | awk -F': ' '{print $2}'")
    product_uuid = _run_cmd("cat /sys/class/dmi/id/product_uuid 2>/dev/null") or ""
    board_serial = _run_cmd("cat /sys/class/dmi/id/board_serial 2>/dev/null") or ""
    return cpu, product_uuid or board_serial

def get_machine_fingerprint(extra_salt=""):
    """
    Return a stable machine fingerprint string.
    extra_salt: 可选，绑定给定额外信息（例如软件名/版本）
    """
    p = platform.system().lower()
    parts = []
    parts.append(platform.node())
    parts.append(platform.system())
    parts.append(platform.release())
    parts.append(_get_mac())

    if p == "windows":
        bios_sn, mb_sn = get_windows_serials()
        parts.extend([bios_sn, mb_sn])
    else:
        cpu, pu = get_unix_serials()
        parts.extend([cpu, pu])

    # 组合并哈希成固定长度
    combined = "|".join([str(x) for x in parts if x])
    combined = combined + "|" + extra_salt
    h = hashlib.sha256(combined.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    # 返回十六进制指纹（也可以 base64）
    return h



def verify_license(public_pem: bytes, token: str,extra_salt=''):
    """ ---------- 客户端：验证 license ---------- """
    try:
        msg_b64, sig_b64 = token.split(".")
        # pad base64
        def pad(s): return s + "=" * (-len(s) % 4)
        message = base64.urlsafe_b64decode(pad(msg_b64))
        signature = base64.urlsafe_b64decode(pad(sig_b64))
        public_key = serialization.load_pem_public_key(public_pem)
        public_key.verify(
            signature,
            message,
            padding.PSS(mgf=padding.MGF1(hashes.SHA256()), salt_length=padding.PSS.MAX_LENGTH),
            hashes.SHA256()
        )
        payload = json.loads(message.decode('utf-8'))
        # 检查 mid 是否与当前机器匹配，并检查过期
        current_mid = get_machine_fingerprint(extra_salt=extra_salt)
        if payload.get("mid") != current_mid:
            return False, "Machine mismatch"
        exp = payload.get("exp")
        if exp and datetime.fromisoformat(exp.replace("Z", "")) < datetime.utcnow():
            return False, "Expired"
        return True, "OK"
    except Exception as e:
        return False, str(e)
# quick demo
if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("Machine fingerprint:", get_machine_fingerprint(extra_salt="MyApp_v1"))

import os.path

from PyQt5.QtGui import QIcon, QFont
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QMainWindow, QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, QStackedWidget, QFrame, QWidget, QLabel, QSizePolicy
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QSpacerItem
from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt
from PyQt5.QtSvg import QSvgWidget
from PyQt5.QtSvg import QSvgWidget

from qframelesswindow import FramelessWindow

from .companent import Panel, Icon,Label
from core.const import Path,Text
from core.pipeline import faultDetectionPipeline

from .interface import DetectionInterface, BottomInfoInterface, SettingInterface,MemoryhInterface,UserManagerInterface
import sys

class Window(FramelessWindow):
    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super(Window, self).__init__(*args, **kwargs)

        self.setWindowIcon(QIcon(os.path.join(Path.ICON, 'logo.svg')))
        self.setWindowTitle(self.tr(Text.APP_NAME))
        self.setMinimumSize(1200, 800)

        self.initWidget()
        self.initLayout()
        self.initData()

    def initWidget(self):
        self.iconLabel = QSvgWidget(self)
        self.iconLabel.load(os.path.join(Path.ICON,'logo.svg'))
        self.iconLabel.setFixedSize(24, 24)
        self.titleLabel = Label(parent=self,text=self.tr(Text.APP_NAME),fontSize=16,weight=QFont.Normal)

        self.panel = Panel(parent=self)
        self.detectionInterface = DetectionInterface(self)
        self.bottoImnfoInterface = BottomInfoInterface(self)
        self.settingInterface = SettingInterface(self)
        self.memoryhInterface = MemoryhInterface(self)
        self.userManagerInterface = UserManagerInterface(self)

        # stackedWidget
        self.stackedWidget = QStackedWidget(self)

    def initData(self):
        self.panel.addButton(text=self.tr('检测'), icon=Icon.DETECTION,
                             onClicked=lambda: self.stackedWidget.setCurrentWidget(self.detectionInterface))
        self.panel.addButton(text=self.tr('设置'), icon=Icon.SETTING,
                             onClicked=lambda: self.stackedWidget.setCurrentWidget(self.settingInterface)                             )
        self.panel.addButton(text=self.tr('记录'), icon=Icon.HISTORY,
                             onClicked=lambda: self.stackedWidget.setCurrentWidget(self.memoryhInterface))
        self.panel.addButton(text=self.tr('数据库'), icon=Icon.DARABASE,
                             onClicked=lambda: self.stackedWidget.setCurrentWidget(self.userManagerInterface))

    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(5, 5, 5, 5)
        self.titleLayout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.contentLayout = QHBoxLayout()

        self.titleLayout.addWidget(self.iconLabel)
        self.titleLayout.addWidget(self.titleLabel)
        self.titleLayout.addSpacerItem(QSpacerItem(0,0,QSizePolicy.Expanding,QSizePolicy.Minimum))

        self.stackedWidget.addWidget(self.detectionInterface)
        self.stackedWidget.addWidget(self.settingInterface)
        self.stackedWidget.addWidget(self.memoryhInterface)
        self.stackedWidget.addWidget(self.userManagerInterface)
        self.stackedWidget.setCurrentWidget(self.detectionInterface)
        self.vlayout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.vlayout.addWidget(self.stackedWidget)
        self.vlayout.addWidget(self.bottoImnfoInterface)

        self.contentLayout.addWidget(self.panel)
        self.contentLayout.addLayout(self.vlayout)
        self.contentLayout.setStretch(0, 0)
        self.contentLayout.setStretch(1, 1)

        self.viewLayout.addLayout(self.titleLayout)
        self.viewLayout.addLayout(self.contentLayout)

        # 提升到最顶层
        self.titleBar.raise_()

    def close(self):
        super(Window,self).close()
        if faultDetectionPipeline.isDetectEnabled():
            faultDetectionPipeline.setDetectEnable(False)
        if faultDetectionPipeline.isCameraEnabled():
            faultDetectionPipeline.setCameraEnabled(False)
        if faultDetectionPipeline.isVSamplerEnabled():
            faultDetectionPipeline.setVSamplerEnabled(False)
        if faultDetectionPipeline.isLightningEnabled():
            faultDetectionPipeline.setLightningEnabled(False)
        if faultDetectionPipeline.isLightSourceEnabled():
            faultDetectionPipeline.setLightSourceEnable(False)
        sys.exit(0)

from core.pipeline import faultDetectionPipeline
from core.signal_bus import SignalBus


class Function():
    @staticmethod
    def swapBatch(batchName: str, wokerName: str = ''):
        """
        更换批次
        param> batchName:批次名
        """
        faultDetectionPipeline.swapBatch(batchName, wokerName)
        SignalBus.batchUpdated.emit(batchName, wokerName)

    @staticmethod
    def onFaultUpdated():
        """ 缺陷更新 """
        SignalBus.faultUpdated.emit()


faultDetectionPipeline.setCallback(callback=Function.onFaultUpdated)
