from typing import Union

from PyQt5.QtCore import QRectF, QSize
from PyQt5.QtGui import QIcon, QPainter
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QPushButton, QApplication, QToolButton

from .font import setFont
from .utils import loadQss
from .icon import drawIcon,Icon

class PushButton(QPushButton):

    def __init__(self, parent: QWidget = None, text: str = ""):
        super().__init__(text, parent)
        loadQss(w=self, path=r'_rc/qss/button.qss')
        self.isPressed = False
        self.isHover = False
        self.setIconSize(QSize(16, 16))
        self.setIcon(None)
        setFont(self)
        self._postInit()

    def _postInit(self):
        pass

    def setIcon(self, icon: Union[QIcon, str]):
        if icon is None or (isinstance(icon, QIcon) and icon.isNull()):
            self.setProperty('hasIcon', False)
        else:
            self.setProperty('hasIcon', True)

        self.setStyle(QApplication.style())
        self._icon = icon or QIcon()
        self.update()

    # def icon(self):
    #     return toQIcon(self._icon)

    def setProperty(self, name: str, value) -> bool:
        if name != 'icon':
            return super().setProperty(name, value)

        self.setIcon(value)
        return True

    def mousePressEvent(self, e):
        self.isPressed = True
        super().mousePressEvent(e)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, e):
        self.isPressed = False
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(e)

    def enterEvent(self, e):
        self.isHover = True
        self.update()

    def leaveEvent(self, e):
        self.isHover = False
        self.update()

    def _drawIcon(self, icon, painter, rect, state=QIcon.Off):
        """ draw icon """
        drawIcon(icon, painter, rect, state)

    def paintEvent(self, e):
        super().paintEvent(e)
        if self.icon().isNull():
            return

        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHints(QPainter.Antialiasing |
                               QPainter.SmoothPixmapTransform)

        if not self.isEnabled():
            painter.setOpacity(0.3628)
        elif self.isPressed:
            painter.setOpacity(0.786)

        w, h = self.iconSize().width(), self.iconSize().height()
        y = (self.height() - h) / 2
        mw = self.minimumSizeHint().width()
        if mw > 0:
            x = 12 + (self.width() - mw) // 2
        else:
            x = 12

        if self.isRightToLeft():
            x = self.width() - w - x

        self._drawIcon(self._icon, painter, QRectF(x, y, w, h))


class ToolButton(QToolButton):
    """ Tool button

    Constructors
    ------------
    * ToolButton(`parent`: QWidget = None)
    * ToolButton(`icon`: QIcon | str | FluentIconBase, `parent`: QWidget = None)
    """

    def __init__(self, parent: QWidget = None, icon: Icon = None, text: str = ""):
        super().__init__(parent)
        loadQss(w=self, path=r'_rc/qss/button.qss')
        self.isPressed = False
        self.isHover = False
        self.setIconSize(QSize(16, 16))
        self.setIcon(icon)
        setFont(self)
        self._postInit()

    def _postInit(self):
        pass

    def setIcon(self, icon: Union[QIcon, str]):
        self._icon = icon
        self.update()

    def setProperty(self, name: str, value) -> bool:
        if name != 'icon':
            return super().setProperty(name, value)

        self.setIcon(value)
        return True

    def mousePressEvent(self, e):
        self.isPressed = True
        super().mousePressEvent(e)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, e):
        self.isPressed = False
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(e)

    def enterEvent(self, e):
        self.isHover = True
        self.update()

    def leaveEvent(self, e):
        self.isHover = False
        self.update()

    def _drawIcon(self, icon, painter: QPainter, rect: QRectF, state=QIcon.Off):
        """ draw icon """
        drawIcon(icon, painter, rect, state)

    def paintEvent(self, e):
        super().paintEvent(e)
        if self._icon is None:
            return

        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHints(QPainter.Antialiasing |
                               QPainter.SmoothPixmapTransform)

        if not self.isEnabled():
            painter.setOpacity(0.43)
        elif self.isPressed:
            painter.setOpacity(0.63)

        w, h = self.iconSize().width(), self.iconSize().height()
        y = (self.height() - h) / 2
        x = (self.width() - w) / 2
        self._drawIcon(self._icon, painter, QRectF(x, y, w, h))

from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QComboBox
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QComboBox
from typing import Iterator,Iterable
import serial
from .utils import loadQss
from .font import setFont
from core.database import UserManager
class ComboBox(QComboBox):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(ComboBox, self).__init__(parent)
        loadQss(w=self, path=r'_rc/qss/combox.qss')
        setFont(self)
        self._items = []

    def _adjust(self):
        text = self.currentText()
        items = self._items.copy()
        if text in items:
            idx = items.index(text)
            for i in range(idx):
                self.removeItem(0)
            for i in range(len(items) - idx - 1):
                self.removeItem(1)
            self.insertItems(1, items[idx + 1:])
            self.insertItems(0, items[:idx])
        else:
            self.clear()
            self.addItems(items)

    def setItems(self, items: Iterable[str]):
        self._items = [i for i in items]
        self._adjust()
        self.update()

    def showPopup(self):
        """ 动态加载Items """
        self._adjust()
        super(ComboBox, self).showPopup()  # 弹出选项框


class SerialPortComboBox(ComboBox):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(SerialPortComboBox, self).__init__(parent)
        loadQss(w=self, path=r'_rc/qss/combox.qss')

    def showPopup(self):
        """ 动态加载Items """
        # 获取接入的所有串口信息，插入combobox的选项中
        posts = [port.device for port in serial.tools.list_ports.comports()]
        self.setItems(['None'] + posts)
        super(SerialPortComboBox, self).showPopup()  # 弹出选项框


class UsersComboBox(ComboBox):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(UsersComboBox, self).__init__(parent)
        loadQss(w=self, path=r'_rc/qss/combox.qss')
        self.manager = UserManager()
        self.setItems(self.manager.getAllUsernames())

    def showPopup(self):
        """ 动态加载Items """
        # 获取接入的所有串口信息，插入combobox的选项中
        self.setItems(self.manager.getAllUsernames())
        super(UsersComboBox, self).showPopup()  # 弹出选项框

class FaultLine(QWidget):
    """显示一根漆包线其中一个面上的缺陷所在点（优化版）"""

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(FaultLine, self).__init__(parent)
        self.fualt_point: Set[float] = set()
        self._scope = (0, 1)

        # ====== 优化缓存 ======
        self._points_px = []  # 归一化后的点（0~1）
        self._cache_dirty = True
        self._pixmap_cache = None

        # 控制最大绘制点数（防止卡顿）
        self._max_points = 2000

        self.clear()

    def setScope(self, minimum, maximum):
        """设置范围"""
        self._scope = (minimum, maximum)
        self._cache_dirty = True
        self.update()

    def addPoint(self, point: float):
        """添加一个缺陷点"""
        self.fualt_point.add(point)
        self._cache_dirty = True

    def clear(self):
        self.fualt_point.clear()
        self._scope = (0, 1)
        self._points_px = []
        self._cache_dirty = True
        self.update()

    # =========================
    # 关键优化：预计算
    # =========================
    def _updateCache(self):
        if not self._cache_dirty:
            return

        _min, _max = self._scope
        _scope = _max - _min if _max != _min else 1.0

        # 归一化
        points = [(p - _min) / _scope for p in self.fualt_point]

        # 过滤非法值
        points = [p for p in points if 0 <= p <= 1]

        # ===== 降采样（关键优化）=====
        if len(points) > self._max_points:
            step = len(points) // self._max_points
            points = points[::step]

        self._points_px = points
        self._cache_dirty = False

    # =========================
    # 可选：Pixmap缓存（极大提升）
    # =========================
    def _updatePixmapCache(self):
        if self._pixmap_cache is not None and not self._cache_dirty:
            return

        self._updateCache()

        w, h = self.width(), self.height()
        if w <= 0 or h <= 0:
            return

        pixmap = QPixmap(w, h)
        pixmap.fill(Qt.transparent)

        painter = QPainter(pixmap)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)

        # 画线
        g = h // 8
        pen = QPen(Qt.gray)
        pen.setWidth(g)
        pen.setCapStyle(Qt.RoundCap)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.drawLine(g, g * 4, w - g, g * 4)

        # 画点（批量）

        if self._points_px:
            # QPen(brush: Union[QBrush, QColor, Qt.GlobalColor, QGradient],
            # width: float,
            # style: Qt.PenStyle = Qt.SolidLine,
            # cap: Qt.PenCapStyle = Qt.SquareCap,
            # join: Qt.PenJoinStyle = Qt.BevelJoin
            painter.setPen(QPen(QColor(255, 0, 0), 2, Qt.SolidLine, Qt.RoundCap))
            y = h / 4
            w = w - g * 2
            lines = [QLineF(g + px * w, y,
                            g + px * w, y * 3) for px in self._points_px]

            painter.drawLines(lines)

        painter.end()
        self._pixmap_cache = pixmap

    def resizeEvent(self, event):
        super(FaultLine, self).resizeEvent(event)
        self._cache_dirty = True

    # =========================
    # 绘制
    # =========================
    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)

        # 使用缓存
        self._updatePixmapCache()

        if self._pixmap_cache:
            painter.drawPixmap(0, 0, self._pixmap_cache)


class FaultOverview(EdgegWidget):
    """ 显示一根漆包线上缺陷的所在点 """

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(FaultOverview, self).__init__(parent)
        self.left_line = FaultLine(self)
        self.top_line = FaultLine(self)
        self.right_line = FaultLine(self)
        self.bottom_line = FaultLine(self)
        self.lightn_line = FaultLine(self)
        self.minimumLabel = Label(parent=self, fontSize=12)
        self.maximumLabel = Label(parent=self, fontSize=12)

        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.hlayout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.gridLayout = QGridLayout()
        add_a_line = lambda i, w, text: (self.gridLayout.addWidget(w, i, 1),
                                         self.gridLayout.addWidget(Label(text=text), i, 0))

        add_a_line(i=0, w=self.left_line, text='左')
        add_a_line(i=1, w=self.top_line, text='上')
        add_a_line(i=2, w=self.right_line, text='右')
        add_a_line(i=3, w=self.bottom_line, text='下')
        add_a_line(i=4, w=self.lightn_line, text='放电')
        self.gridLayout.setColumnStretch(1, 1)
        self.hlayout.addWidget(self.minimumLabel)
        self.hlayout.addSpacerItem(QSpacerItem(0, 0, QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Minimum))
        self.hlayout.addWidget(self.maximumLabel)
        self.viewLayout.addLayout(self.gridLayout)
        self.gridLayout.addLayout(self.hlayout, 5, 1)

    def setScope(self, minimum, maximum):
        if minimum == maximum:
            maximum = minimum + 1
        self.minimumLabel.setText(f"{minimum:.2f}")
        self.maximumLabel.setText(f"{maximum:.2f}")
        self.left_line.setScope(minimum, maximum)
        self.top_line.setScope(minimum, maximum)
        self.right_line.setScope(minimum, maximum)
        self.bottom_line.setScope(minimum, maximum)
        self.lightn_line.setScope(minimum, maximum)
        self.left_line.update()
        self.top_line.update()
        self.right_line.update()
        self.bottom_line.update()
        self.lightn_line.update()

    def clear(self):
        self.left_line.clear()
        self.top_line.clear()
        self.right_line.clear()
        self.bottom_line.clear()
        self.lightn_line.clear()

    def scope(self):
        return self.left_line._scope

from PyQt5.QtCore import pyqtSignal
from PyQt5.QtGui import QFontMetrics
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QTextEdit, QHBoxLayout
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QPushButton, QFileDialog


class FileLoadBox(QWidget):
    pathChanged = pyqtSignal(str)

    def __init__(self, parent, filter='All Files (*)', title='选择文件'):
        super(FileLoadBox, self).__init__(parent)
        self._filter = filter
        self._title = title
        self.textView = QTextEdit(self)
        self.textView.setAcceptRichText(False)
        self.textView.setEnabled(False)
        self.btn = QPushButton(self)
        self.btn.setText(self.tr("修改路径"))

        self.viewLayout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.textView)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.btn)

        self.btn.clicked.connect(self._onClicked)
        self.textView.textChanged.connect(lambda :self.pathChanged.emit(self.textView.toPlainText()))
        fm = QFontMetrics(self.textView.font())
        self.textView.setFixedHeight(fm.height() + fm.descent() + fm.ascent())

    def setPath(self, path):
        self.textView.setText(path)

    def _onClicked(self):
        file_path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self.window(),
            self._title,
            "",
            self._filter
        )

        if not file_path:
            return
        self.textView.setText(file_path)

    def path(self):
        return self.textView.toPlainText()

from PyQt5.QtGui import QFont
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget
def setFont(widget: QWidget, fontSize=14, weight=QFont.Normal):
    """ set the font of widget

    Parameters
    ----------
    widget: QWidget
        the widget to set font

    fontSize: int
        font pixel size

    weight: `QFont.Weight`
        font weight
    """
    widget.setFont(getFont(fontSize, weight))


def getFont(fontSize=14, weight=QFont.Normal):
    """ create font

    Parameters
    ----------
    fontSize: int
        font pixel size

    weight: `QFont.Weight`
        font weight
    """
    font = QFont()
    font.setFamilies(['Segoe UI', 'Microsoft YaHei', 'PingFang SC'])
    font.setPixelSize(fontSize)
    font.setWeight(weight)
    return font





================================================================================
FILE: ./view\companent\icon.py
================================================================================
import os
from enum import Enum
from typing import Union

from PyQt5.QtCore import QRectF, QFile, Qt
from PyQt5.QtGui import QIcon
from PyQt5.QtSvg import QSvgRenderer
from PyQt5.QtXml import QDomDocument

from core.const import Path
from functools import cache

class Icon(Enum):
    SETTING = 'setting.svg'
    DETECTION = 'detection.svg'
    HISTORY = 'history.svg'
    DARABASE = 'database.svg'

    def path(self):
        return os.path.join(Path.ICON, self.value)

    def toQIcon(self, path: Union[str, None] = None) -> QIcon:
        path = path or self.path()
        return QIcon(path)

    def render(self, painter, rect, indexes=None, **attributes):
        """ draw svg icon """
        icon = self.path()
        if icon.endswith('.svg'):
            if attributes:
                icon = writeSvg(self.value, indexes, **attributes).encode()
            drawSvgIcon(icon, painter, rect)
        else:
            icon = QIcon(self.toQIcon())
            rect = QRectF(rect).toRect()
            painter.drawPixmap(rect, icon.pixmap(QRectF(rect).toRect().size()))
@cache
def loadSvg(path):
    f = QFile(path)
    f.open(QFile.ReadOnly)
    return f

def writeSvg(iconPath: str, indexes=None, **attributes):
    """ write svg with specified attributes """
    if not iconPath.lower().endswith('.svg'):
        return ""

    f = loadSvg(path=iconPath)

    dom = QDomDocument()
    dom.setContent(f.readAll())

    f.close()

    # change the color of each path
    pathNodes = dom.elementsByTagName('path')
    indexes = range(pathNodes.length()) if not indexes else indexes
    for i in indexes:
        element = pathNodes.at(i).toElement()

        for k, v in attributes.items():
            element.setAttribute(k, v)

    return dom.toString()


def drawSvgIcon(icon, painter, rect):
    """ draw svg icon """
    renderer = QSvgRenderer(icon)
    renderer.render(painter, QRectF(rect))


def drawIcon(icon, painter, rect, state=QIcon.Off, **attributes):
    """ draw icon

    Parameters
    ----------
    icon: str | QIcon | FluentIconBaseBase
        the icon to be drawn

    painter: QPainter
        painter

    rect: QRect | QRectF
        the rect to render icon

    **attribute:
        the attribute of svg icon
    """
    if isinstance(icon, Icon):
        icon.render(painter, rect, **attributes)
    else:
        icon = QIcon(icon)
        icon.paint(painter, QRectF(rect).toRect(), Qt.AlignCenter, state=state)

# coding:utf-8
from PyQt5.QtCore import QPoint, Qt, QEvent, QPropertyAnimation, QEasingCurve
from PyQt5.QtGui import QColor, QResizeEvent
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import (QDialog, QGraphicsDropShadowEffect,
                             QGraphicsOpacityEffect, QHBoxLayout, QWidget, QFrame)
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QVBoxLayout


class MaskDialogBase(QDialog):
    """ Dialog box base class with a mask """

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent=parent)
        self._isClosableOnMaskClicked = False
        self._isDraggable = False
        self._dragPos = QPoint()
        self._hBoxLayout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        self.windowMask = QWidget(self)

        # dialog box in the center of mask, all widgets take it as parent
        self.widget = QFrame(self, objectName='centerWidget')
        self.setWindowFlags(Qt.FramelessWindowHint)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WA_TranslucentBackground)
        self.setGeometry(0, 0, parent.width(), parent.height())

        c = 225
        self.windowMask.resize(self.size())
        self.windowMask.setStyleSheet(f'background:rgba({c}, {c}, {c}, 0.6)')
        self._hBoxLayout.addWidget(self.widget)
        self.setShadowEffect()

        self.window().installEventFilter(self)
        self.windowMask.installEventFilter(self)
        self.widget.installEventFilter(self)

    def setShadowEffect(self, blurRadius=60, offset=(0, 10), color=QColor(0, 0, 0, 100)):
        """ add shadow to dialog """
        shadowEffect = QGraphicsDropShadowEffect(self.widget)
        shadowEffect.setBlurRadius(blurRadius)
        shadowEffect.setOffset(*offset)
        shadowEffect.setColor(color)
        self.widget.setGraphicsEffect(None)
        self.widget.setGraphicsEffect(shadowEffect)

    def setMaskColor(self, color: QColor):
        """ set the color of mask """
        self.windowMask.setStyleSheet(f"""
            background: rgba({color.red()}, {color.green()}, {color.blue()}, {color.alpha()})
        """)

    def showEvent(self, e):
        """ fade in """
        opacityEffect = QGraphicsOpacityEffect(self)
        self.setGraphicsEffect(opacityEffect)
        opacityAni = QPropertyAnimation(opacityEffect, b'opacity', self)
        opacityAni.setStartValue(0)
        opacityAni.setEndValue(1)
        opacityAni.setDuration(200)
        opacityAni.setEasingCurve(QEasingCurve.InSine)
        opacityAni.finished.connect(lambda: self.setGraphicsEffect(None))
        opacityAni.start()
        super().showEvent(e)

    def done(self, code):
        """ fade out """
        self.widget.setGraphicsEffect(None)
        opacityEffect = QGraphicsOpacityEffect(self)
        self.setGraphicsEffect(opacityEffect)
        opacityAni = QPropertyAnimation(opacityEffect, b'opacity', self)
        opacityAni.setStartValue(1)
        opacityAni.setEndValue(0)
        opacityAni.setDuration(100)
        opacityAni.finished.connect(lambda: self._onDone(code))
        opacityAni.start()

    def _onDone(self, code):
        self.setGraphicsEffect(None)
        QDialog.done(self, code)

    def isClosableOnMaskClicked(self):
        return self._isClosableOnMaskClicked

    def setClosableOnMaskClicked(self, isClosable: bool):
        self._isClosableOnMaskClicked = isClosable

    def setDraggable(self, draggable: bool):
        self._isDraggable = draggable

    def isDraggable(self) -> bool:
        return self._isDraggable

    def resizeEvent(self, e):
        self.windowMask.resize(self.size())

    def eventFilter(self, obj, e: QEvent):
        if obj is self.window():
            if e.type() == QEvent.Resize:
                re = QResizeEvent(e)
                self.resize(re.size())
        elif obj is self.windowMask:
            if e.type() == QEvent.MouseButtonRelease and e.button() == Qt.LeftButton \
                    and self.isClosableOnMaskClicked():
                self.reject()
        elif obj is self.widget and self.isDraggable():
            if e.type() == QEvent.MouseButtonPress and e.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
                if not self.widget.childrenRegion().contains(e.pos()):
                    self._dragPos = e.pos()
                    return True
            elif e.type() == QEvent.MouseMove and not self._dragPos.isNull():
                pos = self.widget.pos() + e.pos() - self._dragPos
                pos.setX(max(0, min(pos.x(), self.width() - self.widget.width())))
                pos.setY(max(0, min(pos.y(), self.height() - self.widget.height())))

                self.widget.move(pos)
                return True
            elif e.type() == QEvent.MouseButtonRelease:
                self._dragPos = QPoint()

        return super().eventFilter(obj, e)


class MaskBox(MaskDialogBase):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        self._w = None
        super(MaskBox, self).__init__(parent)

        self.viewlayout = QVBoxLayout(self.widget)

    def setWidget(self, widget):
        self.viewlayout.addWidget(widget)
        self._w = widget
        self._w.installEventFilter(self)

    def eventFilter(self, obj, event: QEvent):
        # 包容的控件关闭,dialog也关闭
        if event.type() == event.Hide and obj == self._w:
            self._w.show()
            self.hide()
        elif event.type() == event.Close and obj == self._w:
            self.close()
        return super(MaskBox, self).eventFilter(obj, event)

from typing import Union

from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, pyqtSignal, QPoint
from PyQt5.QtGui import QPaintEvent, QPainter, QPen
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QSpacerItem
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QSizePolicy

from .button import ToolButton
from .icon import Icon
from .label import Label
from ..common import Theme


class PanelButton(QWidget):
    """导航栏"""

    clicked = pyqtSignal()

    def __init__(self, text: str, icon: Union[str, Icon], parent: QWidget = None):
        super(PanelButton, self).__init__(parent=parent)

        self._btn = ToolButton(self)
        self._btn.setIcon(icon)
        self._btn.setFixedSize(48, 48)
        self._label = Label(text=text, fontSize=16, parent=self)

        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self._btn, alignment=Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self._label, alignment=Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.viewLayout.setSpacing(5)

        self._btn.clicked.connect(self.clicked.emit)


class Panel(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent):
        self._now_btn: PanelButton = None
        super(Panel, self).__init__(parent)
        self.btns = []

        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(10, 0, 0, 0)
        self.viewLayout.addSpacerItem(QSpacerItem(0, 0, QSizePolicy.Minimum, QSizePolicy.Expanding))
        self.viewLayout.setSpacing(18)

        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.MinimumExpanding, QSizePolicy.Expanding)
        # self.setFixedSize(164, 1164)

    def addButton(self, text: str, icon: Union[str, Icon], onClicked=None):
        btn = PanelButton(text=text, icon=icon)
        # btn.show()
        self.btns.append(btn)
        self.viewLayout.insertWidget(self.viewLayout.count() - 1, btn)
        if onClicked is not None:
            btn.clicked.connect(onClicked)
        btn.clicked.connect(self._onBtnClicked)
        return btn

    def _onBtnClicked(self):
        btn: PanelButton = self.sender()
        self._now_btn = btn
        self.window().update()

    def paintEvent(self, e: QPaintEvent) -> None:
        if self._now_btn is not None:
            geometry = self._now_btn.geometry()
            painter = QPainter(self)
            pen = QPen(QPen(Theme.GrayColor, 2))
            pen.setCapStyle(Qt.RoundCap)
            painter.setPen(pen)
            painter.drawLine(geometry.topLeft() + QPoint(-3, 0), geometry.bottomLeft() + QPoint(-3, 0))
        super(Panel, self).paintEvent(e)

FILE: ./view\companent\pipline_pixmap_label.py

import time, cv2, os, json
import numpy as np
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QLabel, QFontDialog
from PyQt5.QtGui import QPaintEvent, QPixmap, QPainter, QColor, QFontMetrics, QImage
from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, QRectF, QPointF, pyqtSignal, QPoint
from core import faultDetectionPipeline, ChannelInfo
from core.pipeline import FaultInfo
from core import utils
from .font import getFont
from functools import lru_cache
from PyQt5.QtGui import QColor


@lru_cache(maxsize=8)
def _build_lut(colormap: str):
    table = []

    for i in range(256):
        x = i / 255.0

        if colormap == "jds":
            r = 200 * x + 40
            g = 160 * x + 30
            b = 100 * x + 20

        elif colormap == "jet":
            r = min(max(1.5 - abs(4 * x - 3), 0), 1) * 255
            g = min(max(1.5 - abs(4 * x - 2), 0), 1) * 255
            b = min(max(1.5 - abs(4 * x - 1), 0), 1) * 255

        elif colormap == "hot":
            r = min(1, 3 * x) * 255
            g = min(1, max(0, 3 * x - 1)) * 255
            b = min(1, max(0, 3 * x - 2)) * 255

        elif colormap == "cool":
            r = x * 255
            g = (1 - x) * 255
            b = 255

        else:
            r = g = b = i

        table.append(QColor(
            int(min(255, r)),
            int(min(255, g)),
            int(min(255, b))
        ).rgb())

    return table


class PipelinePixmapLabelBase(QLabel):
    """ Base: 管线图像处理部分的显示Label """
    clicked = pyqtSignal(QPoint)

    @staticmethod
    def apply_colormap_to_pixmap(pixmap: QPixmap, colormap: str = "jds") -> QPixmap:
        img = pixmap.toImage()

        if img.format() != QImage.Format_Grayscale8:
            img = img.convertToFormat(QImage.Format_Grayscale8)

        img = img.convertToFormat(QImage.Format_Indexed8)

        img.setColorTable(_build_lut(colormap))

        return QPixmap.fromImage(img)

    def __init__(self, ctx: str, parent=None):
        self._ctx = ctx
        super(PipelinePixmapLabelBase, self).__init__(parent)
        assert isinstance(ctx, str), 'It must be a string'

    def paintEvent(self, event: QPaintEvent):
        super(PipelinePixmapLabelBase, self).paintEvent(event)

        # get image
        info = faultDetectionPipeline.channels(ctx=self._ctx)
        is_success = self._info_process(info=info)

        if not is_success:
            painter = QPainter(self)
            painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)
            painter.setBrush(Qt.red)
            painter.setPen(Qt.white)
            painter.drawRect(self.rect())
            font = getFont(24, True)
            painter.setFont(font)
            mf = QFontMetrics(font)
            text = self.tr('无信号:') + self._ctx
            rect = mf.boundingRect(text)
            res = self.size() - rect.size()
            painter.drawText(res.width() // 2, res.height() // 2 + mf.ascent() + mf.descent(), text)
        self.update()

    def ctx(self):
        return self._ctx

    def mousePressEvent(self, e):
        super(PipelinePixmapLabelBase, self).mousePressEvent(e)
        self.clicked.emit(e.pos())

    def _info_process(self, info: ChannelInfo) -> QPixmap:
        raise NotImplementedError()


class PipelineCameraLabel(PipelinePixmapLabelBase):
    """ Base: 管线中相机部分的原始图像 """

    def _info_process(self, info: ChannelInfo) -> QPixmap:
        if info is None or info.camera is None:
            return False
        # 可能还没有采集到图像
        np_img = info.camera.read()
        if np_img is None: return False
        image: QPixmap = utils.np_2_pixmap(arr=info.camera.read())
        # resize
        if image.size() != self.size():
            image: QPixmap = image.scaled(self.size(), Qt.KeepAspectRatio)

        # Draw
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)
        res = self.size() - image.size()
        painter.drawPixmap(res.width() // 2, res.height() // 2, image)
        return True


class PipelineResultLabel(PipelinePixmapLabelBase):
    """ Base: 管线中经过处理的结果图像 """

    def __init__(self, ctx: str, parent=None):
        super(PipelineResultLabel, self).__init__(ctx=ctx, parent=parent)
        self._font = getFont(12)
        self._fm = QFontMetrics(self._font)

    def _info_process(self, info: ChannelInfo) -> QPixmap:
        if info is None or \
                info.detect_image is None or \
                info.faults is None:
            return None

        # color_img = cv2.applyColorMap(info.oringe_image, cv2.COLORMAP_JET)
        image: QPixmap = utils.np_2_pixmap(arr=info.oringe_image)
        image = self.apply_colormap_to_pixmap(pixmap=image)
        # resize
        if image.size() != self.size():
            resized_image: QPixmap = image.scaled(self.size(), Qt.KeepAspectRatio)
        else:
            resized_image = image

        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)
        res = self.size() - resized_image.size()
        raito = resized_image.width() / image.width()
        offset = QPointF(res.width() // 2, res.height() // 2, )
        # draw
        painter.drawPixmap(offset, resized_image)
        painter.setPen(Qt.red)
        for fault in info.faults:
            painter.drawRect(QRectF(offset.x() + fault.x * raito, offset.y() + fault.y * raito,
                                    fault.w * raito, fault.h * raito))
            painter.drawText(QPointF(offset.x() + fault.x * raito,
                                     offset.y() + fault.y * raito - self._fm.descent() * 1.2),
                             fault.ftype)
        return True


class ResultLabel(QLabel):
    """ 图像识别结果 """

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(ResultLabel, self).__init__(parent)
        self._pixmap = None
        self._font = getFont(12)
        self._fm = QFontMetrics(self._font)

    def setResult(self, path):
        # 可能不存在, 不存在则跳过
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            self._pixmap = None
        else:
            self._pixmap = QPixmap(os.path.join(path, 'image.jpg'))
            self._pixmap = PipelinePixmapLabelBase.apply_colormap_to_pixmap(pixmap=self._pixmap)
            print(123123)
            with open(os.path.join(path, 'fault.json'), 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                faults = json.load(f)
                self._faults = FaultInfo.from_dict(**faults)
        self.update()

    def paintEvent(self, event: QPaintEvent):
        super(ResultLabel, self).paintEvent(event)
        if self._pixmap is None:
            return

        if self._pixmap.size() != self.size():
            resized_image: QPixmap = self._pixmap.scaled(self.size(), Qt.KeepAspectRatio)
        else:
            resized_image = self._pixmap

        # draw to gui
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)
        res = self.size() - resized_image.size()
        raito = resized_image.width() / self._pixmap.width()
        offset = QPointF(res.width() // 2, res.height() // 2, )
        # draw
        painter.drawPixmap(offset, resized_image)
        painter.setPen(Qt.red)
        for fault in self._faults.faults:
            painter.drawRect(QRectF(offset.x() + fault.x * raito, offset.y() + fault.y * raito,
                                    fault.w * raito, fault.h * raito))
            painter.drawText(QPointF(offset.x() + fault.x * raito,
                                     offset.y() + fault.y * raito - self._fm.descent() * 1.2),
                             fault.ftype)

    def _info_process(self, info: ChannelInfo) -> QPixmap:
        raise NotImplementedError()

================================================================================
FILE: ./view\companent\rangle_slider.py
================================================================================
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QApplication
from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, pyqtSignal
from PyQt5.QtGui import QPainter, QBrush, QColor
import sys


class RangeSlider(QWidget):
    """ 范围滑块 """
    rangeChanged = pyqtSignal(int, int)  # 发射 (lower, upper)
    _barHeight = 6
    _handleRadius = 8
    def __init__(self, minimum=0, maximum=100, parent=None):

        super().__init__(parent)
        #self.setMinimumWidth(200)
        self.setFixedHeight(self._handleRadius*2)

        self._minimum = minimum
        self._maximum = maximum
        self._lowerValue = minimum
        self._upperValue = maximum



        self._dragging = None  # 'lower' or 'upper'

    def setRange(self, min_val, max_val):
        self._minimum, self._maximum = min_val, max_val
        self._lowerValue, self._upperValue = min_val, max_val
        self.update()

    def lowerValue(self):
        return self._lowerValue

    def upperValue(self):
        return self._upperValue

    def setLowerValue(self, val):
        self._lowerValue = max(self._minimum, min(val, self._upperValue))
        self.update()
        self.rangeChanged.emit(self._lowerValue, self._upperValue)

    def setUpperValue(self, val):
        self._upperValue = min(self._maximum, max(val, self._lowerValue))
        self.update()
        self.rangeChanged.emit(self._lowerValue, self._upperValue)

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        w, h = self.width(), self.height()

        # 进度条背景
        bar_y = h // 2 - self._barHeight // 2
        painter.setBrush(QBrush(QColor(200, 200, 200)))
        painter.drawRect(0, bar_y, w, self._barHeight)

        # 计算滑块位置
        lower_x = int((self._lowerValue - self._minimum) / (self._maximum - self._minimum) * w)
        upper_x = int((self._upperValue - self._minimum) / (self._maximum - self._minimum) * w)

        # 选中范围
        painter.setBrush(QBrush(QColor(100, 180, 250)))
        painter.drawRect(lower_x, bar_y, upper_x - lower_x, self._barHeight)

        # 左滑块
        painter.setBrush(QBrush(QColor(50, 150, 250)))
        painter.drawEllipse(lower_x - self._handleRadius, h // 2 - self._handleRadius,
                            self._handleRadius * 2, self._handleRadius * 2)

        # 右滑块
        painter.setBrush(QBrush(QColor(50, 150, 250)))
        painter.drawEllipse(upper_x - self._handleRadius, h // 2 - self._handleRadius,
                            self._handleRadius * 2, self._handleRadius * 2)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        x = event.x()
        w = self.width()
        lower_x = int((self._lowerValue - self._minimum) / (self._maximum - self._minimum) * w)
        upper_x = int((self._upperValue - self._minimum) / (self._maximum - self._minimum) * w)

        if abs(x - lower_x) < abs(x - upper_x):
            self._dragging = "lower"
        else:
            self._dragging = "upper"

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event):
        if self._dragging:
            ratio = event.x() / self.width()
            value = int(self._minimum + ratio * (self._maximum - self._minimum))
            if self._dragging == "lower":
                self.setLowerValue(value)
            else:
                self.setUpperValue(value)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        self._dragging = None


if __name__ == "__main__":
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    win = QWidget()
    win.setWindowTitle("双滑块 RangeSlider Demo")

    slider = RangeSlider(0, 100)
    slider.setLowerValue(20)
    slider.setUpperValue(80)

    def on_range_changed(l, u):
        print(f"范围: {l} - {u}")

    slider.rangeChanged.connect(on_range_changed)

    # 简单布局
    from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QVBoxLayout
    layout = QVBoxLayout(win)
    layout.addWidget(slider)

    win.show()
    sys.exit(app.exec_())

from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QApplication
from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, QPointF, QPropertyAnimation, pyqtProperty,pyqtSignal
from PyQt5.QtGui import QColor, QPainter, QBrush, QPen
import sys


class ToggleButton(QWidget):
    clicked = pyqtSignal()
    toogled = pyqtSignal(bool)
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._checked = False
        self._position = 0.0  # 动画位置 0~1
        self.animation = QPropertyAnimation(self, b"position", self)
        self.animation.setDuration(200)

        self.setFixedSize(55, 30)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        self.setChecked(not self._checked)
        super().mousePressEvent(event)
        self.clicked.emit()

    def setChecked(self, state: bool):
        if self._checked != state:
            self._checked = state
            self.animation.stop()
            self.animation.setEndValue(1.0 if state else 0.0)
            self.animation.start()
            self.update()
            self.toogled.emit(state)

    def isChecked(self):
        return self._checked

    def getPosition(self):
        return self._position

    def setPosition(self, pos):
        self._position = pos
        self.update()

    position = pyqtProperty(float, fget=getPosition, fset=setPosition)

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        radius = self.height() / 2
        circle_radius = radius - 3

        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)

        # 背景颜色
        if self._checked:
            bg_color = QColor(76, 217, 100)  # iOS 绿色
        else:
            bg_color = QColor(180, 180, 180)  # 灰色

        painter.setBrush(QBrush(bg_color))
        painter.setPen(Qt.NoPen)

        # 背景圆角矩形
        painter.drawRoundedRect(0, 0, self.width(), self.height(), radius, radius)

        # 滑块位置
        x = 3 + self._position * (self.width() - 2 * (circle_radius + 3))

        # 滑块
        painter.setBrush(QBrush(Qt.white))
        painter.drawEllipse(QPointF(x + circle_radius, circle_radius + 3), circle_radius, circle_radius)


# 示例窗口
class MainWindow(QWidget):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.setWindowTitle("Custom iOS Toggle Switch")

        self.toggle = ToggleButton(self)
        self.toggle.move(20, 20)
        self.toggle.setChecked(False)  # 设定初始状态

        self.resize(150, 80)

        # 监听状态变化
        self.toggle.animation.finished.connect(self.on_toggle_changed)

    def on_toggle_changed(self):
        print("当前开关状态:", self.toggle.isChecked())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    w = MainWindow()
    w.show()
    sys.exit(app.exec_())

from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget
from functools import cache
@cache
def loadQssFile(path):
    with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf8') as f:
        s = f.read()
    return s
def loadQss(w: QWidget, path):
    w.setStyleSheet(loadQssFile(path))

from ..companent import Label
from datetime import datetime

from PyQt5.QtCore import QTimer
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QHBoxLayout, QWidget

from core import faultDetectionPipeline
from ..companent import Label


def time_diff(start_time, end_time):
    """计算两个时间的差距（天、时、分、秒）"""
    delta = end_time - start_time
    total_seconds = int(delta.total_seconds())

    days = total_seconds // (24 * 3600)
    hours = (total_seconds % (24 * 3600)) // 3600
    minutes = (total_seconds % 3600) // 60
    seconds = total_seconds % 60

    return days, hours, minutes, seconds


class BottomInfoInterface(QWidget):

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(BottomInfoInterface, self).__init__(parent)
        self.setObjectName("BottomInfoInterface")
        self.timer = QTimer(self)
        self.timer.timeout.connect(self._onTimeOut)
        self._startTime = datetime.now()

        self.initWidget()
        self.initLayout()
        self.timer.start(300)  # 1000ms

        self._onTimeOut()

    def initWidget(self):
        self.vsamper_label = Label(text='', fontSize=12, parent=self, )
        self.camera_0_label = Label(text='', fontSize=12, parent=self, )
        self.camera_1_label = Label(text='', fontSize=12, parent=self, )
        self.camera_2_label = Label(text='', fontSize=12, parent=self, )
        self.camera_3_label = Label(text='', fontSize=12, parent=self, )
        self.run_time_label = Label(text='', fontSize=12, parent=self, )
        self.length_label = Label(text='', fontSize=12, parent=self, )
        self.time_label = Label(text='', fontSize=12, parent=self, )

    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.viewLayout.setSpacing(32)

        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.camera_0_label)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.camera_1_label)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.camera_2_label)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.camera_3_label)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.vsamper_label)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.length_label)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.run_time_label)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.time_label)

    def _onTimeOut(self):
        # whether connect with camera
        is_connect_title = {True: self.tr('已连接'), False: self.tr('未连接')}
        for idx, (label, prefix) in enumerate((
                (self.camera_0_label, self.tr('相机1:')),
                (self.camera_1_label, self.tr('相机2:')),
                (self.camera_2_label, self.tr('相机3:')),
                (self.camera_3_label, self.tr('相机4:')),
        )):
            camera_ctx = faultDetectionPipeline.CAMERA_LIST[idx]
            label.setText(prefix + is_connect_title[faultDetectionPipeline.channels(ctx=camera_ctx) is not None])
        # whether connect with vsampler
        self.vsamper_label.setText(self.tr('计米轮:') + is_connect_title[faultDetectionPipeline._vsampler is not None])
        # length
        self.length_label.setText(
            self.tr('检测长度:') + '{:.2f}'.format(faultDetectionPipeline.accumulatedDistance()) + 'm')
        # time
        d, h, m, s = time_diff(self._startTime, datetime.now())
        self.run_time_label.setText(self.tr('运行时间:') + \
                                    str(d) + self.tr('天') + \
                                    str(h) + self.tr('时') + \
                                    str(m) + self.tr('分') + \
                                    str(s) + self.tr('秒'))
        now_time = datetime.now()
        self.time_label.setText(self.tr('当地时间:') + \
                                str(now_time.year) + self.tr('-') + \
                                str(now_time.month) + self.tr('-') + \
                                str(now_time.day) + self.tr(' ') + \
                                str(now_time.hour) + self.tr('-') +
                                str(now_time.minute) + self.tr('-') + \
                                str(now_time.second))

from PyQt5.QtWidgets import (
    QApplication, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout,
    QLabel, QLineEdit, QPushButton, QListWidget,
    QMessageBox, QComboBox, QGroupBox, QFormLayout
)
from core.database import *
from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt
from ...companent import PushButton, ComboBox,LineEdit,ListWidget,UsersComboBox


# ==========================
# PyQt5 可视化组件
# ==========================

class UserManagerWidget(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.manager = UserManager()
        self.currentUser = None
        self._initUi()

    def _initUi(self):
        mainLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        self._createLoginLayout(mainLayout)
        self._createBatchLayout(mainLayout)
        self._createAdminLayout(mainLayout)

        mainLayout.addStretch()

    # -----------------------
    # 登录区
    # -----------------------
    def _createLoginLayout(self, parentLayout):
        loginGroup = QGroupBox("登录")
        loginLayout = QFormLayout()

        self.userCombo = UsersComboBox()

        self.passwordEdit = LineEdit()
        self.passwordEdit.setEchoMode(LineEdit.Password)

        loginBtn = PushButton(self, "登录")
        loginBtn.clicked.connect(self._login)

        loginLayout.addRow("用户：", self.userCombo)
        loginLayout.addRow("密码：", self.passwordEdit)
        loginLayout.addRow(loginBtn)

        loginGroup.setLayout(loginLayout)
        parentLayout.addWidget(loginGroup)

    # -----------------------
    # 批次区
    # -----------------------
    def _createBatchLayout(self, parentLayout):
        batchGroup = QGroupBox("批次管理")
        batchLayout = QVBoxLayout()

        self.batchList = ListWidget()
        batchLayout.addWidget(self.batchList)

        hLayout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.batchEdit = LineEdit()
        self.batchEdit.setPlaceholderText("输入批次名称")

        #addBtn = PushButton(self, "添加批次")
        #addBtn.clicked.connect(self._addBatch)

        hLayout.addWidget(self.batchEdit)
        #hLayout.addWidget(addBtn)
        batchLayout.addLayout(hLayout)

        batchGroup.setLayout(batchLayout)
        parentLayout.addWidget(batchGroup)

    # -----------------------
    # 管理员区
    # -----------------------
    def _createAdminLayout(self, parentLayout):
        self.adminGroup = QGroupBox("管理员功能")
        adminLayout = QFormLayout()

        self.newUserEdit = LineEdit()
        self.newPwdEdit = LineEdit()
        self.newPwdEdit.setEchoMode(QLineEdit.Password)

        self.roleCombo = ComboBox()
        self.roleCombo.addItems([r.value for r in Role])

        createBtn = PushButton(self, "创建用户")
        createBtn.clicked.connect(self._createUser)

        adminLayout.addRow("用户名：", self.newUserEdit)
        adminLayout.addRow("密码：", self.newPwdEdit)
        adminLayout.addRow("角色：", self.roleCombo)
        adminLayout.addRow(createBtn)

        self.adminGroup.setLayout(adminLayout)
        self.adminGroup.hide()

        parentLayout.addWidget(self.adminGroup)

    def _login(self):
        username = self.userCombo.currentText()
        password = self.passwordEdit.text()

        success, data = self.manager.login(username, password)
        if success:
            self.currentUser = data
            QMessageBox.information(self, "成功", "登录成功")
            self._refresh()
            if data["role"] == Role.ADMIN:
                self.adminGroup.show()
            else:
                self.adminGroup.hide()
        else:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "错误", data)

    def _refresh(self):
        user = self.manager.getUser(self.currentUser["id"])
        self.batchList.clear()
        self.batchList.addItems(user["strList"])

    def _addBatch(self):
        if not self.currentUser:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "错误", "请先登录")
            return

        batchName = self.batchEdit.text()
        success, msg = self.manager.addBatch(
            self.currentUser["role"],
            self.currentUser["id"],
            batchName
        )

        if success:
            self._refresh()
        else:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "错误", msg)

    def _createUser(self):
        success, msg = self.manager.createUser(
            self.currentUser["role"],
            self.newUserEdit.text(),
            self.newPwdEdit.text(),
            Role(self.roleCombo.currentText())
        )

        if success:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "成功", msg)
        else:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "错误", msg)


# ==========================
# 测试运行
# ==========================

    widget.setWindowTitle("批次人员管理系统")
    widget.resize(500, 600)
    widget.show()
    sys.exit(app.exec_())

import os.path

from PyQt5.QtGui import QIcon, QPainter, QPixmap, QPen

    QSpacerItem
from fontTools.misc.iterTools import batched

class SingleAffineTransformWidget(QWidget):
    _range_slider_maxmum = 10000

    def __init__(self, ctx: str, parent=None):
        self._ctx = ctx
        super(SingleAffineTransformWidget, self).__init__(parent)

        self.initWidget()
        self.initLayout()
        self.initData()
        self.initConnect()

    def initWidget(self):
        self.canvas = QWidget(self)
        self.canvas.installEventFilter(self)
        self.top_slider = RangeSlider(parent=self)
        self.bottom_slider = RangeSlider(parent=self)

    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.viewLayout.setSpacing(0)

        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.top_slider, alignment=Qt.AlignHCenter)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.canvas, 1)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.bottom_slider, alignment=Qt.AlignHCenter)

    def initData(self):
        self._set_slider_range(minmun=0, maxmun=self._range_slider_maxmum)

    def initConnect(self):
        pass

    def setValue(self, top_lower, top_upper, bottom_lower, bottom_upper):
        self.top_slider.setLowerValue(int(top_lower * self._range_slider_maxmum))
        self.top_slider.setUpperValue(int(top_upper * self._range_slider_maxmum))
        self.bottom_slider.setLowerValue(int(bottom_lower * self._range_slider_maxmum))
        self.bottom_slider.setUpperValue(int(bottom_upper * self._range_slider_maxmum))

    def _set_slider_width(self, width):
        self.top_slider.setFixedWidth(width)
        self.bottom_slider.setFixedWidth(width)

    def _set_slider_range(self, minmun, maxmun):
        self.top_slider.setRange(minmun, maxmun)
        self.bottom_slider.setRange(minmun, maxmun)

    def eventFilter(self, obj, e: QEvent):
        # 绘制
        if obj is self.canvas and e.type() == QEvent.Paint:
            info = faultDetectionPipeline.channels(ctx=self._ctx)
            painter = QPainter(self.canvas)
            painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)
            if info.camera is not None:
                image_np = info.camera.read()
                pixmap = utils.np_2_pixmap(arr=image_np)
                # resize
                if pixmap.size() != self.canvas.size():
                    pixmap: QPixmap = pixmap.scaled(self.canvas.size(), Qt.KeepAspectRatio)

                res = self.canvas.size() - pixmap.size()
                offset = QPoint(res.width() // 2, res.height() // 2)
                painter.drawPixmap(offset, pixmap)

                # 绘制放射变换的tips
                painter.setPen(QPen(Qt.red, 3))
                painter.drawLine(
                    QPoint(
                        offset.x() + int(self.top_slider.lowerValue() / self._range_slider_maxmum * pixmap.width()),
                        offset.y()),
                    QPoint(
                        offset.x() + int(self.bottom_slider.lowerValue() / self._range_slider_maxmum * pixmap.width()),
                        offset.y() + pixmap.height()))
                painter.drawLine(
                    QPoint(
                        offset.x() + int(self.top_slider.upperValue() / self._range_slider_maxmum * pixmap.width()),
                        offset.y()),
                    QPoint(
                        offset.x() + int(self.bottom_slider.upperValue() / self._range_slider_maxmum * pixmap.width()),
                        offset.y() + pixmap.height()))

                self._set_slider_width(pixmap.width())

        return super(SingleAffineTransformWidget, self).eventFilter(obj, e)

    def value(self):
        top_lower = self.top_slider.lowerValue() / self._range_slider_maxmum
        top_upper = self.top_slider.upperValue() / self._range_slider_maxmum
        bottom_lower = self.bottom_slider.lowerValue() / self._range_slider_maxmum
        bottom_upper = self.bottom_slider.upperValue() / self._range_slider_maxmum
        return top_lower, top_upper, bottom_lower, bottom_upper


class AffineTransformWidget(QWidget):

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(AffineTransformWidget, self).__init__(parent)

        self.initWidget()
        self.initLayout()
        self.initConnect()

    def initWidget(self):
        self.return_btn = PushButton(self,self.tr('返回'))
        self.save_btn = PushButton(self,self.tr('设置'))
        self.pivot = Pivot(parent=self)
        self.stackedWidget = QStackedWidget(self)

        # 每个相机单独设置放射变换
        self.singleAffineTransformWidget_list: List[SingleAffineTransformWidget] = []
        for i, (channel, _) in enumerate(zip(faultDetectionPipeline.CHANNELS,
                                               (1, 2, 3, 4))):
            w = SingleAffineTransformWidget(ctx=channel, parent=self)
            self.singleAffineTransformWidget_list.append(w)
            self.stackedWidget.addWidget(w)
            self.pivot.addButton(text=self.tr(f'通道{i}'),
                                 onClicked=partial(self.stackedWidget.setCurrentWidget, w))

    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.hlayout1 = QHBoxLayout()

        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.pivot)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.stackedWidget, 1)

        self.hlayout1.addWidget(self.return_btn)
        self.hlayout1.addWidget(self.save_btn)
        self.viewLayout.addLayout(self.hlayout1)

    def initConnect(self):
        self.return_btn.clicked.connect(self.close)
        self.save_btn.clicked.connect(self._save_to_model_config)
        self.save_btn.clicked.connect(self.close)

    def setModelConfig(self, model_config: ModelConfig):
        self._model_config = model_config
        for single in self.singleAffineTransformWidget_list:
            top_lower, top_upper, bottom_lower, bottom_upper = model_config.rectification[single._ctx]
            single.setValue(top_lower=top_lower, top_upper=top_upper,
                            bottom_lower=bottom_lower, bottom_upper=bottom_upper)

    def _save_to_model_config(self):
        for single in self.singleAffineTransformWidget_list:
            top_lower, top_upper, bottom_lower, bottom_upper = single.value()

            self._model_config.rectification[single._ctx] = (top_lower, top_upper, bottom_lower, bottom_upper)

    def paintEvent(self, e):
        super(AffineTransformWidget, self).paintEvent(e)
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)

        painter.setPen(Qt.NoPen)
        painter.setBrush(Qt.white)
        painter.drawRoundedRect(self.rect(), 5, 5)


================================================================================
FILE: ./view\interface\detection_interface\camera_display_interface.py
================================================================================
from functools import partial
from typing import List

from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt
from PyQt5.QtGui import QFont
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QGridLayout, QSizePolicy, QSpacerItem
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, QStackedWidget, QWidget

from core import faultDetectionPipeline
from ...companent import Label, Pivot, EdgegWidget, PushButton
from ...companent import PipelineCameraLabel, PipelineResultLabel, MaskBox
from .fault_view import FaultView




class CameraDisplayInterface(EdgegWidget):

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(CameraDisplayInterface, self).__init__(parent)

        self.initWidget()
        self.initConnect()
        self.initLayout()

    def initWidget(self):
        # 导航栏
        self.pivot = Pivot(self)
        self.pivot.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Minimum, QSizePolicy.Minimum)
        self.resultDisplay = QWidget(self)
        self.origineDisplay = QWidget(self)
        self.stackedWidget = QStackedWidget(self)
        self._result_labels: List[PipelineResultLabel] = []
        for idx in faultDetectionPipeline.CHANNELS:
            label = PipelineResultLabel(ctx=idx, parent=self)
            self._result_labels.append(label)

        self._merge_labels: List[PipelineCameraLabel] = []
        for idx in faultDetectionPipeline.CHANNELS:
            label = PipelineCameraLabel(ctx=idx, parent=self)
            self._merge_labels.append(label)

    def initConnect(self):
        for label in self._result_labels:
            label.clicked.connect(partial(self._onLabelClickedEvent, label))
        for label in self._merge_labels:
            label.clicked.connect(partial(self._onLabelClickedEvent, label))

    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(5, 5, 5, 5)
        self.hlayout1 = QHBoxLayout()
        self.hlayout1.addWidget(self.pivot)
        self.hlayout1.addSpacerItem(QSpacerItem(0, 0, QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Expanding))
        self.viewLayout.addLayout(self.hlayout1)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.stackedWidget)
        self.viewLayout.setStretch(0, 0)
        self.viewLayout.setStretch(1, 1)

        self.viewLayout2 = QGridLayout(self.origineDisplay)
        self.viewLayout2.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.viewLayout2.addWidget(Label(self.tr('原始图像'), parent=self, weight=QFont.Bold, fontSize=20), 0, 0)
        for idx, label in enumerate(self._merge_labels):
            self.viewLayout2.addWidget(Label(f"管线{idx + 1}"), 1, idx, alignment=Qt.AlignCenter)
            self.viewLayout2.addWidget(label, 2, idx)
        self.viewLayout2.setRowStretch(0, 0)
        self.viewLayout2.setRowStretch(1, 0)
        self.viewLayout2.setRowStretch(2, 1)

        self.viewLayout3 = QGridLayout(self.resultDisplay)
        self.viewLayout3.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.viewLayout3.addWidget(Label(self.tr('检测结果'), parent=self, weight=QFont.Bold, fontSize=20), 0, 0)
        for idx, label in enumerate(self._result_labels):
            self.viewLayout3.addWidget(Label(f"管线{idx + 1}"), 1, idx, alignment=Qt.AlignCenter)
            self.viewLayout3.addWidget(label, 2, idx)
        self.viewLayout3.setRowStretch(0, 0)
        self.viewLayout3.setRowStretch(1, 0)
        self.viewLayout3.setRowStretch(2, 1)

        self.pivot.addButton(text=self.tr('检测结果'),
                             onClicked=lambda: self.stackedWidget.setCurrentWidget(self.origineDisplay))
        self.pivot.addButton(text=self.tr('检测结果'),
                             onClicked=lambda: self.stackedWidget.setCurrentWidget(self.resultDisplay))
        self.stackedWidget.addWidget(self.origineDisplay)
        self.stackedWidget.addWidget(self.resultDisplay)

    def _onLabelClickedEvent(self, label: PipelineCameraLabel):
        view = FaultView()
        label = type(label)(ctx=label.ctx())
        view.viewLayout.addWidget(label)

        # mask
        w = MaskBox(parent=self.window())
        w.setWidget(widget=view)
        w.exec()

from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QPushButton

from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, QStackedWidget, QWidget, QMessageBox,QSplitter

from .info import InfoWidget
from .project_infomation import BatchInfomationWidget
from .project_setter import BatchSettingWidget

from .fault_overview import OnlineFaultOverview

from .enable_interface import EnableInterface

class DetectionInterface(QWidget):

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(DetectionInterface, self).__init__(parent)
        self.setObjectName("DetectionInterface")

        self.initWidget()
        self.initConnect()
        self.initLayout()

    def initWidget(self):
        # 当前批次的显示
        self.faultOverview = OnlineFaultOverview(self)
        # 摄像头的原始图像和处理结果可视化
        self.cameraDisplayInterface = CameraDisplayInterface(parent=self)
        # 放电检测的数据显示
        self.lightnInterface = LightnInterface(parent=self)
        # 设备的使能窗口
        self.enableInterface = EnableInterface(parent=self)

        # info
        self.infoWidget = InfoWidget(parent=self)

        # 控制区域
        self.panel = Pivot(parent=self)
        self.w3 = QWidget(self)
        self.stack = QStackedWidget(self)
        self.batchSettingWidget = BatchSettingWidget(parent=self)
        self.batchInfomationWidget = BatchInfomationWidget(parent=self)
        self.stack.addWidget(self.batchSettingWidget)
        self.stack.addWidget(self.batchInfomationWidget)
        self.panel.addButton(text="设置", onClicked=lambda: self.stack.setCurrentWidget(self.batchSettingWidget))
        self.panel.addButton(text="结果", onClicked=lambda: self.stack.setCurrentWidget(self.batchInfomationWidget))

        # 分割
        self.splitter = QSplitter(self)
        self.splitter.setContentsMargins(0,0,0,0)

    def initConnect(self):
        pass

    def initLayout(self):
        self.w1= QWidget(self)
        self.w2 = QWidget(self)
        self.viewLayout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)

        self.vlayout = QVBoxLayout(self.w1)
        self.hlayout1 = QHBoxLayout()
        self.hlayout1.addWidget(self.faultOverview)
        self.hlayout1.addWidget(self.lightnInterface)
        self.vlayout.addLayout(self.hlayout1)
        self.vlayout.addWidget(self.cameraDisplayInterface)
        self.vlayout.addWidget(self.infoWidget)
        self.hlayout1.setStretch(0, 3)
        self.hlayout1.setStretch(1, 0)
        self.vlayout.setStretch(0, 1)
        self.vlayout.setStretch(1, 4)

        self.settinglLayout = QVBoxLayout(self.w2)
        self.settinglLayout.addWidget(self.panel)
        self.settinglLayout.addWidget(self.stack, 1)
        self.settinglLayout.addWidget(self.enableInterface)

        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.splitter)
        self.splitter.addWidget(self.w1)
        self.splitter.addWidget(self.w2)
        self.splitter.setStretchFactor(0, 7)
        self.splitter.setStretchFactor(1,3)
        self.viewLayout.setStretch(0, 1)

from typing import Callable

import serial
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, QWidget, QMessageBox
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QPushButton

from core.config import config
from core.pipeline import VSamplerSerialConnectError
from core.hk_camera.error import CameraInitError
from core.pipeline import faultDetectionPipeline, LightningSerialConnectError, LightSourceControlerSerialConnectError
from ...companent.font import setFont
from ...companent import PushButton


class EnableInterface(QWidget):

    def _change_serials_state(self,
                              btn: QPushButton,
                              control_text: str,
                              check_enable_func: Callable,
                              set_enable_func: Callable,
                              port,
                              title: str,
                              connect_error: Exception
                              ):
        # 检查是否能开启
        if not check_enable_func():
            if port == 'None':
                QMessageBox.warning(self.window(), self.tr(title), self.tr('未设置串口，无法启动！'))
                return
        try:
            set_enable_func(not check_enable_func())
        except serial.serialutil.SerialException as e:
            print(e)
            QMessageBox.warning(self.window(), self.tr(title), self.tr(f'{port}链接失败！'))
        except connect_error as e:
            print(e)
            QMessageBox.warning(self.window(), self.tr(title),
                                self.tr(f'{port}链接成功,但在初始化过程中失败！') + '\n' + str(e))
        except Exception as e:
            raise e
        btn.setText(self.tr(('关闭' if check_enable_func() else '启动') + control_text))

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(EnableInterface, self).__init__(parent)

        self.initWidget()
        self.initConnect()
        self.initLayout()

    def initWidget(self):
        self.vsamplerBtn = PushButton(text=self.tr('启动计米'), parent=self)
        self.cameraBtn = PushButton(text=self.tr('启动摄像头'), parent=self)
        self.detectBtn = PushButton(parent=self, text=self.tr('启动检测'), )
        self.lightningBtn = PushButton(text=self.tr('启动放电检测'), parent=self)
        self.lightsourceBtn = PushButton(text=self.tr('启动光源'), parent=self)
        self.vsamplerBtn.setMinimumHeight(32)
        self.lightningBtn.setMinimumHeight(32)
        self.cameraBtn.setMinimumHeight(32)
        self.lightsourceBtn.setMinimumHeight(32)
        self.detectBtn.setMinimumHeight(64)
        setFont(self.detectBtn, fontSize=24)

    def initConnect(self):
        self.cameraBtn.clicked.connect(self._onCameraBtnCliked)
        self.detectBtn.clicked.connect(self._onDetectBtnCliked)
        self.vsamplerBtn.clicked.connect(lambda: print(123123123))
        self.vsamplerBtn.clicked.connect(lambda: self._change_serials_state(
            btn=self.vsamplerBtn,
            control_text='计米',
            check_enable_func=faultDetectionPipeline.isVSamplerEnabled,
            set_enable_func=faultDetectionPipeline.setVSamplerEnabled,
            port=config.get(config.VSampler_Port),
            title='计米',
            connect_error=VSamplerSerialConnectError
        ))
        self.lightningBtn.clicked.connect(lambda: self._change_serials_state(
            btn=self.lightningBtn,
            control_text='放电检测',
            check_enable_func=faultDetectionPipeline.isLightningEnabled,
            set_enable_func=faultDetectionPipeline.setLightningEnabled,
            port=config.get(config.Lightning_Port),
            title='放电检测',
            connect_error=LightningSerialConnectError
        ))
        self.lightsourceBtn.clicked.connect(lambda: self._change_serials_state(
            btn=self.lightsourceBtn,
            control_text='光源控制器',
            check_enable_func=faultDetectionPipeline.isLightSourceEnabled,
            set_enable_func=faultDetectionPipeline.setLightSourceEnable,
            port=config.get(config.LightSource_Port),
            title='光源控制器',
            connect_error=LightSourceControlerSerialConnectError
        ))

    def _onCameraBtnCliked(self):
        try:
            faultDetectionPipeline.setCameraEnabled(not faultDetectionPipeline.isCameraEnabled())
        except CameraInitError as e:
            QMessageBox.warning(self.window(), self.tr('启动摄像头'),
                                self.tr(f'摄像头初始化失败！') + '\n' + str(e))
        except Exception as e:
            raise e
        self.cameraBtn.setText(self.tr('启动摄像头' if not faultDetectionPipeline.isCameraEnabled() else '关闭摄像头'))


    def _onDetectBtnCliked(self):
        self.detectBtn.setText(self.tr('启动检测' if faultDetectionPipeline.isDetectEnabled() else '关闭检测'))
        faultDetectionPipeline.setDetectEnable(not faultDetectionPipeline.isDetectEnabled())

    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)

        self.vsampler_lightning_btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.vsampler_lightning_btn_layout.addWidget(self.vsamplerBtn)
        self.vsampler_lightning_btn_layout.addWidget(self.lightningBtn)

        self.camera_lightsource_btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.camera_lightsource_btn_layout.addWidget(self.cameraBtn)
        self.camera_lightsource_btn_layout.addWidget(self.lightsourceBtn)

        self.viewLayout.addLayout(self.vsampler_lightning_btn_layout)
        self.viewLayout.addLayout(self.camera_lightsource_btn_layout)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.detectBtn)

        self.viewLayout.setStretch(0, 1)

FILE: ./view\interface\detection_interface\fault_overview.py

from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QGridLayout
from PyQt5.QtGui import QPixmap, QOpenGLShader, QPainter, QPen
from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, QTimer, QPoint, QPointF, QRectF
from ...companent import FaultOverview
from core.pipeline import faultDetectionPipeline
from core.signal_bus import SignalBus
from typing import List, Set


class OnlineFaultOverview(FaultOverview):
    """ 实时更新最新的检测结果数据 """
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(OnlineFaultOverview, self).__init__(parent)
        # 当批次更新后, 重新计数
        SignalBus.batchUpdated.connect(self.clear)
        SignalBus.faultUpdated.connect(self.onFaultUpdated)

    def onFaultUpdated(self):
        """ 缺陷更新 """
        distance = faultDetectionPipeline.accumulatedDistance(obj='batch')
        minimum, maximum = self.scope()
        for line, ctx in zip((self.left_line, self.top_line, self.right_line, self.bottom_line),
                             faultDetectionPipeline.CHANNELS):
            camera = faultDetectionPipeline.channels(ctx=ctx)
            # 如果没有进行检测可以跳过
            if camera.camera is None: continue
            if camera.faults is None: continue
            if len(camera.faults) == 0: continue

            minimum = min(minimum, distance)
            maximum = max(maximum, distance)
            line.addPoint(distance)
            line.update()
        # =============
        # 放电
        # ============
        if faultDetectionPipeline.lightningOutput().fault == True:
            minimum = min(minimum, distance)
            maximum = max(maximum, distance)
            self.lightn_line.addPoint(distance)
            self.update()
        self.setScope(minimum=minimum, maximum=maximum)

from functools import partial
from typing import List

from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt
from PyQt5.QtGui import QFont

        self.mainLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        self.mainLayout.addLayout(self.hlayout)

        self.hlayout.setStretch(0, 1)
        self.hlayout.setStretch(1, 1)
        self.hlayout.setStretch(2, 1)
        self.returnBtn.setFixedHeight(64)

        self.returnBtn.clicked.connect(self.close)

from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QGridLayout, QPushButton
from PyQt5.QtCore import pyqtSignal, QTimer
from view.companent import Label
from typing import List
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QMainWindow, QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, QStackedWidget, QFrame, QWidget
from core import faultDetectionPipeline


class InfoWidget(QWidget):
    """一些总体的信息显示 """
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(InfoWidget, self).__init__(parent)
        self.timer = QTimer(self)
        self.timer.timeout.connect(self._onTimeOut)

        self.initWidget()
        self.initConnect()
        self.initLayout()

        self.timer.start(50)  # 50ms
        self._onTimeOut()

    def initWidget(self):
        self.v_label = Label(text='', fontSize=14, parent=self, )
        self.av_label = Label(text='', fontSize=14, parent=self, )
        self.process_time_label = Label(text='', fontSize=14, parent=self, )
        self.pipeline_state = Label(text='', fontSize=14, parent=self, )

    def initConnect(self):
        pass

    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QGridLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)

        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.v_label, 0, 0)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.av_label, 1, 0)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.process_time_label, 0, 1)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.pipeline_state, 0, 2)

    def _onTimeOut(self):
        self.v_label.setText(self.tr('速度(m/s):') + "%.3f" % faultDetectionPipeline.velocity())
        self.av_label.setText(self.tr('角速度(转/s):') + "%.3f" % faultDetectionPipeline.angularVelocity())
        self.process_time_label.setText(self.tr('处理时间:') + "%.3f" % faultDetectionPipeline.processTime())
        self.pipeline_state.setText(self.tr('处理状态:') +  str(faultDetectionPipeline.state()))

from ...companent import Pivot, LightnInterface
from core.signal_bus import SignalBus
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QGridLayout
from PyQt5.QtGui import QPixmap, QOpenGLShader, QPainter, QPen
from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, QTimer, QPoint, QPointF, QRectF
from ...companent import FaultOverview
from core.pipeline import faultDetectionPipeline
from core.signal_bus import SignalBus
from typing import List, Set

class LightnInterface(LightnInterface):
    """实时更新最新的放电检测结果数据"""
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(LightnInterface,self).__init__(parent=parent)

        # 当批次更新后, 重新计数
        SignalBus.batchUpdated.connect(self.clear)
        SignalBus.faultUpdated.connect(self.onFaultUpdated)
        self.update()

    def onFaultUpdated(self):
        output = faultDetectionPipeline.lightningOutput()
        if output.fault:
            self.setDistance(output.distance)
            self.update()

import os.path


from PyQt5.QtGui import QIcon, QStandardItemModel, QStandardItem
from PyQt5.QtCore import pyqtSlot, QTimer,Qt
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QGridLayout, QPushButton, QLabel, QLineEdit, QSpacerItem, QSizePolicy
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QTableView, QHeaderView
from core.signal_bus import SignalBus
from view.companent import PipelineCameraLabel, PipelineResultLabel, Label,ResultLabel
from typing import List
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QMainWindow, QAbstractItemView, QVBoxLayout, QStackedWidget, QFrame, QWidget
from core import faultDetectionPipeline
from core.const import Path
from ...companent import Label, EdgegWidget,TableView,MaskBox
from .fault_view import FaultView

class BatchInfomationWidget(EdgegWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent):
        super(BatchInfomationWidget, self).__init__(parent)
        self.initWidget()
        self.initConnect()
        self.initLayout()

    def initWidget(self):
        self.batch_name_label = Label(text='检查结果:', fontSize=16, parent=self)
        self.table_view = TableView(self)
        self.table_view.setFrameShape(QFrame.NoFrame)
        self.table_view.setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView.NoEditTriggers)
        self.table_view.verticalHeader().setHidden(True)
        # the model of table view
        self.model = QStandardItemModel()
        self.model.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(['位置', '传感器', '缺陷类型'])
        self.table_view.setModel(self.model)

    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(5, 5, 5, 5)

        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.batch_name_label)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.table_view)
        self.viewLayout.setStretch(1, 1)

    def initConnect(self):
        SignalBus.batchUpdated.connect(
            lambda: self.batch_name_label.setText(self.tr('检查结果:') + faultDetectionPipeline.batchName()))
        SignalBus.batchUpdated.connect(lambda :self.model.clear())
        SignalBus.faultUpdated.connect(self._onFaultUpdated)
        # table clicked
        self.table_view.clicked.connect(self._onTableViewClicked)

    def _onFaultUpdated(self):
        distance = faultDetectionPipeline.accumulatedDistance(obj='batch')
        for fault in faultDetectionPipeline.lastFault():
            for f in fault.faults:
                fualts_string = ','.join(f.ftype[:min(len(f.ftype), 3)]) + ('...' if len(f.ftype) > 3 else '')
                sampler_name = fault.sampler

                rowCount = self.model.rowCount()
                item = QStandardItem(f'{distance:.2f}')
                item.setData(faultDetectionPipeline.batchName(), Qt.UserRole)
                self.model.setItem(rowCount, 0, item)
                self.model.setItem(rowCount, 1, QStandardItem(sampler_name))
                self.model.setItem(rowCount, 2, QStandardItem(fualts_string))




    def _onTableViewClicked(self, index):
        """ event """

        row = index.row()

        distance = self.model.item(row, 0).text()
        batchName = self.model.item(row, 0).data(Qt.UserRole)
        sensor = self.model.item(row, 1).text()
        fault = self.model.item(row, 2).text()

        if batchName is None:
            return

        view = FaultView()
        view.label = ResultLabel(parent=view)
        view.label.setResult(os.path.join(Path.OUTPUT_DIR,batchName,distance,sensor))
        view.viewLayout.addWidget(view.label)

        # mask
        w = MaskBox(parent=self.window())
        w.setWidget(widget=view)
        w.exec()

import functools

from typing import List

MODEL_CONFIG_PATH = os.path.join(Path.CONFIG_DIR, 'model')


class ModelComboBox(ComboBox):
    def showPopup(self):
        """ 动态加载Items """
        items = [i.removesuffix('.json') for i in os.listdir(MODEL_CONFIG_PATH) if i.endswith('.json')]
        self.setItems(items)
        super(ModelComboBox, self).showPopup()  # 弹出选项框


class PipelineCameraComboBox(ComboBox):
    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super(PipelineCameraComboBox, self).__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self._items = [f"相机{i + 1}" for i, _ in enumerate(faultDetectionPipeline.CAMERA_LIST)]
        self.addItems(self._items)


class ExposureBatchBox(ComboBox):
