        self._items = [f"批次:{i + 1}" for i, _ in enumerate(faultDetectionPipeline.CAMERA_LIST)]
        self.addItems(self._items)


class BatchNameWidget(EdgegWidget):
    PREX_NOW = '当前'
    PREX_BATCH = '批次:'
    PREX_WORKER = '负责人:'

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(BatchNameWidget, self).__init__(parent)
        self.batch_name_label = Label(text=self.PREX_NOW + self.PREX_BATCH, fontSize=16, parent=self)
        self.woker_label = Label(text=self.PREX_NOW + self.PREX_WORKER, fontSize=16, parent=self)
        self.batch_name_edit = LineEdit(parent=self)
        self.swap_batch_btn = PushButton(text=self.tr('更换\n批次'), parent=self)
        self.usersBox = UsersComboBox(self)

        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(5, 5, 5, 5)
        self.hlayout1 = QHBoxLayout()
        self.hlayout2 = QHBoxLayout()
        self.glayout1 = QGridLayout()
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.batch_name_label)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.woker_label)
        # layout: 2
        self.glayout1.addWidget(Label(self.tr(self.PREX_BATCH), fontSize=16, parent=self), 0, 0,
                                alignment=Qt.AlignRight)
        self.glayout1.addWidget(self.batch_name_edit, 0, 1)
        self.glayout1.addWidget(Label(self.tr(self.PREX_WORKER), fontSize=16, parent=self), 1, 0,
                                alignment=Qt.AlignRight)
        self.glayout1.addWidget(self.usersBox, 1, 1)
        self.hlayout2.addLayout(self.glayout1)
        self.hlayout2.addWidget(self.swap_batch_btn)
        self.viewLayout.addLayout(self.hlayout2)

        self.swap_batch_btn.clicked.connect(self._batch_name_clicked)
        SignalBus.batchUpdated.connect(
            lambda b, w: (self.batch_name_label.setText(self.tr(self.PREX_NOW + self.PREX_BATCH) + b),
                          self.woker_label.setText(self.tr(self.PREX_NOW + self.PREX_WORKER) + w),))

    def _batch_name_clicked(self, ):
        if self.batch_name_edit.text() == '':
            QMessageBox.warning(self.window(),
                                '替换批次',
                                f"不接受空本作为批次名称")
            return
        if QMessageBox.question(self.window(),
                                '替换批次',
                                f"{self.PREX_BATCH}{faultDetectionPipeline.batchName() or ''} -> {self.batch_name_edit.text()}\n"
                                f"{self.PREX_WORKER}{faultDetectionPipeline.workerName() or ''} -> {self.usersBox.currentText()}\n"
                                f"替换后,新的缺陷信息将记录到新批次",
                                QMessageBox.StandardButtons(QMessageBox.Yes | QMessageBox.No), ):
            Function.swapBatch(batchName=self.batch_name_edit.text(), wokerName=self.usersBox.currentText())
            self.batch_name_edit.clear()


class ModelCameraSettingWidget(QWidget):
    """ 对摄像头在系统和在物理装置对应的设置 """
    configChanged = pyqtSignal()  # 通知外部组件, config发生了变更

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        self._model_config: ModelConfig = None
        self._syncing = False
        super(ModelCameraSettingWidget, self).__init__(parent)
        self.viewlayout = QGridLayout(self)
        self.viewlayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.viewlayout.addWidget(Label(text=self.tr('摄像头')), 0, 1, alignment=Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.viewlayout.addWidget(Label(text=self.tr('曝光批次')), 0, 2, alignment=Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.viewlayout.addWidget(Label(text=self.tr('使能')), 0, 3, alignment=Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.cameraComboxs: List[PipelineCameraComboBox] = []
        self.exposureBatchComboxs: List[ExposureBatchBox] = []
        self.btns: List[ToggleButton] = []
        for i, c in enumerate(faultDetectionPipeline.CHANNELS):
            combox1 = PipelineCameraComboBox()
            combox2 = ExposureBatchBox()
            btn = ToggleButton()
            self.viewlayout.addWidget(Label(self.tr('管线') + str(i + 1)), i + 1, 0, )
            self.viewlayout.addWidget(combox1, i + 1, 1)
            self.viewlayout.addWidget(combox2, i + 1, 2)
            self.viewlayout.addWidget(btn, i + 1, 3)
            combox1.currentTextChanged.connect(functools.partial(self._on_combox1_currentTextChanged, c, combox1))
            combox2.currentTextChanged.connect(
                functools.partial(self._on_combox2_currentTextChanged, c, combox1, combox2))
            btn.clicked.connect(functools.partial(self._on_btn_clicked, c, btn))
            self.cameraComboxs.append(combox1)
            self.btns.append(btn)
            self.exposureBatchComboxs.append(combox2)
        self.viewlayout.setColumnStretch(0, 0)
        self.viewlayout.setColumnStretch(1, 1)
        self.viewlayout.setColumnStretch(2, 1)

    def _on_combox1_currentTextChanged(self, ctx, combox: PipelineCameraComboBox):
        if self._model_config is None: return
        self._model_config.mapper[ctx] = faultDetectionPipeline.CAMERA_LIST[combox.currentIndex()]
        self._update_from_config()
        self.configChanged.emit()

    def _on_combox2_currentTextChanged(self, ctx, cc: PipelineCameraComboBox, ec: ExposureBatchBox):
        if self._model_config is None: return
        self._model_config.exposureBatch[faultDetectionPipeline.CAMERA_LIST[cc.currentIndex()]] = ec.currentIndex()
        self._update_from_config()
        self.configChanged.emit()

    def _on_btn_clicked(self, ctx, btn: ToggleButton):
        if self._model_config is None: return
        self._model_config.enable[ctx] = btn.isChecked()
        self._update_from_config()
        self.configChanged.emit()

    def setModelConfig(self, config: ModelConfig):
        """ setter """
        self._model_config = config
        self._update_from_config()

    def _update_from_config(self):
        if self._syncing:
            return
        self._syncing = True
        # 同步
        for c, cc, ec, btn in zip(faultDetectionPipeline.CHANNELS, self.cameraComboxs, self.exposureBatchComboxs,
                                  self.btns):
            cc.setCurrentIndex(faultDetectionPipeline.CAMERA_LIST.index(self._model_config.mapper[c]))
            ec.setCurrentIndex(self._model_config.exposureBatch[faultDetectionPipeline.CAMERA_LIST[cc.currentIndex()]])

            btn.setChecked(self._model_config.enable[c])
        self._syncing = False


class ModelSettingWidget(EdgegWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        self._model_config: ModelConfig = None
        super(ModelSettingWidget, self).__init__(parent)

        self.initWidget()
        self.initConnect()
        self.initLayout()

    def initWidget(self):
        # model configuation
        self.now_model_label = Label(text=self.tr('当前配置:'), fontSize=18, parent=self)
        self.model_combo_box = ModelComboBox(parent=self)
        self.add_model_btn = PushButton(text=self.tr('添加型号'), parent=self)
        self.set_model_btn = PushButton(text=self.tr('使用配置'), parent=self)
        self.save_model_btn = PushButton(text=self.tr('保存配置'), parent=self)
        self.set_affineTransform_btn = PushButton(text='放射变换', parent=self)
        self.set_camera_mapper = ModelCameraSettingWidget(parent=self)

    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(5, 5, 5, 5)
        self.hlayout1 = QHBoxLayout()
        self.hlayout2 = QHBoxLayout()
        self.hlayout3 = QHBoxLayout()

        # layout: 2
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.now_model_label)
        self.hlayout2.addWidget(self.model_combo_box)
        self.hlayout2.addWidget(self.add_model_btn)
        self.viewLayout.addLayout(self.hlayout2)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.set_affineTransform_btn)

        self.hlayout3.addWidget(self.set_model_btn)
        self.hlayout3.addWidget(self.save_model_btn)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.set_camera_mapper)
        self.viewLayout.addLayout(self.hlayout3)

        self.viewLayout.addSpacerItem(QSpacerItem(0, 0, QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Expanding))

    def initConnect(self):
        self.add_model_btn.clicked.connect(self._on_add_model_btn_clicked)
        self.model_combo_box.currentTextChanged.connect(self._open_model_config)
        self.set_affineTransform_btn.clicked.connect(self._on_set_affineTransform_btn_clecked)
        self.save_model_btn.clicked.connect(self._save_model_config)
        self.set_model_btn.clicked.connect(self._on_set_model_btn_clicked)

    def _on_add_model_btn_clicked(self):
        """ 添加新的型号配置"""
        string, success = QInputDialog.getText(self.window(), self.tr('创建型号配置'), self.tr('输入型号'))
        # 是否取消
        if not success:
            return

        # 是否已经存在
        config = os.path.join(MODEL_CONFIG_PATH, f'{string}.json')
        if os.path.exists(config):
            reply = QMessageBox.warning(self, "创建型号配置", f"{string}已被占用", QMessageBox.No, QMessageBox.No)
            return

        # 创建
        if not os.path.exists(os.path.dirname(config)):
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(config))
        with open(config, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump({}, f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)

    def _open_model_config(self):
        """ 打开一个型号配置 """
        path = os.path.join(MODEL_CONFIG_PATH, f'{self.model_combo_box.currentText()}.json')
        self._model_config = ModelConfig.load(path)
        # 同步到子组件
        self.set_camera_mapper.setModelConfig(self._model_config)

    def _save_model_config(self):
        """ 保存一个型号配置 """
        if self._model_config is None: return
        path = os.path.join(MODEL_CONFIG_PATH, f'{self.model_combo_box.currentText()}.json')
        self._model_config.save(path)

    def _on_set_model_btn_clicked(self):
        """ 设置 型号配置 运用到管线中"""
        if self._model_config is None:
            return
        if QMessageBox.question(self.window(),
                                '设置型号配置',
                                f"新的型号配置将会被保存，以及用于检测管线，请确保所有配置已经设置",
                                QMessageBox.StandardButtons(QMessageBox.Yes | QMessageBox.No), ):
            path = os.path.join(MODEL_CONFIG_PATH, f'{self.model_combo_box.currentText()}.json')
            self._model_config.save(path)
            faultDetectionPipeline.setModelConfig(path)
            self.now_model_label.setText(self.tr('当前配置:') + ' ' + self.model_combo_box.currentText())

    def _on_set_affineTransform_btn_clecked(self):
        """ 点击事件 """
        if self._model_config is None: return
        affineTransformWidget = AffineTransformWidget()
        affineTransformWidget.setModelConfig(self._model_config)

        w = MaskBox(parent=self.window())
        w.setWidget(widget=affineTransformWidget)
        w.exec()


class BatchSettingWidget(EdgegWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent):
        super(BatchSettingWidget, self).__init__(parent)

        self.batchNameWidget = BatchNameWidget(parent=self)
        self.modelSettingWidget = ModelSettingWidget(parent=self)
        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(5, 5, 5, 5)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.batchNameWidget)
        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.modelSettingWidget)

        self.setMinimumWidth(256)

class MemoryhInterface(QWidget):

        self.visionDetectWidgwt.load(path=path)
        self.lightnInterface.load(path=path)

import os

from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QGridLayout
from PyQt5.QtGui import QPixmap, QOpenGLShader, QPainter, QPen
from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, QTimer, QPoint, QPointF, QRectF

from ...companent import FaultOverview
from core.pipeline import faultDetectionPipeline
from typing import List, Set


class BatchFaultOverview(FaultOverview):
    """ 读取本地数据, 展示在一条漆包线上的权限位置"""

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(BatchFaultOverview, self).__init__(parent)

    def load(self, path):
        self.clear()
        minimum, maximum = None, None
        for i in os.listdir(path):
            _f = tuple(os.listdir(os.path.join(path, i)))
            i = float(i)
            minimum = min(minimum, i) if minimum is not None else i
            maximum = max(maximum, i) if maximum is not None else i
            if 'c1' in _f:
                self.left_line.addPoint(i)
            if 'c2' in _f:
                self.top_line.addPoint(i)
            if 'c3' in _f:
                self.right_line.addPoint(i)
            if 'c4' in _f:
                self.bottom_line.addPoint(i)
            if 'lightning' in _f:
                self.lightn_line.addPoint(i)
        self.setScope(minimum=minimum, maximum=maximum)
        self.update()

import json
import os
from urllib.response import addinfo

from PyQt5.QtCore import QTimer, QStandardPaths, QStringListModel, Qt
from PyQt5.QtGui import QStandardItemModel, QStandardItem
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, QStackedWidget, QListView, QGridLayout
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QApplication, QMainWindow, QListView

from ...companent import Label, Pivot, LightnInterface, FaultOverview

from .vision import VisionDetectWidgwt
from .navigation import Navigation
from .fault_overview import BatchFaultOverview
from core.const import Path
from core.database import UserManager
from core.pipeline import faultDetectionPipeline
from core.pipeline import FaultInfo


class LightnReviewInterface(LightnInterface):
    """ review """

    def load(self, path):
        """ 加载 """
        path = os.path.join(path, 'lightning', 'fault.json')
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            self.clear()
            return

        try:
            with open(path, 'r') as f:
                info = FaultInfo.from_dict(**json.load(f))
        except json.decoder.JSONDecodeError:
            return
        except Exception as e:
            raise e

        self.setDistance(info.location)
        self.update()


================================================================================
FILE: ./view\interface\memory_interface\navigation.py
================================================================================
import os

from PyQt5.QtCore import QTimer, pyqtSignal
from PyQt5.QtGui import QStandardItemModel, QStandardItem, QFont
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QListView
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout

from core.const import Path
from core.database import UserManager
from ...companent import Label, ListView


class Navigation(QWidget):
    """ 显示保存的所有批次, 并可以选择缺陷点并展示"""
    faultLoadRequsted = pyqtSignal(str)
    faultSetUpdated = pyqtSignal(str)

    BATCH_NAME_TAG = '批次:'
    USER_NAME_TAG = '负责人:'

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        self._check_batch_name = None
        self._manager = UserManager()
        super().__init__(parent)

        self.initWidget()
        self.initLayout()
        self.initConnect()

        self._timer = QTimer(self)
        self._timer.timeout.connect(self._onTimeout)
        self._timer.start(500)  # 500ms

    def initWidget(self):
        self.batch_label = Label(text=self.BATCH_NAME_TAG, parent=self, fontSize=20, weight=QFont.Bold)
        self.user_label = Label(text=self.USER_NAME_TAG, parent=self, fontSize=20, weight=QFont.Bold)
        self.batch_list = ListView(self)
        self.batch_list.setModel(QStandardItemModel())
        self.batch_list.setEditTriggers(QListView.NoEditTriggers)
        self.result_list = ListView(self)
        self.result_list.setModel(QStandardItemModel())
        self.result_list.setEditTriggers(QListView.NoEditTriggers)

    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.hLayout1 = QHBoxLayout()

        self.vLayout1 = QVBoxLayout()
        self.vLayout2 = QVBoxLayout()

        self.viewLayout.addLayout(self.vLayout1)
        self.vLayout1.addWidget(self.batch_label)
        self.vLayout1.addWidget(self.user_label)
        self.vLayout1.addLayout(self.hLayout1)
        self.hLayout1.addWidget(self.batch_list)

        self.hLayout1.addWidget(self.result_list)

    def initConnect(self):
        self.batch_list.clicked.connect(self._on_batch_list_clicked)
        self.result_list.clicked.connect(self._on_result_list_clicked)

    def _onTimeout(self):
        self._update_all_list()

    def _update_all_list(self):
        """ 更新所有list """
        if not self.isVisible(): return
        # updage list
        if not os.path.exists(Path.OUTPUT_DIR): return

        # 同步
        self._update_list(list_view=self.batch_list, items=os.listdir(Path.OUTPUT_DIR))
        if self._check_batch_name is not None:
            read_dir = os.path.join(Path.OUTPUT_DIR, self._check_batch_name)
            items = os.listdir(read_dir)
            items = list(set(items))
            items = sorted(items, key=lambda item: float(item))
            self._update_list(list_view=self.result_list, items=items)


    def _update_list(self, list_view, items):
        """ 根据一个 path 更新 listView"""
        # 同步
        model: QStandardItemModel = list_view.model()
        idx = -1
        for idx, name in enumerate(items):
            if idx < model.rowCount():
                model.item(idx, 0).setText(name)
            else:
                model.appendRow(QStandardItem(name))
        while idx != model.rowCount() - 1:
            model.removeRow(idx)

    def _on_batch_list_clicked(self, index):
        """ 点击 """
        model: QStandardItemModel = self.batch_list.model()
        item = model.itemFromIndex(index)
        username = self._manager.getUsersByBatch(batchName=item.text())

        self.batch_label.setText(self.tr(self.BATCH_NAME_TAG) + ' ' + item.text())
        self.user_label.setText(self.tr(self.USER_NAME_TAG) + ' ' + (username or '无'))
        self._check_batch_name = item.text()
        self._update_all_list()
        # 发送型号
        read_dir = os.path.join(Path.OUTPUT_DIR, self._check_batch_name)
        self.faultSetUpdated.emit(read_dir)

    def _on_result_list_clicked(self, index):
        """ 点击 """
        model: QStandardItemModel = self.result_list.model()
        item = model.itemFromIndex(index)
        path = os.path.join(Path.OUTPUT_DIR, self._check_batch_name, item.text())

        self.faultLoadRequsted.emit(path)

    def showEvent(self, event):
        self._update_all_list()
        super(Navigation, self).showEvent(event)

================================================================================
import os
from urllib.response import addinfo

from PyQt5.QtCore import QTimer, QStandardPaths, QStringListModel, Qt
from PyQt5.QtGui import QStandardItemModel, QStandardItem
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, QStackedWidget, QListView, QGridLayout

from ...companent import Label, Pivot,LightnInterface,EdgegWidget,ResultLabel
# from .image import ResultLabel
from core.const import Path
from core.pipeline import faultDetectionPipeline


class VisionDetectWidgwt(EdgegWidget):
    """ 四个相机的检测结果展示"""
    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super(VisionDetectWidgwt, self).__init__(*args, **kwargs)

        self.initWidget()
        self.initLayout()

    def initWidget(self):
        self.result_label_1 = ResultLabel(parent=self)
        self.result_label_2 = ResultLabel(parent=self)
        self.result_label_3 = ResultLabel(parent=self)
        self.result_label_4 = ResultLabel(parent=self)

    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QGridLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)

        for i, label in enumerate((self.result_label_1, self.result_label_2, self.result_label_3, self.result_label_4)):
            self.viewLayout.addWidget(Label(text=self.tr(f'通道{i + 1}'), fontSize=16, parent=self), 0, i,
                                      alignment=Qt.AlignCenter)
            self.viewLayout.addWidget(label, 1, i)
        self.viewLayout.setRowStretch(1, 1)

    def load(self, path: str):
        for label, camera_name in zip((self.result_label_1,
                                       self.result_label_2,
                                       self.result_label_3,
                                           self.result_label_4,),
                                      faultDetectionPipeline.CHANNELS):
            label.setResult(os.path.join(path, camera_name))

from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt
from PyQt5.QtGui import QFont
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QSpinBox, QVBoxLayout, QGridLayout, QSpacerItem, QSizePolicy
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QStackedWidget

from core import config
from core.pipeline.device.camera import Camera
from ...companent import Label, ComboBox, FileLoadBox,SpinBox



class ModeComboBox(ComboBox):
    modes = ["海康威视", "摄像头", "视频", "图片"]

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(ModeComboBox, self).__init__(parent=parent)
        self.setItems(items=self.modes)

    def readMode(self) -> str:
        return [Camera.ReadMode_Hk, Camera.ReadMode_Capture, Camera.ReadMode_Vedio, Camera.ReadMode_Img][
            self.currentIndex()]

    def indexOf(self, mode):
        if mode not in (Camera.ReadMode_Hk, Camera.ReadMode_Capture, Camera.ReadMode_Vedio, Camera.ReadMode_Img):
            return 0
        return [Camera.ReadMode_Hk, Camera.ReadMode_Capture, Camera.ReadMode_Vedio, Camera.ReadMode_Img].index(mode)


class PortComboBox(ComboBox):
    def showPopup(self):
        """ 动态加载Items """
        # 获取接入的所有串口信息，插入combobox的选项中
        posts = Camera.HK_deivces()
        self.setItems(['None'] + posts)
        super(PortComboBox, self).showPopup()  # 弹出选项框


class SingleCameraSettingWidget(QWidget):
    @classmethod
    def set(cls, item, ctx, value):
        _item = config.get(item)
        _item[ctx] = value
        config.set(item, _item.copy())
        config.save()

    @classmethod
    def get(cls, item, ctx):
        return config.get(item)[ctx]

    def __init__(self, ctx: int, parent=None):
        self._ctx = ctx
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.initWidget()
        self.initLayout()
        self.initData()
        self.initConnect()

    def initWidget(self):
        self.mode_combox = ModeComboBox(self)
        self.prot_combox = PortComboBox(self)
        self.exposure_spinbox = SpinBox(self)
        self.exposure_spinbox.setRange(0, 2 ** 16)
        self.capture_spinbox = SpinBox(self)
        self.capture_spinbox.setRange(0, 16)
        self.imageBox = FileLoadBox(parent=self, title='选择图片', filter='Images (*.png *.jpg *.jpeg)')
        self.videoBox = FileLoadBox(self, title='选择视频', filter='Video Files (*.mp4 *.avi *.mov *.mkv)')

        self.stackedWidget = QStackedWidget(self)
        self._w1 = QWidget(self)
        self._w2 = QWidget(self)
        self._w3 = QWidget(self)
        self._w4 = QWidget(self)

    def initConnect(self):
        ctx = self._ctx
        self.prot_combox.currentTextChanged.connect(lambda v: self.set(config.Camera_Prots, ctx=ctx, value=v))
        self.exposure_spinbox.valueChanged.connect(lambda v: self.set(config.Camera_Exposures, ctx=ctx, value=v))
        self.mode_combox.currentIndexChanged.connect(lambda index: self.stackedWidget.setCurrentIndex(index))
        self.mode_combox.currentIndexChanged.connect(lambda v: self.set(config.Camera_ReadModes, ctx=ctx,
                                                                        value=self.mode_combox.readMode()))
        self.imageBox.pathChanged.connect(lambda v: self.set(config.Camera_Imgs, ctx=ctx, value=v))
        self.videoBox.pathChanged.connect(lambda v: self.set(config.Camera_Videos, ctx=ctx, value=v))

    def initData(self):
        ctx = self._ctx
        if (port := self.get(config.Camera_Prots, ctx=ctx)) is not None:
            self.prot_combox.setCurrentText(port)
            self.prot_combox.addItem(port)
        if (img := self.get(config.Camera_Imgs, ctx=ctx)) is not None:
            self.imageBox.setPath(img)
        if (video := self.get(config.Camera_Videos, ctx=ctx)) is not None:
            self.videoBox.setPath(video)
        # exposure
        self.exposure_spinbox.setValue(self.get(config.Camera_Exposures, ctx=ctx))
        # capture check
        self.capture_spinbox.setValue(self.get(config.Camera_Captures, ctx=ctx))
        # read mode
        self.mode_combox.setCurrentIndex(self.mode_combox.indexOf(self.get(config.Camera_ReadModes, ctx=ctx)))
        # go to widget
        self.stackedWidget.setCurrentIndex(self.mode_combox.currentIndex())

    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.gridLayout = QGridLayout()
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)

        self.viewLayout.addLayout(self.gridLayout)

        for i, (name, item) in enumerate([('读取模式', self.mode_combox), ]):
            self.gridLayout.addWidget(Label(self.tr(name), 16), i, 0, alignment=Qt.AlignRight)
            self.gridLayout.addWidget(item, i, 1, )

        for w, items in [
            (self._w1, (('HK端口', self.prot_combox), ('曝光时间', self.exposure_spinbox))),
            (self._w2, (('摄像头', self.capture_spinbox),)),
            (self._w3, (('视频路径', self.videoBox),)),
            (self._w4, (("图片路径", self.imageBox),))
        ]:
            self.stackedWidget.addWidget(w)
            layout = QGridLayout(w)
            layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
            for i, (name, item) in enumerate(items):
                layout.addWidget(Label(self.tr(name), 16), i, 0, alignment=Qt.AlignRight)
                layout.addWidget(item, i, 1, )
            layout.setColumnStretch(0, 0)
            layout.setColumnStretch(1, 1)

        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.stackedWidget)
        self.gridLayout.setColumnStretch(0, 0)
        self.gridLayout.setColumnStretch(1, 1)
        self.viewLayout.setStretch(0, 0)
        self.viewLayout.setStretch(1, 1)
        self.viewLayout.addSpacerItem(QSpacerItem(0, 0, QSizePolicy.Minimum, QSizePolicy.Expanding))


class CameraSettingInterface(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.initWidget()
        self.initLayout()
        self.initData()

        self.camera_1 = SingleCameraSettingWidget(ctx=0, parent=self)
        self.camera_2 = SingleCameraSettingWidget(ctx=1, parent=self)
        self.camera_3 = SingleCameraSettingWidget(ctx=2, parent=self)
        self.camera_4 = SingleCameraSettingWidget(ctx=3, parent=self)

    def initConnect(self):
        pass

    def initData(self):
        pass

    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.vlayout = QVBoxLayout()

        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.titleLabel)
        for idx, (title, w) in enumerate(
                [('摄像头-1', self.camera_1),
                 ('摄像头-2', self.camera_2),
                 ('摄像头-3', self.camera_3),
                 ('摄像头-4', self.camera_4)]):
            self.vlayout.addWidget(Label(text=self.tr(title), fontSize=24, parent=self, ))
            self.vlayout.addWidget(w)
            self.vlayout.setStretch(1 + idx * 2, 0)
            self.vlayout.setStretch(2 + idx * 2, 1)
        self.viewLayout.addLayout(self.vlayout)
        self.viewLayout.setStretch(0, 0)

from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, QStackedWidget, QSpacerItem, QSizePolicy, QFrame
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QScrollArea
from ...companent import Label, Pivot
from .encoder import VSamplerSettingInterface

from .camera import CameraSettingInterface
from .lightning import LightningSettingInterface
from .light_source import LightSourceSettingInterface

class SettingInterface(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)

        self.initWidget()
        self.initLayout()

    def initWidget(self):
        self.scrollArea = QScrollArea(self)
        self.scrollArea.setFrameShape(QFrame.NoFrame)
        self._w = QWidget(self.scrollArea)
        self.scrollArea.setWidget(self._w)
        self.scrollArea.setWidgetResizable(True)

        self.vsamplerSettingInterface = VSamplerSettingInterface(parent=self, )
        self.cameraSettingInterface = CameraSettingInterface(parent=self, )
        self.lightningSettingInterface = LightningSettingInterface(parent=self, )
        self.lightSourceSettingInterface = LightSourceSettingInterface(parent=self)



    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self._wlayout = QVBoxLayout(self._w)

        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.scrollArea)
        self._wlayout.addWidget(self.vsamplerSettingInterface)
        self._wlayout.addWidget(self.lightningSettingInterface)
        self._wlayout.addWidget(self.lightSourceSettingInterface)
        self._wlayout.addWidget(self.cameraSettingInterface)
        self._wlayout.addSpacerItem(QSpacerItem(0, 0, QSizePolicy.Fixed, QSizePolicy.Expanding))

from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, QComboBox, QGridLayout, QSpacerItem, QSizePolicy
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QSpinBox, QDoubleSpinBox, QVBoxLayout, QComboBox, QGridLayout, QSpacerItem, QSizePolicy
from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, pyqtSignal
from PyQt5.QtGui import QIcon, QFont
from ...companent import Label, SerialPortComboBox,SpinBox,DoubleSpinBox,ToggleButton
from core import config, Serial




class VSamplerSettingInterface(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.initWidget()
        self.initLayout()
        self.initData()
        self.initConnect()

    def initWidget(self):
        self.titleLabel = Label(text=self.tr('测速编码器'), weight=QFont.Bold, fontSize=32, parent=self, )

        self.port_combox = SerialPortComboBox(self)
        self.baudrate_spinbox = SpinBox(self)
        self.perimeter_spinbox = DoubleSpinBox(self)
        self.directionToggleBtn = ToggleButton(self)

    def initConnect(self):
        self.baudrate_spinbox.valueChanged.connect(lambda v: config.set(config.VSampler_Baudrate, value=v))
        self.perimeter_spinbox.valueChanged.connect(lambda v: config.set(config.VSampler_Perimeter, value=v / 100))
        self.port_combox.currentTextChanged.connect(
            lambda v: config.set(config.VSampler_Port, value=self.port_combox.currentText()))
        self.port_combox.currentIndexChanged.connect(
            lambda v: config.set(config.VSampler_Port, value=self.port_combox.currentText()))
        self.directionToggleBtn.toogled.connect(lambda v: config.set(config.VSampler_Direction, value=v))
    def initData(self):
        self.port_combox.setCurrentText(config.get(config.VSampler_Port))
        self.port_combox.addItem(config.get(config.VSampler_Port))
        self.baudrate_spinbox.setRange(0, 2 ** 31 - 1, )
        self.baudrate_spinbox.setValue(config.get(config.VSampler_Baudrate))
        self.perimeter_spinbox.setRange(0, 2 ** 31 - 1)
        self.perimeter_spinbox.setValue(config.get(item=config.VSampler_Perimeter))
        self.perimeter_spinbox.setSingleStep(0.01)
        self.directionToggleBtn.setChecked(config.get(config.VSampler_Direction))

    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.gridLayout = QGridLayout()
        self.hlayout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)

        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.titleLabel)
        self.viewLayout.addLayout(self.gridLayout)
        self.hlayout.addWidget(self.directionToggleBtn)
        self.hlayout.addSpacerItem(QSpacerItem(0, 0, QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Fixed))
        self.hlayout.setStretch(0,1)
        self.hlayout.setStretch(1,1)

        for i, (name, item) in enumerate([
            ('端口号', self.port_combox),
            ('波特率', self.baudrate_spinbox),
            ('单圈周长(cm)', self.perimeter_spinbox),
        ]):
            self.gridLayout.addWidget(Label(self.tr(name), 16), i, 0, alignment=Qt.AlignRight)
            self.gridLayout.addWidget(item, i, 1, )
        self.gridLayout.addWidget(Label(self.tr('是否反向'), 16), i + 1, 0, alignment=Qt.AlignRight)
        self.gridLayout.addLayout(self.hlayout, i+1, 1, )

        self.gridLayout.setColumnStretch(0, 0)
        self.gridLayout.setColumnStretch(1, 1)
        self.viewLayout.setStretch(0, 0)
        self.viewLayout.setStretch(1, 1)
        self.viewLayout.addSpacerItem(QSpacerItem(0, 0, QSizePolicy.Minimum, QSizePolicy.Expanding))

    def _onProtComboxClicked(self):
        print(Serial.posts())

from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, QComboBox, QGridLayout, QSpacerItem, QSizePolicy
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QSpinBox, QDoubleSpinBox, QVBoxLayout, QComboBox, QGridLayout, QSpacerItem, QSizePolicy
from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, pyqtSignal
from PyQt5.QtGui import QIcon, QFont
from ...companent import Label, SerialPortComboBox,SpinBox
from core import config, Serial


class LightSourceSettingInterface(QWidget):
    """ 光源设置 """

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.initWidget()
        self.initLayout()
        self.initData()
        self.initConnect()

    def initWidget(self):
        self.titleLabel = Label(text=self.tr('光源'), weight=QFont.Bold, fontSize=32, parent=self, )
        self.port_combox = SerialPortComboBox(self)  # 端口
        self.baudrate_spinbox = SpinBox(self)  # 波特率
        self.light_level_spinbox = SpinBox(self)  # 亮度

    def initConnect(self):
        # 波特率
        self.baudrate_spinbox.valueChanged.connect(lambda v: config.set(config.LightSource_Baudrate, value=v))
        # 端口
        _func = lambda v: config.set(config.LightSource_Port, value=self.port_combox.currentText())
        self.port_combox.currentTextChanged.connect(_func)
        self.port_combox.currentIndexChanged.connect(_func)
        # 亮度
        self.light_level_spinbox.valueChanged.connect(lambda v: config.set(config.LightSource_Level, value=v))

    def initData(self):
        self.port_combox.setCurrentText(config.get(config.LightSource_Port))
        self.port_combox.addItem(config.get(config.LightSource_Port))
        self.baudrate_spinbox.setRange(0, 2 ** 31 - 1, )
        self.baudrate_spinbox.setValue(config.get(config.LightSource_Baudrate))
        self.light_level_spinbox.setRange(0, 255)
        self.light_level_spinbox.setValue(config.get(config.LightSource_Level))

    def initLayout(self):
        self.viewLayout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.gridLayout = QGridLayout()
        self.viewLayout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)

        self.viewLayout.addWidget(self.titleLabel)
        self.viewLayout.addLayout(self.gridLayout)

        for i, (name, item) in enumerate([
            ('端口号', self.port_combox),
            ('波特率', self.baudrate_spinbox),
            ('亮度', self.light_level_spinbox)
        ]):
            self.gridLayout.addWidget(Label(self.tr(name), 16), i, 0, alignment=Qt.AlignRight)
            self.gridLayout.addWidget(item, i, 1, )

        self.gridLayout.setColumnStretch(0, 0)
        self.gridLayout.setColumnStretch(1, 1)
        self.viewLayout.setStretch(0, 0)
        self.viewLayout.setStretch(1, 1)
        self.viewLayout.addSpacerItem(QSpacerItem(0, 0, QSizePolicy.Minimum, QSizePolicy.Expanding))
